                    <option value="degraded">Operacional — {isPlant ? 'em risco' : 'com restrições'}</option>
                    <option value="normal">Operacional normal</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Descrição Detalhada do Problema / Avaria *
                </label>
                <textarea
                  required
                  rows={4}
                  value={problemDescription}
                  onChange={(e) => setProblemDescription(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Descreva detalhadamente o sintoma, ruído, fuga de óleo, vibração ou falha observada no equipamento..."
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] rounded-lg px-3 py-2.5 text-xs text-slate-900 outline-none leading-relaxed"
                />
              </div>

            </div>
          </div>

          {/* BLOCO INFERIOR: ANEXOS E EVIDÊNCIAS */}
          <div className="bg-white rounded-lg border border-slate-200 overflow-hidden shadow-none">
            
            {/* Faixa Superior Azul Escuro */}
            <div className="bg-[#0B132B] px-4 py-2.5 text-white font-bold text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
              ANEXOS E EVIDÊNCIAS (FOTOS E DOCUMENTOS)
            </div>

            <div className="p-5 space-y-4">
              
              {/* DROPZONE PONTILHADA */}
              <div
                onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setIsDragging(true); }}
                onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}
                onDrop={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  setIsDragging(false);
                  handleFileAdd(e.dataTransfer.files);
                }}
                className={`border-2 border-dashed rounded-xl p-6 text-center transition-colors cursor-pointer relative ${
                  isDragging ? 'border-[#0066FF] bg-blue-50/50' : 'border-slate-300 hover:border-slate-400 bg-slate-50/50'
                }`}
              >
                <input
                  type="file"
                  multiple
                  accept="image/*,video/*,application/pdf"
                  onChange={(e) => handleFileAdd(e.target.files)}
                  className="absolute inset-0 opacity-0 cursor-pointer w-full h-full"
                />
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-[#0066FF] flex items-center justify-center border border-blue-100">
                    <UploadCloud size={20} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">
                      Arraste fotos, vídeos ou documentos aqui
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                      ou clique para selecionar ficheiros do seu dispositivo
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* LISTA DE FICHEIROS ANEXADOS */}
              {attachedFiles.length > 0 && (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block">
                    Ficheiros Anexados ({attachedFiles.length})
                  </span>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5">
                    {attachedFiles.map((item) => (
                      <div
                        key={item.id}
                        className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-2.5 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-between text-xs"
                      >
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 truncate mr-2">
                          {item.previewUrl ? (
                            <img
                              src={item.previewUrl}
                              alt="Preview"
                              className="w-8 h-8 rounded object-cover border border-slate-200 shrink-0"
                            />
                          ) : (
                            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 font-bold text-[10px]">
                              DOC
                            </div>
                          )}
                          <div className="truncate">
                            <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 block truncate">{item.name}</span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 block">{item.size}</span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRemoveFile(item.id)}
                          className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer shrink-0"
                          title="Remover ficheiro"
                        >
                          <X size={14} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* FAIXA INFORMATIVA */}
              <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50/60 border border-blue-200 text-blue-900 text-xs rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <Info size={16} className="text-[#0066FF] shrink-0" />
                <span>
                  Após a triagem, esta solicitação poderá originar uma Ordem de Serviço na equipa de manutenção.
                </span>
              </div>

            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        {/* RODAPÉ DE AÇÕES */}
        <div className="p-5 border-t border-slate-200 bg-white flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 shrink-0">
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">
            Os campos marcados com * são de preenchimento obrigatório.
          </span>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 w-full sm:w-auto justify-end">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            >
              Cancelar
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleSubmit('draft')}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              Guardar como rascunho
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleSubmit('pending_triage')}
              disabled={submitting}
              className="px-4 py-2 bg-[#0066FF] hover:bg-blue-700 active:bg-blue-800 text-white font-semibold text-xs rounded-lg transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 shadow-none"
            >
              {submitting ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                  <span>A submeter...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <span>Enviar solicitação</span>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default MaintenanceNotificationModal;


// ===== src/components/QRScannerModal.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { Html5Qrcode } from 'html5-qrcode';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  X,
  QrCode,
  Loader2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  FileText,
  File,
  Eye,
  Wrench,
  Gauge,
  Activity,
  ArrowRight,
  UploadCloud,
  ChevronRight,
  Shield,
  HelpCircle,
  Clock,
  Car,
  Settings
} from 'lucide-react';

interface QRScannerModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
}

interface Asset {
  id: string;
  code_tag: string;
  codeTag?: string;
  name: string;
  brand?: string;
  model: string;
  category: 'vehicle_light' | 'vehicle_heavy' | 'generator' | 'machinery' | string;
  criticality_level?: 'A' | 'B' | 'C' | string;
  criticalityLevel?: string;
  current_meter?: number;
  currentMeter?: number;
  meter_kind?: 'odometer_km' | 'hourmeter_hours' | string;
  meterKind?: string;
  status: 'operational' | 'breakdown' | 'maintenance' | string;
  cost_center: string;
  costCenter?: string;
  
  // Detalhes adicionais
  license_plate?: string;
  licensePlate?: string;
  plant_sector?: string;
  plantSector?: string;
  voltage_specs?: string;
  voltageSpecs?: string;
  power_kw_kva?: number;
  powerKwKva?: number;

  inatro_inspection_expiry?: string;
  inatroInspectionExpiry?: string;
  innoq_calibration_expiry?: string;
  innoqCalibrationExpiry?: string;
}

interface Document {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  document_type: string;
  documentType?: string;
  signed_url: string;
  signedUrl?: string;
  created_at: string;
}

export const QRScannerModal: React.FC<QRScannerModalProps> = ({ isOpen, onClose }) => {
  useEscapeKey(onClose, isOpen);
  const [scannerError, setScannerError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loadingAsset, setLoadingAsset] = useState(false);
  const [activeAsset, setActiveAsset] = useState<Asset | null>(null);
  const [documents, setDocuments] = useState<Document[]>([]);
  const [loadingDocs, setLoadingDocs] = useState(false);
  
  // Formulários de Campo
  const [meterValue, setMeterValue] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingMeter, setSubmittingMeter] = useState(false);
  const [meterSuccess, setMeterSuccess] = useState(false);
  
  const [reportIssue, setReportIssue] = useState(false);
  const [issueTitle, setIssueTitle] = useState('');
  const [issueDescription, setReportIssueDescription] = useState('');
  const [issuePriority, setIssuePriority] = useState<'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent'>('medium');
  const [issueMeterAtService, setIssueMeterAtService] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingIssue, setSubmittingIssue] = useState(false);
  const [issueSuccess, setIssueSuccess] = useState(false);

  const scannerRef = useRef<Html5Qrcode | null>(null);
  const scannerId = "qr-reader-viewport";

  // Inicialização e Liberação da Câmera
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;

    // Reinicia os estados de ativo escaneado ao abrir
    setActiveAsset(null);
    setDocuments([]);
    setScannerError(null);
    setMeterValue('');
    setMeterSuccess(false);
    setReportIssue(false);
    setIssueTitle('');
    setReportIssueDescription('');
    setIssuePriority('medium');
    setIssueMeterAtService('');
    setIssueSuccess(false);

    // Pequeno atraso para garantir que o contêiner DOM esteja montado
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      startCamera();
    }, 400);

    return () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      stopCamera();
    };
  }, [isOpen]);

  const startCamera = async () => {
    try {
      const html5Qrcode = new Html5Qrcode(scannerId);
      scannerRef.current = html5Qrcode;

      await html5Qrcode.start(
        { facingMode: "environment" },
        {
          fps: 10,
          qrbox: (width, height) => {
            const minEdge = Math.min(width, height);
            const boxSize = Math.floor(minEdge * 0.7);
            return { width: boxSize, height: boxSize };
          }
        },
        onScanSuccess,
        onScanFailure
      );
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao iniciar câmera:", err);
      if (err.toString().includes("NotAllowedError") || err.toString().includes("Permission denied")) {
        setScannerError("Permissão de acesso à câmara foi negada. Por favor, ative as permissões nas configurações do navegador para escanear etiquetas.");
      } else {
        setScannerError("Não foi possível aceder à câmara traseira ou nenhuma câmara foi encontrada.");
      }
    }
  };

  const stopCamera = async () => {
    if (scannerRef.current && scannerRef.current.isScanning) {
      try {
        await scannerRef.current.stop();
        scannerRef.current.clear();
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Erro ao parar câmera:", err);
      }
    }
    scannerRef.current = null;
  };

  const onScanSuccess = async (decodedText: string) => {
    // 1. Vibração tátil leve (se disponível)
    if (navigator.vibrate) {
      navigator.vibrate(100);
    }

    // 2. Para a câmera imediatamente para evitar múltiplos scans
    await stopCamera();

    // 3. Carrega o ativo escaneado
    loadAssetData(decodedText);
  };

  const onScanFailure = () => {
    // Ignoramos erros de frame individual para não poluir o console
  };

  const loadAssetData = async (assetId: string) => {
    setLoadingAsset(true);
    setScannerError(null);
    try {
      // Faz a requisição segura para obter o ativo pelo ID ou CodeTag
      const res = await api.get(`/assets/${encodeURIComponent(assetId)}`);
      const asset = res.data?.asset || res.data?.data || res.data;
      
      if (!asset || !asset.id) {
        throw new Error("Equipamento ou viatura não cadastrada no CMMS.");
      }

      setActiveAsset(asset);
      setMeterValue(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);
      setIssueMeterAtService(asset.current_meter ?? asset.currentMeter ?? 0);
      setIssuePriority('medium');
      setIssueTitle('');
      
      // Carrega os documentos desse ativo
      loadAssetDocs(asset.id);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao buscar ativo escaneado:", err);
      setScannerError(
        err.response?.data?.message || 
        err.message || 
        "Código QR lido com sucesso, mas o ativo correspondente não foi encontrado na base de dados real."
      );
      // Reinicia câmera para tentar novamente
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (isOpen && !activeAsset) {
          startCamera();
        }
      }, 5000);
    } finally {
      setLoadingAsset(false);
    }
  };

  const loadAssetDocs = async (id: string) => {
    setLoadingDocs(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/assets/${id}/documents`);
      const docs = res.data?.documents || res.data || [];
      setDocuments(docs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error("Erro ao carregar documentos:", err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingDocs(false);
    }
  };

  // Enviar leitura do contador
  const handleUpdateMeter = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeAsset || meterValue === '') return;

    const current = activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0;
    if (Number(meterValue) < current) {
      alert(`O novo valor não pode ser menor do que o registo atual (${current}).`);
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingMeter(true);
    try {
      await api.patch(`/assets/${activeAsset.id}/meter`, {
        meterValue: Number(meterValue)
      });
      setMeterSuccess(true);
      
      // Atualiza o ativo local
      setActiveAsset(prev => {
        if (!prev) return null;
        return {
          ...prev,
          current_meter: Number(meterValue),
          currentMeter: Number(meterValue)
        };
      });
      
      setTimeout(() => setMeterSuccess(false), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao atualizar medidor:", err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || "Erro ao atualizar o contador no servidor.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingMeter(false);
    }
  };

  // Reportar Avaria (Criar Ordem de Serviço Corretiva)
  const handleReportIssueSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!activeAsset || !issueTitle.trim() || !issueDescription.trim() || issueMeterAtService === '') return;

    setSubmittingIssue(true);
    try {
      const payload = {
        assetId: activeAsset.id,
        asset_id: activeAsset.id,
        title: issueTitle,
        description: issueDescription,
        orderType: 'corrective',
        order_type: 'corrective',
        priority: issuePriority,
        meterAtService: Number(issueMeterAtService),
        meter_at_service: Number(issueMeterAtService),
        status: 'open'
      };

      await api.post('/work-orders', payload);
      setIssueSuccess(true);
      setIssueTitle('');
      setReportIssueDescription('');
      
      setTimeout(() => {
        setIssueSuccess(false);
        setReportIssue(false);
      }, 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error("Erro ao reportar avaria:", err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || "Erro ao criar ordem de serviço corretiva.");
    } finally {
      setSubmittingIssue(false);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  const isVehicle = activeAsset && (activeAsset.category === 'vehicle_light' || activeAsset.category === 'vehicle_heavy');
  const level = activeAsset?.criticality_level || activeAsset?.criticalityLevel || 'B';

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-lg rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
        
        {/* Header do Scanner */}
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <QrCode className="text-blue-700" size={20} />
            <span className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900">Leitor de QR Code Móvel</span>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={() => {
              stopCamera();
              onClose();
            }}
            className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
          >
            <X size={18} />
          </button>
        </div>

        {/* Corpo do Modal */}
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 space-y-4">
          
          {/* MODO A: ESCANEANDO (Sem ativo selecionado) */}
          {!activeAsset && !loadingAsset && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-6 space-y-6">
              
              {/* Moldura do Leitor QR */}
              <div className="relative w-72 h-72 border border-slate-300 rounded-md bg-slate-100 overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center">
                
                {/* Visualizador HTML5-QRCode */}
                <div id={scannerId} className="w-full h-full object-cover"></div>

                {/* Laser de Scan de Campo */}
                {!scannerError && (
                  <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 h-0.5 bg-blue-600 shadow-md shadow-blue-500/50 animate-laser-scanning z-10 pointer-events-none"></div>
                )}

                {/* Cantos Iluminados (Moldura Tecnológica) */}
                <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 w-6 h-6 border-t-2 border-l-2 border-blue-700 rounded-tl-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 border-t-2 border-r-2 border-blue-700 rounded-tr-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-3 w-6 h-6 border-b-2 border-l-2 border-blue-700 rounded-bl-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
                <div className="absolute bottom-3 right-3 w-6 h-6 border-b-2 border-r-2 border-blue-700 rounded-br-sm pointer-events-none z-10"></div>
              </div>

              {/* Mensagem orientadora */}
              <div className="text-center px-4 max-w-sm">
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 font-medium leading-relaxed">
                  Aponte a câmara do telemóvel para a etiqueta QR Code colada na viatura ou gerador.
                </p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-1 uppercase tracking-wider font-semibold">
                  Abertura automática de ficha técnica
                </p>
              </div>

              {/* Tratamento de Erros de Permissão ou Scanner */}
              {scannerError && (
                <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start max-w-sm">
                  <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                    <span className="font-bold block text-red-800">Falha no Scanner</span>
                    <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{scannerError}</span>
                    <button
                      onClick={startCamera}
                      className="mt-2 text-xs font-medium bg-white text-red-700 px-2 py-1 rounded-md border border-red-300 hover:bg-red-50 cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      Tentar Novamente
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODO B: CARREGANDO DETALHES DO ATIVO */}
          {loadingAsset && (
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-20 space-y-3">
              <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700" size={36} />
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium tracking-wide">A carregar detalhes de campo do ativo...</p>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* MODO C: PAINEL DE AÇÃO RÁPIDA DE CAMPO (Ativo Identificado!) */}
          {activeAsset && (
            <div className="space-y-4 animate-slide-up">
              
              {/* Header de Identificação do Ativo */}
              <div className="p-4 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-xs text-blue-700 block tracking-wider uppercase">
                    {activeAsset.code_tag || activeAsset.codeTag}
                  </span>
                  <h3 className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-base leading-tight">{activeAsset.name}</h3>
                  <span className="text-xs text-slate-600 block mt-0.5">{activeAsset.brand} {activeAsset.model}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                  <span className={`inline-flex px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium border ${
                    level === 'A' 
                      ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-200' 
                      : level === 'B' 
                      ? 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border-amber-200' 
                      : 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-200'
                  }`}>
                    Criticidade {level}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium uppercase">
                    {isVehicle ? "Frota de Campo" : "Equipamento Industrial"}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Detalhes Técnicos e de Conformidade */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md space-y-3">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block border-b border-slate-200 pb-2">
                  Ficha Operacional & Conformidade
                </span>
                
                {isVehicle ? (
                  /* INFORMAÇÕES DE VIATURA */
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Matrícula</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-mono font-bold">{activeAsset.license_plate || activeAsset.licensePlate || '---'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Odómetro Atual</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{(activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0).toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="col-span-2">
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Validade Inspeção INATRO</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 mt-0.5">
                        <Clock size={12} className="text-blue-700" />
                        {activeAsset.inatro_inspection_expiry || activeAsset.inatroInspectionExpiry 
                          ? new Date(activeAsset.inatro_inspection_expiry || activeAsset.inatroInspectionExpiry || '').toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ') 
                          : 'Pendente de Certificado'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  /* INFORMAÇÕES DE MAQUINARIA/GERADOR */
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-xs">
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Setor da Planta</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium">{activeAsset.plant_sector || activeAsset.plantSector || 'Não especificado'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Horímetro Atual</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{(activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0).toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} horas</span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Tensão / Potência</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-mono font-bold">
                        {activeAsset.voltage_specs || activeAsset.voltageSpecs || '---'} / {activeAsset.power_kw_kva || activeAsset.powerKwKva || '---'} kW
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <span className="text-slate-500 block uppercase text-[10px] font-semibold">Calibração INNOQ</span>
                      <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Clock size={12} className="text-blue-700" />
                        {activeAsset.innoq_calibration_expiry || activeAsset.innoqCalibrationExpiry 
                          ? new Date(activeAsset.innoq_calibration_expiry || activeAsset.innoqCalibrationExpiry || '').toLocaleDateString('pt-MZ') 
                          : 'Sem calibração ativa'}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* AÇÃO A: ATUALIZAR CONTADOR DE CAMPO */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Gauge size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                  <span>Atualizar Contador de Campo</span>
                </span>
                
                <form onSubmit={handleUpdateMeter} className="flex gap-2 items-end">
                  <div className="flex-1 relative">
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      required
                      min={activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0}
                      value={meterValue}
                      onChange={(e) => setMeterValue(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                    <span className="absolute right-3.5 top-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500">
                      {isVehicle ? "KM" : "HORAS"}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={submittingMeter || meterValue === '' || Number(meterValue) < (activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0)}
                    className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 shrink-0 h-9"
                  >
                    {submittingMeter ? (
                      <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" />
                    ) : (
                      <span>Guardar</span>
                    )}
                  </button>
                </form>

                {meterSuccess && (
                  <div className="mt-2 text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium flex items-center gap-1">
                    <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                    <span>Contador atualizado e sincronizado com sucesso!</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 block mt-1.5">
                  O valor atual é de {activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0} {isVehicle ? "km" : "horas"}. Não é possível inserir valores inferiores ao atual.
                </span>
              </div>

              {/* AÇÃO B: CONSULTAR DOCUMENTAÇÃO DE CAMPO */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md">
                <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <FileText size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                  <span>Documentos e Certificados do Ativo ({documents.length})</span>
                </span>

                {loadingDocs ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-4">
                    <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400 mx-auto" />
                  </div>
                ) : documents.length === 0 ? (
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">Nenhum certificado ou ficha anexada.</p>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2 max-h-36 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                    {documents.map((doc) => (
                      <div key={doc.id} className="p-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-between text-xs hover:border-slate-300 transition-colors">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 truncate mr-3">
                          <File size={14} className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" />
                          <span className="font-medium text-slate-900 truncate" title={doc.title}>{doc.title}</span>
                        </div>
                        <a
                          href={doc.signed_url || doc.signedUrl}
                          target="_blank"
                          rel="noopener noreferrer"
                          className="text-xs text-blue-700 hover:text-blue-800 hover:underline flex items-center gap-0.5 shrink-0"
                        >
                          <Eye size={12} />
                          <span>Abrir</span>
                        </a>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* AÇÃO C: REPORTAR ANOMALIA / AVARIA */}
              <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md">
                {!reportIssue ? (
                  <button
                    onClick={() => setReportIssue(true)}
                    className="w-full py-2 bg-white hover:bg-red-50 border border-red-300 text-red-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Activity size={14} />
                    <span>Reportar Anomalia / Avaria na Viatura</span>
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <form onSubmit={handleReportIssueSubmit} className="space-y-3 animate-fade-in">
                    <span className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 uppercase tracking-wide block border-b border-slate-200 pb-2 mb-2">
                      Nova Ordem de Serviço (Avaria Corretiva)
                    </span>
                    
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Título da Avaria / Intervenção *</label>
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={issueTitle}
                        onChange={(e) => setIssueTitle(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: Fuga de óleo no retentor ou motor com ruído"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Descrição detalhada do problema *</label>
                      <textarea
                        required
                        rows={2}
                        value={issueDescription}
                        onChange={(e) => setReportIssueDescription(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="Descreva minuciosamente o problema técnico encontrado..."
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Contador no Momento ({isVehicle ? 'km' : 'horas'}) *</label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          required
                          min={activeAsset.current_meter ?? activeAsset.currentMeter ?? 0}
                          value={issueMeterAtService}
                          onChange={(e) => setIssueMeterAtService(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">Prioridade Sugerida *</label>
                        <select
                          value={issuePriority}
                          onChange={(e) => setIssuePriority(e.target.value as any)}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <option value="low">Baixa</option>
                          <option value="medium">Média</option>
                          <option value="high">Alta</option>
                          <option value="urgent">Urgente</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    
                    {issueSuccess && (
                      <div className="text-xs text-emerald-700 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 py-1">
                        <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                        <span>Ordem de Manutenção Corretiva Aberta!</span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2 border-t border-slate-200">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setReportIssue(false)}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md"
                      >
                        Cancelar
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={submittingIssue || !issueTitle.trim() || !issueDescription.trim() || issueMeterAtService === ''}
                        className="px-3 py-1.5 bg-red-700 hover:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        {submittingIssue ? (
                          <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" />
                        ) : (
                          <span>Abrir OS Corretiva</span>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </form>
                )}
              </div>

              {/* Botão para ler outro código */}
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setActiveAsset(null);
                  setDocuments([]);
                  startCamera();
                }}
                className="w-full py-2 border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 bg-white text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Escanear Outro Equipamento
              </button>

            </div>
          )}

        </div>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export const formatMoney = (val: number, currency: string = 'MZN') => {
  const num = (val || 0).toLocaleString('pt-MZ', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 });
  return `${num} ${currency}`;
};

export const useCurrentDomain = (): DomainConfig => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const scope = user?.operational_scope || user?.tenant?.operational_scope || user?.tenant?.operationalScope;
  const domain: 'fleet' | 'plant' = scope === 'plant' ? 'plant' : 'fleet';
  return DOMAIN_REGISTRY[domain];
};

// Subtipos exclusivos para categoria CAT-06 (Trelas/Reboques)
export const TRAILER_SUBTYPES = [
  'Carga geral', 'Plataforma', 'Basculante', 'Cisterna', 'Frigorífica', 'Porta-contentores', 'Transporte de máquinas', 'Extensível', 'Silo', 'Madeira/toros', 'Animais', 'Outro (especificar)'

export const ENERGY_TYPES = ['Diesel', 'Gasolina', 'Elétrico', 'Híbrido', 'GPL', 'GNV'];
export const OWNERSHIP_TYPES = ['Próprio', 'Alugado', 'Leasing', 'Cedido'];

// ==============================================
// SINCRONIZAÇÃO DE CATEGORIAS COM O BACKEND
// ==============================================
// O backend (POST/PUT /api/assets) só aceita as categorias canónicas abaixo
// (ver ASSET_CATEGORIES em src/routes/assets.js). Códigos fora dessa lista
// (CAT-06, CAT-07, CAT-08, CAT-09, CAT-14, other_equipment) são rejeitados
// com 400 "Categoria inválida" e o CAT-03 do front (veículos pesados) seria
// convertido em "machinery" pelo backend. Por isso, o envio ao backend é
// sempre feito através de toBackendCategory(), preservando o código fino
// (CAT-xx) apenas na interface (onde alimenta a lista de tipos/subtipos).
export const BACKEND_ASSET_CATEGORIES = [
  'vehicle_light',
  'vehicle_heavy',
  'machinery',
  'plant_equipment',
  'generator',
] as const;

const UI_CATEGORY_TO_BACKEND: Record<string, string> = {
  'CAT-01': 'vehicle_light', // Veículos ligeiros
  'CAT-02': 'vehicle_light', // Veículos comerciais ligeiros
  'CAT-03': 'vehicle_heavy', // Veículos pesados de mercadorias
  'CAT-06': 'vehicle_heavy', // Reboques e semirreboques
  'CAT-07': 'machinery', // Tratores e máquinas agrícolas
  'CAT-08': 'machinery', // Máquinas de construção civil
  'CAT-09': 'machinery', // Equipamentos de movimentação de cargas
  'CAT-14': 'machinery', // Outros equipamentos móveis terrestres
};

// Converte a categoria selecionada no formulário para o valor canónico aceite pelo backend.
// Valores já canónicos (ex.: generator, machinery) são preservados.
export const toBackendCategory = (
  category?: string | null,
  domain?: 'fleet' | 'plant',
): string => {
  const value = (category ?? '').trim();
  if (!value) return value;
  if (value === 'other_equipment') {
    return domain === 'plant' ? 'plant_equipment' : 'machinery';
  }
  if ((BACKEND_ASSET_CATEGORIES as readonly string[]).includes(value)) return value;
  return UI_CATEGORY_TO_BACKEND[value] ?? value;
};


// ===== src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx =====
import React, { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';

export type UserRole = 'super_admin' | 'tenant_admin' | 'supervisor' | 'technician' | 'driver_operator';

export interface User {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  avatar_url?: string;
  role: UserRole;
  tenantName: string;
  tenantId: string;
  operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
  operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
  isSuspended?: boolean;
  suspensionReason?: string;
  isActive?: boolean;
  is_active?: boolean;
  tenant?: {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    max_users?: number;
    maxUsers?: number;
    operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
    operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
  };
}

const decodeJwtPayload = (token?: string | null): Record<string, any> => {
  try {
    const encodedPayload = token?.split('.')[1];
    if (!encodedPayload) return {};
    const base64 = encodedPayload.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    const decoded = atob(base64.padEnd(Math.ceil(base64.length / 4) * 4, '='));
    const bytes = Uint8Array.from(decoded, (character) => character.charCodeAt(0));
    return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(bytes));
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
};

const toOptionalBoolean = (value: unknown): boolean | undefined => {
  if (typeof value === 'boolean') return value;
  if (value === 1 || value === '1' || value === 'true') return true;
  if (value === 0 || value === '0' || value === 'false') return false;
  return undefined;
};

const normalizeUser = (userData: any, token?: string | null): User => {
  const tokenClaims = decodeJwtPayload(token);
  const claims = tokenClaims.user || tokenClaims;
  const active = toOptionalBoolean(
    userData.isActive ?? userData.is_active ?? claims.isActive ?? claims.is_active ?? tokenClaims.isActive ?? tokenClaims.is_active
  );
  const suspended = toOptionalBoolean(
    userData.isSuspended ?? userData.is_suspended ?? claims.isSuspended ?? claims.is_suspended ?? claims.suspended ?? tokenClaims.isSuspended ?? tokenClaims.is_suspended ?? tokenClaims.suspended
  ) ?? (active === false ? true : undefined);
  const suspensionReason = userData.suspensionReason ?? userData.suspension_reason ?? claims.suspensionReason ?? claims.suspension_reason ?? tokenClaims.suspensionReason ?? tokenClaims.suspension_reason;

  return {
    ...userData,
    isSuspended: suspended,
    suspensionReason: typeof suspensionReason === 'string' ? suspensionReason : undefined,
    isActive: active,
    is_active: active
  };
};

interface AuthContextType {
  user: User | null;
  loading: boolean;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<User>;
  setUser: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<User | null>>;
  isSuperAdmin: boolean;
  isTenantAdmin: boolean;
  isSupervisor: boolean;
  isTechnician: boolean;
  isDriverOperator: boolean;
  isFleet: boolean;
  isPlant: boolean;
  currentScope: 'fleet' | 'plant';
  setCurrentScope: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<'fleet' | 'plant'>>;
}

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextType | undefined>(undefined);

export const AuthProvider: React.FC<{ children: React.ReactNode }> = ({ children }) => {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [currentScope, setCurrentScope] = useState<'fleet' | 'plant'>('fleet');

  const fetchProfile = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/auth/me');
      const rawUserData = response.data?.user || response.data;
      // Com cookies HttpOnly, o token não é acessível via JavaScript.
      // Os dados do utilizador vêm do corpo da resposta (/auth/me).
      const userData = rawUserData ? normalizeUser(rawUserData) : null;
      if (userData && (userData.id || userData.email) && userData.role) {
        setUser(userData);
        // Inicializar o escopo padrão do utilizador
        const scope = userData.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operationalScope;
        if (scope === 'plant') {
          setCurrentScope('plant');
        } else {
          setCurrentScope('fleet');
        }
      } else {
        setUser(null);
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setUser(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchProfile();

    const handleWindowFocus = () => {
      fetchProfile();
    };
    window.addEventListener('focus', handleWindowFocus);

    // Ouve o evento de expiração de sessão disparado pelo interceptor do Axios
    const handleAuthExpired = () => {
      void logout();
    };
    window.addEventListener('cmms:auth-expired', handleAuthExpired);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', handleWindowFocus);
      window.removeEventListener('cmms:auth-expired', handleAuthExpired);
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      const scope = user.operational_scope || user.tenant?.operational_scope || user.tenant?.operationalScope;
      if (scope === 'plant') {
        setCurrentScope('plant');
      } else if (scope === 'fleet') {
        setCurrentScope('fleet');
      }
    }
  }, [user]);

  const login = async (email: string, password: string): Promise<User> => {
    try {
      const response = await api.post('/auth/login', { email, password });

      // Com cookies HttpOnly, o token de sessão é definido pelo backend via Set-Cookie
      // e gerido pelo navegador. Não é lido nem armazenado via JavaScript.
      const rawUserData = response.data?.user || response.data;

      if (!rawUserData || !rawUserData.id || !rawUserData.role) {
        throw new Error('Servidor retornou dados de utilizador inválidos.');
      }

      const userData = normalizeUser(rawUserData);

      setUser(userData);
      const scope = userData.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operational_scope || userData.tenant?.operationalScope;
      if (scope === 'plant') {
        setCurrentScope('plant');
      } else {
        setCurrentScope('fleet');
      }

      return userData;
    } catch (err: any) {
      const errorMsg = err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.error || err.message || 'Erro ao efetuar login.';
      throw new Error(errorMsg);
    }
  };

  const logout = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      await api.post('/auth/logout').catch(() => {});
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao efetuar logout no servidor', err);
    } finally {
      // Com cookies HttpOnly, o cookie de sessão é limpo pelo backend via Set-Cookie.
      // Não há tokens armazenados em sessionStorage/localStorage para remover.
      setUser(null);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const isSuperAdmin = user?.role === 'super_admin';
  const isTenantAdmin = user?.role === 'tenant_admin';
  const isSupervisor = user?.role === 'supervisor';
  const isTechnician = user?.role === 'technician';
  const isDriverOperator = user?.role === 'driver_operator';

  const isFleet = currentScope === 'fleet';
  const isPlant = currentScope === 'plant';

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider
      value={{
        user,
        loading,
        logout,
        login,
        setUser,
        isSuperAdmin,
        isTenantAdmin,
        isSupervisor,
        isTechnician,
        isDriverOperator,
        isFleet,
        isPlant,
        currentScope,
        setCurrentScope
      }}
    >
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
};

export const useAuth = () => {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (context === undefined) {
    throw new Error('useAuth deve ser utilizado dentro de um AuthProvider');
  }
  return context;
};

export const useSuspensionGuard = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const isSuspended = Boolean(user?.isSuspended || user?.isActive === false || user?.is_active === false);

  const checkSuspension = () => {
    if (!isSuspended) return false;
    alert(`Conta Suspensa. O seu acesso foi limitado a modo de leitura. Motivo: ${user?.suspensionReason || 'Contacte a administração.'}`);
    return true;
  };

  const handleActionClick = (actionFn: () => void | Promise<unknown>) => {
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    void actionFn();
  };

  return { checkSuspension, handleActionClick, isSuspended };
};


// ===== src/hooks/useEscapeKey.ts =====
import { useEffect } from 'react';

export const useEscapeKey = (onClose: () => void, isOpen: boolean = true) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' || e.key === 'Esc') {
        onClose();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);
};

export default useEscapeKey;


// ===== src/hooks/usePreventRefresh.ts =====
import { useEffect } from 'react';

/**
 * Hook para previnir o recarregamento ou encerramento acidental da página
 * no navegador (F5, fechar aba, fechar janela, botão recarregar).
 *
 * @param enabled Define se a proteção contra recarregamento está ativa (padrão: true)
 */
export const usePreventRefresh = (enabled: boolean = false) => {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!enabled) return;

    const handleBeforeUnload = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {
      // Cancela o evento conforme especificação
      event.preventDefault();
      // O Chrome requer que returnValue seja definido com uma string
      event.returnValue = '';
      return '';
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', handleBeforeUnload);
    };
  }, [enabled]);
};

export default usePreventRefresh;


// ===== src/hooks/useVersionCheck.ts =====
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';

export const useVersionCheck = () => {
  const [hasNewVersion, setHasNewVersion] = useState(false);

  const checkVersion = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/version.json?t=${Date.now()}`, {
        cache: 'no-store',
        headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-cache' }
      });
      if (!res.ok) return;

      const data = await res.json();
      const storedVersion = localStorage.getItem('cmms_app_version');

      if (!storedVersion) {
        localStorage.setItem('cmms_app_version', String(data.version));
      } else if (String(data.version) !== storedVersion) {
        setHasNewVersion(true);
      }
    } catch {}
  };

  const updateApp = () => {
    if ('caches' in window) {
      caches.keys().then((names) => names.forEach((name) => caches.delete(name)));
    }
    localStorage.removeItem('cmms_app_version');
    window.location.reload();
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    checkVersion();
    const interval = setInterval(checkVersion, 3 * 60 * 1000); // 3 minutos
    const onFocus = () => checkVersion();
    window.addEventListener('focus', onFocus);
    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval);
      window.removeEventListener('focus', onFocus);
    };
  }, []);

  return { hasNewVersion, updateApp };
};

      console.error('Erro ao buscar ativos:', err);

          err.response?.data?.message ||
          err.message ||
          'Falha na comunicação com a API de ativos.'
        );
      }
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  const fetchCombinations = async () => {
    setLoadingCombinations(true);
    setCombinationError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/fleet-combinations');
      const data = response.data;
      const list = Array.isArray(data)
        ? data
        : data?.combinations || data?.fleetCombinations || data?.fleet_combinations || data?.data?.combinations || data?.data || [];
      setActiveCombinations(Array.isArray(list) ? list : []);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar conjuntos rodoviários:', err);
      setCombinationError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível carregar os conjuntos rodoviários.');
    } finally {
      setLoadingCombinations(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenCombinationsModal = () => {
    setIsCombinationsModalOpen(true);
    setCombTruckId('');
    setCombCode('');
    setCombLeaderId('');
    setCombFollowerId('');
    fetchCombinations();
  };

  const handleCreateCombination = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!combCode.trim() || !combTruckId || !combLeaderId) {
      setCombinationError('Preencha o código, o camião trator e a trela líder.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingCombination(true);
    setCombinationError(null);
    try {
      await api.post('/fleet-combinations', {
        combination_code: combCode.trim(),
        truck_id: combTruckId,
        leader_trailer_id: combLeaderId,
        follower_trailer_id: combFollowerId || undefined
      });
      setCombCode('');
      setCombTruckId('');
      setCombLeaderId('');
      setCombFollowerId('');
      await fetchCombinations();
      setSuccessMessage('Conjunto rodoviário criado com sucesso.');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao criar conjunto rodoviário:', err);
      setCombinationError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível criar o conjunto rodoviário.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingCombination(false);
    }
  };

  const handleTerminateCombination = async (combinationId: string) => {
    try {
      await api.patch(`/fleet-combinations/${combinationId}/terminate`);
      setActiveCombinations((previous) => previous.filter((combination) => combination.id !== combinationId));
      setSuccessMessage('Conjunto desassociado com sucesso.');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao desassociar conjunto:', err);
      setCombinationError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível desassociar o conjunto.');
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAssets();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isModalOpen) return;

    let isCurrent = true;
    const fetchMaintenancePlans = async () => {
      setMaintenancePlansError(null);
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/maintenance-plans');
        const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
          ? response.data
          : response.data?.plans || response.data?.data || [];
        if (isCurrent) {
          setMaintenancePlans(data.map((plan: { id: string; title?: string }) => ({
            id: plan.id,
            title: plan.title || plan.id
          })));
        }
      } catch (err: any) {
        console.error('Erro ao carregar planos de manutenção:', err);
        if (isCurrent) {
          setMaintenancePlansError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível carregar os planos de manutenção.');
        }
      }
    };

    fetchMaintenancePlans();
    return () => {
      isCurrent = false;
    };
  }, [isModalOpen]);

  // ==========================================
  // CARREGAR DOCUMENTOS DE UM ATIVO
  // ==========================================
  const loadDocuments = async (assetId: string) => {
    setDocLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/assets/${assetId}/documents`);
      const docs = response.data?.documents || response.data || [];
      setDocuments(docs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao obter documentos:', err);
    } finally {
      setDocLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // GERAÇÃO DE QR CODE (CANVAS)
  // ==========================================
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isQrModalOpen && selectedQrAsset && qrCanvasRef.current) {
      const valueToEncode = selectedQrAsset.id;
      QRCode.toCanvas(
        qrCanvasRef.current,
        valueToEncode,
        {
          width: 200,
          margin: 1.5,
          color: {
            dark: '#0f172a', // slate-900
            light: '#ffffff'
          }
        },
        (err) => {
          if (err) console.error('Erro ao gerar QR Code:', err);
        }
      );
    }
  }, [isQrModalOpen, selectedQrAsset]);

  // Download do QR Code como PNG
  const handleDownloadQrCode = () => {
    if (qrCanvasRef.current && selectedQrAsset) {
      const url = qrCanvasRef.current.toDataURL('image/png');
      const link = document.createElement('a');
      link.href = url;
      link.download = `qrcode_ativo_${selectedQrAsset.code_tag || selectedQrAsset.id}.png`;
      link.click();
    }
  };

  // Imprimir Etiqueta
  const handlePrintLabel = () => {
    window.print();
  };

  // ==========================================
  // MANIPULAÇÃO DE ARQUIVOS (DRAG & DROP)
  // ==========================================
  const handleDrag = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    if (e.type === 'dragenter' || e.type === 'dragover') {
      setIsDragActive(true);
    } else if (e.type === 'dragleave') {
      setIsDragActive(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setIsDragActive(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];
      if (file.size <= 20 * 1024 * 1024) {
        setWizardFile(file);
      } else {
        alert('Tamanho limite de ficheiro excedido (máximo 20MB).');
      }
    }
  };

  const handleFileChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files[0]) {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (file.size <= 20 * 1024 * 1024) {
        setWizardFile(file);
      } else {
        alert('Tamanho limite de ficheiro excedido (máximo 20MB).');
      }
    }
  };

  const updateFormField = <K extends keyof AssetFormData>(field: K, value: AssetFormData[K]) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({ ...previous, [field]: value }));
  };

  const updateTechnicalSpec = (field: keyof AssetFormData['technical_specs'], value: string) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      energy_type: field === 'fuel' ? value : previous.energy_type,
      technical_specs: { ...previous.technical_specs, [field]: value }
    }));
  };

  const updateSafetyCheck = (field: keyof AssetFormData['safety_checks'], value: string) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      safety_checks: { ...previous.safety_checks, [field]: value }
    }));
  };

  const validateForm = (): boolean => {
    if (!formData.name.trim()) {
      setActiveTab(0);
      alert('O Nome do Ativo é obrigatório.');
      return false;
    }
    if (!formData.cost_center.trim()) {
      setActiveTab(0);
      alert('O Centro de Custo é obrigatório.');
      return false;
    }
    if (!formData.model.trim()) {
      setActiveTab(1);
      alert('O Modelo é obrigatório.');
      return false;
    }
    if (domain === 'fleet' && !formData.asset_type.trim()) {
      setActiveTab(0);
      alert('O Tipo de Equipamento é obrigatório.');
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  };

  const handleCategoryChange = (nextCategory: string) => {
    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      category: nextCategory,
      asset_type: '',
      asset_subtype: ''
    }));
  };

  const handleSubmitForm = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!validateForm()) return;

    setSubmitting(true);
    const isVehicle = domain === 'fleet';
    const meterKind = isVehicle ? 'odometer_km' : 'hourmeter_hours';

    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // Validações/normalizações locais para não enviar dados que o
    // backend rejeita (400) ou que se perderiam em silêncio (NaN, etc).
    // ---------------------------------------------------------------
    // Eixos: backend exige inteiro >= 1 (ou ausente).
    const axleConfigRaw = toFiniteNumber(formData.axle_config);
    const axleConfig = axleConfigRaw !== undefined && Number.isInteger(axleConfigRaw) && axleConfigRaw >= 1
      ? axleConfigRaw
      : undefined;
    // Ano de fabrico: backend exige inteiro (ou ausente).
    const manufactureYearRaw = toFiniteNumber(formData.manufacture_year);
    const manufactureYear = manufactureYearRaw !== undefined && manufactureYearRaw >= 0
      ? Math.trunc(manufactureYearRaw)
      : undefined;
    // Capacidade do tanque e potência: números >= 0 (ou ausentes).
    const tankCapacityRaw = toFiniteNumber(formData.technical_specs.tank_capacity);
    const fuelTankCapacityLiters = tankCapacityRaw !== undefined && tankCapacityRaw >= 0
      ? tankCapacityRaw
      : undefined;
    const powerKwKvaRaw = toFiniteNumber(formData.power_kw_kva);
    const powerKwKva = powerKwKvaRaw !== undefined && powerKwKvaRaw >= 0 ? powerKwKvaRaw : undefined;
    // Valor de aquisição: backend exige número >= 0 (ou ausente).
    const acquisitionValueRaw = toFiniteNumber(formData.acquisition_value);
    const acquisitionValue = acquisitionValueRaw !== undefined && acquisitionValueRaw >= 0
      ? acquisitionValueRaw
      : undefined;
    // Contadores: backend exige número >= 0 (default 0).
    const initialMeter = Math.max(0, toFiniteNumber(formData.initial_meter) ?? 0);
    const currentMeter = Math.max(0, toFiniteNumber(formData.current_meter) ?? 0);
    // Estado: o backend só aceita operational | under_maintenance | breakdown | decommissioned.
    const status = formData.status === 'maintenance'
      ? 'under_maintenance'
      : formData.status || (modalMode === 'create' ? 'operational' : undefined);

    const payload = {
      ...formData,
      // Categoria convertida para o conjunto canónico aceite pelo backend.
      // Na edição o campo é bloqueado no formulário, por isso nunca é reenviado
      // (evita que a categoria original seja corrompida/normalizada indevidamente).
      category: modalMode === 'create' ? toBackendCategory(formData.category, domain) : undefined,
      codeTag: formData.code_tag,
      assetType: formData.asset_type || undefined,
      asset_subtype: formData.asset_subtype || undefined,
      assetSubtype: formData.asset_subtype || undefined,
      energy_type: formData.technical_specs.fuel || formData.energy_type,
      energyType: formData.technical_specs.fuel || formData.energy_type,
      ownership_type: formData.ownership_type,
      ownershipType: formData.ownership_type,
      axle_config: axleConfig,
      axleConfig,
      criticalityLevel: formData.criticality_level,
      manufacture_year: manufactureYear,
      manufactureYear,
      costCenter: formData.cost_center,
      meter_kind: meterKind,
      meterKind,
      initial_meter: initialMeter,
      current_meter: currentMeter,
      currentMeter,
      // Campos do formulário mapeados para as colunas padrão do backend
      // (sem este mapeamento eram simplesmente ignorados no servidor).
      measurement_unit: formData.meter_unit || undefined,
      measurementUnit: formData.meter_unit || undefined,
      operational_shift: formData.shift || undefined,
      operationalShift: formData.shift || undefined,
      operation_start_date: formData.operation_date || undefined,
      operationStartDate: formData.operation_date || undefined,
      operator_name: formData.operator_driver || undefined,
      operatorName: formData.operator_driver || undefined,
      color_config: formData.color || undefined,
      acquisition_value: acquisitionValue,
      acquisitionValue,
      licensePlate: formData.license_plate,
      chassisVin: formData.chassis_vin,
      engineNumber: formData.engine_number,
      fuel_tank_capacity_liters: fuelTankCapacityLiters,
      fuelTankCapacityLiters,
      plantSector: formData.plant_sector,
      voltageSpecs: formData.voltage_specs,
      power_kw_kva: powerKwKva,
      powerKwKva,
      inatro_inspection_expiry: isVehicle ? formData.inspection_expiry || undefined : undefined,
      inatroInspectionExpiry: isVehicle ? formData.inspection_expiry || undefined : undefined,
      innoq_calibration_expiry: !isVehicle ? formData.inspection_expiry || undefined : undefined,
      innoqCalibrationExpiry: !isVehicle ? formData.inspection_expiry || undefined : undefined,
      insuranceCompany: formData.insurance_company,
      insurancePolicyNumber: formData.insurance_policy_number,
      warrantyExpiry: formData.warranty_expiry,
      local_supplier_name: formData.supplier || undefined,
      localSupplierName: formData.supplier || undefined,
      status
    };

    try {
      let assetId = '';
      if (modalMode === 'create') {
        const res = await api.post('/assets', payload);
        const createdAsset = res.data?.asset || res.data?.data || res.data;
        assetId = createdAsset?.id;

        if (assetId && wizardFile) {
          const documentFormData = new FormData();
          documentFormData.append('file', wizardFile);
          documentFormData.append('documentType', 'tech_sheet');
          documentFormData.append('title', 'Ficha Técnica Inicial');

          try {
            await api.post(`/assets/${assetId}/documents`, documentFormData);
          } catch (docErr) {
            console.error('Falha ao enviar documento inicial:', docErr);
            alert('Ativo cadastrado com sucesso, mas o upload da ficha falhou. Você pode reenviar o documento na lista.');
          }
        }
        setSuccessMessage('Ativo cadastrado com sucesso no CMMS!');
      } else {
        await api.put(`/assets/${editingAssetId}`, payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Dados do Ativo atualizados com sucesso!');
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      resetWizardForm();
      await fetchAssets();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar ativo:', err);
      alert(getApiErrorMessage(err, 'Erro ao guardar dados do ativo.'));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const resetWizardForm = () => {
    setActiveTab(0);
    setFormData(createInitialAssetFormData(domain, categories));
    setWizardFile(null);
    setIsDragActive(false);
    setEditingAssetId(null);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (asset: Asset) => {
    resetWizardForm();
    setModalOpenMode('edit');
    setEditingAssetId(asset.id);

    const assetData = asset as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
    const read = (...keys: string[]): string => {
      const value = keys.map((key) => assetData[key]).find((item) => item !== undefined && item !== null);
      return value === undefined ? '' : String(value);
    };
    // Categoria inicial da edição: mantém o código de UI quando existir opção
    // correspondente; caso contrário preserva o valor canónico vindo do backend
    // (o <select> é bloqueado na edição e o valor nunca é reenviado ao servidor).
    const knownUiCategory = FLEET_CATEGORIES.some((fleetCategory) => fleetCategory.code === asset.category)
      || categories.some((item) => item.value === asset.category);
    const initialCategory = knownUiCategory
      ? asset.category || ''
      : asset.category === 'vehicle_heavy'
        ? 'CAT-03'
        : asset.category === 'vehicle_light'
          ? 'CAT-01'
          : asset.category
            || (domain === 'fleet'
              ? FLEET_CATEGORIES[0].code
              : categories[0]?.value || 'vehicle_light');
    const initialData = createInitialAssetFormData(domain, categories);
    const technicalSpecs = assetData.technical_specs as AssetFormData['technical_specs'] | undefined;
    const safetyChecks = assetData.safety_checks as AssetFormData['safety_checks'] | undefined;

    setFormData({
      ...initialData,
      category: initialCategory,
      asset_type: read('asset_type', 'assetType'),
      asset_subtype: read('asset_subtype', 'assetSubtype'),
      code_tag: read('code_tag', 'codeTag'),
      name: asset.name || '',
      company: read('company'),
      department: read('department'),
      cost_center: read('cost_center', 'costCenter'),
      status: read('status') || 'operational',
      criticality_level: read('criticality_level', 'criticalityLevel') || 'B',
      operator_driver: read('operator_driver', 'operatorDriver', 'operator_name', 'operatorName'),
      brand: asset.brand || '',
      model: asset.model || '',
      manufacture_year: read('manufacture_year', 'manufactureYear') || initialData.manufacture_year,
      serial_number: read('serial_number', 'serialNumber'),
      license_plate: read('license_plate', 'licensePlate'),
      chassis_vin: read('chassis_vin', 'chassisVin'),
      engine_number: read('engine_number', 'engineNumber'),
      color: read('color', 'color_config', 'colorConfig'),
      energy_type: read('energy_type', 'energyType') || 'Diesel',
      ownership_type: read('ownership_type', 'ownershipType') || 'Próprio',
      axle_config: read('axle_config', 'axleConfig'),
      plant_sector: read('plant_sector', 'plantSector'),
      voltage_specs: read('voltage_specs', 'voltageSpecs'),
      power_kw_kva: read('power_kw_kva', 'powerKwKva'),
      initial_meter: read('initial_meter', 'initialMeter') || '0',
      current_meter: read('current_meter', 'currentMeter') || '0',
      meter_unit: read('meter_unit', 'meterUnit', 'measurement_unit', 'measurementUnit') || initialData.meter_unit,
      shift: read('shift', 'operational_shift', 'operationalShift'),
      acquisition_date: read('acquisition_date', 'acquisitionDate'),
      operation_date: read('operation_date', 'operationDate', 'operation_start_date', 'operationStartDate'),
      acquisition_value: read('acquisition_value', 'acquisitionValue'),
      currency: read('currency') || 'MZN',
      supplier: read('supplier', 'local_supplier_name', 'localSupplierName'),
      warranty_expiry: read('warranty_expiry', 'warrantyExpiry'),
      insurance_company: read('insurance_company', 'insuranceCompany'),
      insurance_policy_number: read('insurance_policy_number', 'insurancePolicyNumber'),
      insurance_expiry: read('insurance_expiry', 'insuranceExpiry'),
      inspection_expiry: read(
        'inspection_expiry',
        domain === 'fleet' ? 'inatro_inspection_expiry' : 'innoq_calibration_expiry',
        domain === 'fleet' ? 'inatroInspectionExpiry' : 'innoqCalibrationExpiry'
      ) || read('pressure_test_expiry', 'pressureTestExpiry'),
      maintenance_plan_id: read('maintenance_plan_id', 'maintenancePlanId'),
      maintenance_type: read('maintenance_type', 'maintenanceType'),
      maintenance_periodicity: read('maintenance_periodicity', 'maintenancePeriodicity'),
      technical_specs: {
        ...initialData.technical_specs,
        ...technicalSpecs,
        fuel: technicalSpecs?.fuel || read('energy_type', 'energyType') || initialData.technical_specs.fuel
      },
      safety_checks: {
        ...initialData.safety_checks,
        ...safetyChecks,
        warning_triangle: safetyChecks?.warning_triangle === undefined
          ? initialData.safety_checks.warning_triangle
          : String(safetyChecks.warning_triangle),
        first_aid_kit: safetyChecks?.first_aid_kit === undefined
          ? initialData.safety_checks.first_aid_kit
          : String(safetyChecks.first_aid_kit)
      }
    });

    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // ADICIONAR DOCUMENTAÇÃO COMPLEMENTAR
  // ==========================================
  const handleAddAdditionalDocument = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedDocAsset || !selectedFile || !docTitle.trim()) {
      alert('Preencha o título e selecione um documento.');
      return;
    }

    setIsUploadingDoc(true);

    const formData = new FormData();
    formData.append('file', selectedFile);
    formData.append('title', docTitle);
    formData.append('documentType', docType);

    try {
      // Execute o post diretamente com a instância api sem cabeçalho manual de Content-Type
      await api.post(`/assets/${selectedDocAsset.id}/documents`, formData);
      alert('Documento anexado com sucesso!');
      setDocTitle('');
      setSelectedFile(null);
      if (fileInputRef.current) {
        fileInputRef.current.value = '';
      }
      // atualize a listagem chamando api.get
      await loadDocuments(selectedDocAsset.id);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao fazer upload do documento:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao fazer upload do documento.');
    } finally {
      setIsUploadingDoc(false);
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // SOFT-DELETE (ARQUIVAR ATIVO)
  // ==========================================
  const handleDeleteAsset = async () => {
    if (!deletingAsset) return;
    try {
      await api.delete(`/assets/${deletingAsset.id}`);
      setSuccessMessage(`Ativo "${deletingAsset.code_tag || deletingAsset.codeTag}" arquivado com sucesso.`);
      setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
      setDeletingAsset(null);
      await fetchAssets();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao arquivar ativo:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao arquivar ativo.');
    }
  };

  // ==========================================
  // FUNÇÕES DE IMPORTAÇÃO EM LOTE (CSV)
  // ==========================================
  const handleDownloadCsvTemplate = () => {
    let csvContent = '';
    if (currentScope === 'plant') {
      const plantCat1 = categories.length > 0 ? categories[0].value : 'generator';
      const plantCat2 = categories.length > 1 ? categories[1].value : 'machinery';
      csvContent = `Nome,Categoria,Tag,Fabricante,Modelo,Ano,SetorFabril,Potencia,Voltagem,HorimetroInicial\n` +
        `Gerador de Emergência 250kVA,${plantCat1},G-001,Caterpillar,CAT-250,2022,Subestação Principal,250,380V,1200\n` +
        `Compressor de Ar Comprimido,${plantCat2},C-002,Atlas Copco,GA37,2021,Linha de Produção 1,45,400V,3400`;
    } else {
      const fleetCat1 = categories.length > 0 ? categories[0].value : 'vehicle_light';
      const fleetCat2 = categories.length > 1 ? categories[1].value : 'vehicle_heavy';
      csvContent = `Nome,Categoria,Tag,Marca,Modelo,Ano,Matricula,Chassis,OdometroInicial\n` +
        `Camionete Hilux 2.8 4x4,${fleetCat1},V-001,Toyota,Hilux,2022,ABC-123-MC,CH123456789,15000\n` +
        `Camião Basculante 15T,${fleetCat2},V-002,Volvo,FMX 440,2020,AFG-890-MC,CH987654321,85000`;
    }

    const blob = new Blob(['\uFEFF' + csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const link = document.createElement('a');
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', `modelo_importacao_ativos_${currentScope || 'general'}.csv`);
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  };

  const parseCsvFile = (file: File) => {
    setBatchError(null);
    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (e) => {
      const text = e.target?.result as string;
      if (!text) {
        setBatchError('O ficheiro CSV selecionado está vazio.');
        setParsedBatchData([]);
        return;
      }

      const lines = text.split(/\r\n|\n/).filter((line) => line.trim().length > 0);
      if (lines.length < 2) {
        setBatchError('O ficheiro CSV deve conter a linha de cabeçalho e pelo menos um registo de ativo.');
        setParsedBatchData([]);
        return;
      }

      const firstLine = lines[0];
      const delimiter = firstLine.includes(';') ? ';' : ',';
      const headers = firstLine.split(delimiter).map((h) => h.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));

      const rows: any[] = [];
      let hasEmptyName = false;

      for (let i = 1; i < lines.length; i++) {
        const currentLine = lines[i].trim();
        if (!currentLine) continue;

        const values = currentLine.split(delimiter).map((v) => v.trim().replace(/^"|"$/g, ''));
        const rowObj: Record<string, string> = {};

        headers.forEach((header, index) => {
          rowObj[header] = values[index] || '';
        });

        const nomeValue = rowObj['Nome'] || rowObj['nome'] || rowObj['NOME'];
        if (!nomeValue || !nomeValue.trim()) {
          hasEmptyName = true;
        }

        rows.push(rowObj);
      }

      if (hasEmptyName) {
        setBatchError('Validação de Dados: Foram encontradas linhas sem o campo "Nome" preenchido. O campo Nome é obrigatório para todos os ativos.');
      }

      setParsedBatchData(rows);
    };

    reader.onerror = () => {
      setBatchError('Falha ao efetuar a leitura do ficheiro CSV.');
    };

    reader.readAsText(file);
  };

  const handleBatchFileSelect = (file: File) => {
    if (!file.name.toLowerCase().endsWith('.csv') && file.type !== 'text/csv' && file.type !== 'application/vnd.ms-excel') {
      setBatchError('Formato inválido. Por favor, carregue um ficheiro no formato CSV (.csv).');
      return;
    }
    setBatchFile(file);
    parseCsvFile(file);
  };

  const handleBatchDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setBatchDragActive(true);
  };

  const handleBatchDragLeave = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setBatchDragActive(false);
  };

  const handleBatchDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    setBatchDragActive(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0]) {
      handleBatchFileSelect(e.dataTransfer.files[0]);
    }
  };

  const handleBatchSubmit = async () => {
    if (parsedBatchData.length === 0) {
      setBatchError('Nenhum registo válido encontrado no ficheiro CSV para importar.');
      return;
    }

    const invalidIndex = parsedBatchData.findIndex(
      row => !(row.Nome || row.nome || row.NOME)?.trim()
    );

    if (invalidIndex !== -1) {
      setBatchError(`A linha ${invalidIndex + 1} possui o campo "Nome" em branco. Corrija o ficheiro antes de importar.`);
      return;
    }

    setBatchSubmitting(true);
    setBatchError(null);

    const assetsToImport = parsedBatchData.map((row) => {
      // Categoria normalizada para o conjunto aceite pelo backend (400 "Categoria inválida").
      const rawCategory = String(row.Categoria || row.categoria || '').trim();
      const category = toBackendCategory(rawCategory, domain);
      const isVehicle = currentScope === 'fleet'
        || category === 'vehicle_light'
        || category === 'vehicle_heavy';
      const meterKind = isVehicle ? 'odometer_km' : 'hourmeter_hours';
