                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-500 block">Estado Operacional:</span>
                  <span className="inline-flex mt-1 items-center px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 rounded">
                    {selectedVehicle.status === 'operational' ? 'Operacional' : selectedVehicle.status === 'breakdown' ? 'Avariado' : 'Em Oficina'}
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* CHECKLIST DIÁRIA DE SEGURANÇA */}
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 space-y-4 shadow-sm relative">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <CheckCircle2 className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" size={16} />
                Inspeção Diária de Segurança (Checklist)
              </h2>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Marque todos os pontos regulamentares antes de iniciar o serviço.</p>
            </div>

            {checklistSuccess ? (
              <div className="bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 rounded-md p-4 text-emerald-700 text-xs text-center flex items-center justify-center gap-2 animate-fade-in">
                <CheckCircle2 size={16} />
                <span className="font-bold">Checklist guardado com sucesso! Boa viagem com segurança.</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleChecklistSubmit} className="space-y-3 pt-2">
                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-3.5 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer group">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={fluids}
                    onChange={(e) => setFluids(e.target.checked)}
                    className="accent-blue-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1 text-xs">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-slate-900">Fluidos & Óleos</span>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Níveis de óleo de motor e água de refrigeração estão corretos e sem fugas visíveis.</p>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-3.5 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer group">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={brakes}
                    onChange={(e) => setBrakes(e.target.checked)}
                    className="accent-blue-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1 text-xs">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-slate-900">Travões / Sistema de Travagem</span>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Travão de pé e travão de estacionamento (mão) operam perfeitamente.</p>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-3.5 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer group">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={lights}
                    onChange={(e) => setLights(e.target.checked)}
                    className="accent-blue-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1 text-xs">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-slate-900">Iluminação & Sinalização</span>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Faróis médios/máximos, piscas direcionais, stops e luzes de emergência limpos e funcionais.</p>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-3.5 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer group">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={tires}
                    onChange={(e) => setTires(e.target.checked)}
                    className="accent-blue-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1 text-xs">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-slate-900">Pneus & Jantes</span>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Rastos em condições regulamentares, pressão correta e pneu suplente disponível.</p>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <label className="flex items-center gap-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-3.5 hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer group">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    checked={safety}
                    onChange={(e) => setSafety(e.target.checked)}
                    className="accent-blue-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded w-4 h-4 cursor-pointer"
                  />
                  <div className="flex-1 text-xs">
                    <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 group-hover:text-slate-900">Equipamentos de Proteção</span>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Cintos de segurança operacionais, triângulo, colete refletor e extintor de incêndio carregado.</p>
                  </div>
                </label>

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={!fluids || !brakes || !lights || !tires || !safety}
                  className="w-full bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-3 px-4 rounded-md transition-all shadow-none mt-4 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                >
                  Validar Vistoria de Segurança
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* BOTÃO DE ANOMALIA / AVARIA */}
          <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-md p-5 space-y-3 shadow-sm">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertTriangle className="text-red-600" size={16} />
                Reportar Avaria ou Anomalia Mecânica
              </h2>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-600 mt-0.5">
                Detetou algum problema durante a condução? Abra imediatamente uma ocorrência para os técnicos.
              </p>
            </div>

            <button
              onClick={() => setIsAnomalyModalOpen(true)}
              className="w-full bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-none"
            >
              <AlertTriangle size={15} />
              <span>Reportar Anomalia Técnica</span>
            </button>
          </div>

        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL DE SUBMETER ANOMALIA */}
      {isAnomalyModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-md rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col relative">

            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertTriangle size={16} />
                Reportar Ocorrência em Campo
              </h3>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsAnomalyModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {anomalySuccess ? (
              <div className="p-8 text-center space-y-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center border border-emerald-200 mx-auto">
                  <CheckCircle2 size={24} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Ocorrência Registada!</h4>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1">A equipa de manutenção foi notificada e uma OS Corretiva foi aberta.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleAnomalySubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4 text-xs">
                <div>
                  <span className="text-slate-500 font-semibold block">Viatura Selecionada:</span>
                  <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold font-mono mt-1 block">
                    {selectedVehicle?.code_tag || selectedVehicle?.codeTag} - {selectedVehicle?.name}
                  </span>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Título / Resumo da Avaria *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={anomalyTitle}
                    onChange={(e) => setAnomalyTitle(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ex: Fuga de óleo sob o radiador"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-red-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none font-mono"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                    Descrição Detalhada do Problema *
                  </label>
                  <textarea
                    required
                    value={anomalyDesc}
                    onChange={(e) => setAnomalyDesc(e.target.value)}
                    rows={3}
                    placeholder="Descreva o comportamento do veículo, barulhos suspeitos ou luzes do painel acesas..."
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-red-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-red-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none leading-relaxed font-sans"
                  />
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                      Quilometragem Atual *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="number"
                      required
                      value={currentOdometer}
                      onChange={(e) => setCurrentOdometer(e.target.value === '' ? '' : Number(e.target.value))}
                      placeholder="km atual"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
                      Gravidade Indicada
                    </label>
                    <select
                      value={anomalyPriority}
                      onChange={(e) => setAnomalyPriority(e.target.value as any)}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    >
                      <option value="low">Baixa (Pode circular)</option>
                      <option value="medium">Média (Requer atenção)</option>
                      <option value="high">Alta (Intervenção urgente)</option>
                      <option value="urgent">Crítica (Imobilizar viatura)</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setIsAnomalyModalOpen(false)}
                    className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    Cancelar
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={submittingAnomaly}
                    className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors shadow-none flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {submittingAnomaly ? (
                      <>
                        <RefreshCw className="animate-spin" size={13} />
                        <span>A reportar...</span>
                      </>
                    ) : (
                      <span>Submeter Ocorrência</span>
                    )}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default AssetsPage;


// ===== src/pages/Dashboard.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { FLEET_CATEGORIES } from '../config/fleetCategories';
import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  RefreshCw,
  Activity,
  ClipboardList,
  DollarSign,
  AlertTriangle,
  Plus,
  Wrench,
  QrCode,
  Layers,
  ArrowRight,
  CheckCircle2
} from 'lucide-react';

import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';

const formatMZN = (val: number) => {
  const formatted = (val || 0).toLocaleString('pt-MZ', {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2
  });
  return `${formatted} MZN`;
};

const fleetCategoryCodes = new Set(FLEET_CATEGORIES.map((category) => category.code));
const isFleetCategory = (category: unknown) =>
  typeof category === 'string' && (
    category === 'vehicle_light' ||
    category === 'vehicle_heavy' ||
    category.startsWith('vehicle') ||
    fleetCategoryCodes.has(category)
  );
const isPlantCategory = (category: unknown) =>
  category === 'generator' || category === 'machinery' || category === 'plant';

export const DashboardPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { currentScope } = useAuth();
  const isPlant = currentScope === 'plant';

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<any>(null);

  const fetchStats = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [assetsRes, woRes, fuelRes, partsRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
        api.get('/assets'),
        api.get('/work-orders'),
        api.get('/fuel'),
        api.get('/parts')
      ]);

      // Desempacotamento seguro
      const rawAssets = assetsRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(assetsRes.value.data) ? assetsRes.value.data : assetsRes.value.data?.assets || assetsRes.value.data?.data || []) : [];
      const rawWo = woRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(woRes.value.data) ? woRes.value.data : woRes.value.data?.workOrders || woRes.value.data?.work_orders || woRes.value.data?.data || []) : [];
      const rawFuel = fuelRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(fuelRes.value.data) ? fuelRes.value.data : fuelRes.value.data?.fuelLogs || fuelRes.value.data?.data || []) : [];
      const rawParts = partsRes.status === 'fulfilled' ? (Array.isArray(partsRes.value.data) ? partsRes.value.data : partsRes.value.data?.parts || partsRes.value.data?.data || []) : [];

      // Filtragem estrita pelo currentScope do Dashboard:
      const assetsList = rawAssets.filter((asset: any) => {
        const scope = asset.domain || asset.operational_scope || asset.operationalScope;
        if (scope === 'plant' || scope === 'fleet') return scope === currentScope;
        if (currentScope === 'plant') return !isFleetCategory(asset.category);
        return !isPlantCategory(asset.category);
      });

      const woList = rawWo.filter((order: any) => {
        const matchingAsset = rawAssets.find((asset: any) =>
          asset.id === (order.asset_id || order.assetId || order.asset?.id)
        );
        const scope = order.domain || order.operational_scope || order.asset_category ||
          matchingAsset?.domain || matchingAsset?.operational_scope || matchingAsset?.operationalScope || matchingAsset?.category;
        if (!scope) return true;
        if (scope === 'plant' || scope === 'fleet') return scope === currentScope;
        if (currentScope === 'plant') return !isFleetCategory(scope);
        return !isPlantCategory(scope);
      });

      const fuelList = rawFuel; // Combustível aplica-se quase sempre a frotas, mantemos o total.

      const totalAssets = assetsList.length;
      const opAssets = assetsList.filter((asset: any) => asset.status === 'operational').length;
      // O backend usa o enum asset_status: operational | under_maintenance | breakdown | decommissioned
      const maintAssets = assetsList.filter((asset: any) => asset.status === 'under_maintenance' || asset.status === 'maintenance' || asset.status === 'breakdown').length;

      const pendingWo = woList.filter((order: any) => order.status === 'open' || order.status === 'pending' || !order.status).length;
      const inProgWo = woList.filter((order: any) => order.status === 'in_progress' || order.status === 'waiting_parts' || order.status === 'paused').length;

      const totalOpCost = woList.reduce((acc: number, order: any) => acc + Number(order.total_cost_mzn || order.totalCostMzn || 0), 0) + fuelList.reduce((acc: number, fuel: any) => acc + Number(fuel.total_cost_mzn || fuel.totalCostMzn || 0), 0);
      const downtime = woList.reduce((acc: number, order: any) => acc + Number(order.downtime_hours || order.downtimeHours || 0), 0);

      setStats({
        assets: { total: totalAssets, operational: opAssets, maintenance: maintAssets },
        workOrders: {
          pending: pendingWo,
          in_progress: inProgWo,
          total_month_cost_mzn: totalOpCost,
          total_downtime_hours: downtime
        },
        inventory: {
          lowStockCount: rawParts.filter((part: any) => (part.current_stock ?? part.currentStock ?? 0) <= (part.min_stock ?? part.minStock ?? 5)).length
        },
        compliance: {
          expiringCount: 0
        },
        recentWorkOrders: woList.slice(0, 5)
      });
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao calcular estatísticas locais:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchStats();
  }, [currentScope]);

  // Normalização segura de atributos
  const totalAssets = stats?.assets?.total ?? stats?.assets?.total_assets ?? 0;
  const opAssets = stats?.assets?.operational ?? stats?.assets?.operational_assets ?? totalAssets;
  const availabilityRate = totalAssets > 0 ? ((opAssets / totalAssets) * 100).toFixed(1) : '100.0';

  const pendingWO = stats?.workOrders?.pending ?? stats?.work_orders?.pending ?? 0;
  const inProgressWO = stats?.workOrders?.in_progress ?? stats?.workOrders?.inProgress ?? stats?.work_orders?.in_progress ?? 0;
  const activeWO = pendingWO + inProgressWO;

  const totalMonthCost = stats?.workOrders?.total_month_cost_mzn ?? stats?.workOrders?.totalMonthCostMzn ?? stats?.work_orders?.total_month_cost_mzn ?? 0;
  const totalDowntimeHours = stats?.workOrders?.total_downtime_hours ?? stats?.workOrders?.totalDowntimeHours ?? stats?.work_orders?.total_downtime_hours ?? 0;

  const lowStockCount = stats?.inventory?.lowStockCount ?? stats?.inventory?.low_stock_count ?? 0;
  const expiringCount = stats?.compliance?.expiringCount ?? stats?.compliance?.expiring_count ?? 0;

  const recentOrders = Array.isArray(stats?.recentWorkOrders || stats?.recent_work_orders || stats?.criticalOrders)
    ? (stats?.recentWorkOrders || stats?.recent_work_orders || stats?.criticalOrders)
    : [];

  const getPriorityBadge = (priority: string) => {
    switch (priority) {
      case 'urgent':
        return 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200';
      case 'high':
        return 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200';
      case 'medium':
        return 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200';
    }
  };

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'in_progress':
        return { label: 'Em Curso', color: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200' };
      case 'completed':
        return { label: 'Concluída', color: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200' };
      case 'waiting_parts':
        return { label: 'Aguardando Peças', color: 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200' };
      default:
        return { label: 'Pendente', color: 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200' };
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <LayoutDashboard className="text-[#0066FF]" size={24} />
            Visão Geral Operacional
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 leading-relaxed">
            {isPlant ? 'Monitorização do Chão de Fábrica e Equipamentos Industriais' : 'Monitorização de Frota e Manutenção de Viaturas'}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start md:self-auto">
          <button
            onClick={fetchStats}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-md border border-slate-300 transition-colors text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-2 shadow-none cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Atualizar Indicadores"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} className={loading ? 'animate-spin text-[#0066FF]' : ''} />
            <span>Atualizar Dados</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LINHA SUPERIOR - 4 CARDS DE KPIs */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">

        {/* KPI 1: DISPONIBILIDADE OPERACIONAL & ATIVOS */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 shadow-none flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Disponibilidade</span>
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center">
              <Activity size={18} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="my-3">
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              {availabilityRate}%
            </div>

            {/* Badges de Ativos Operacionais vs Imobilizados */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2">
              <span className="bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                {opAssets} operacionais
              </span>
              <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                {totalAssets - opAssets} imobilizados
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Link to="/app/assets" className="text-[11px] text-[#0066FF] hover:underline font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
            Gerir parque de ativos <ArrowRight size={12} />
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 2: ORDENS DE SERVIÇO EM CURSO / PENDENTES */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 shadow-none flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Ordens de Serviço</span>
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-[#0066FF] border border-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center">
              <ClipboardList size={18} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="my-3">
            <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">
              {activeWO} <span className="text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">ativas</span>
            </div>

            {/* Badges Compactos */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 mt-2">
              <span className="bg-blue-50 text-[#0066FF] border border-blue-200 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                {inProgressWO} em curso
              </span>
              <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                {pendingWO} por iniciar
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <Link to="/app/work-orders" className="text-[11px] text-[#0066FF] hover:underline font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
            Ver todas as ordens de serviço <ArrowRight size={12} />
          </Link>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 3: CUSTO DE MANUTENÇÃO DO MÊS */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 shadow-none flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Custos do Mês</span>
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-600 border border-indigo-200 flex items-center justify-center">
              <DollarSign size={18} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="my-3">
            <div className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight truncate">
              {formatMZN(totalMonthCost)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
            {totalDowntimeHours}h de imobilização registadas este mês
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* KPI 4: ALERTAS DO SISTEMA */}
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 shadow-none flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Alertas do Sistema</span>
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 border border-amber-200 flex items-center justify-center">
              <AlertTriangle size={18} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="my-2 space-y-1.5">
            <Link
              to="/app/inventory"
              className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors group"
            >
              <span className="text-slate-600 group-hover:text-slate-900 truncate">Stock mínimo:</span>
              <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${lowStockCount > 0 ? 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'}`}>
                {lowStockCount} peças
              </span>
            </Link>

            <Link
              to="/app/assets"
              className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs p-1.5 rounded hover:bg-slate-50 transition-colors group"
            >
              <span className="text-slate-600 group-hover:text-slate-900 truncate">Conformidades a expirar:</span>
              <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${expiringCount > 0 ? 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-800' : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600'}`}>
                {expiringCount} docs
              </span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 font-medium">
            Ações requerem atenção preventiva
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* SECÇÃO CENTRAL - ORDENS CRÍTICAS & INTERVENÇÕES RECENTES */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 shadow-none space-y-4">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Ordens Críticas & Intervenções Recentes</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Últimas tarefas operacionais registadas nas frotas e equipamentos</p>
          </div>
          <Link
            to="/app/work-orders"
            className="text-xs text-[#0066FF] hover:underline font-semibold flex items-center gap-1"
          >
            Ver Todas <ArrowRight size={13} />
          </Link>
        </div>

        {loading ? (
          <div className="py-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
            <RefreshCw className="animate-spin text-[#0066FF] mx-auto mb-2" size={20} />
            A carregar intervenções recentes do servidor...
          </div>
        ) : recentOrders.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="py-8 text-center text-slate-500">
            <CheckCircle2 size={32} className="mx-auto text-emerald-500 mb-2" />
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-800">Sem ordens críticas pendentes</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Todas as intervenções recentes foram concluídas com sucesso.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-[11px] font-semibold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Código</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Ativo</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Prioridade</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-3">Técnico Encarregue</th>
                  <th className="py-2.5 px-3 text-right">Estado</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                {recentOrders.map((order: any) => {
                  const statusInfo = getStatusBadge(order.status);
                  const techName = order.assigned_technician_name || order.technician_name || order.technician?.name || 'Pendente de Atribuição';
                  const assetName = order.asset_name || order.asset?.name || order.asset?.code_tag || 'Ativo Operacional';

                  return (
                    <tr
                      key={order.id}
                      onClick={() => navigate('/app/work-orders')}
                      className="hover:bg-slate-50 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                    >
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 font-mono font-semibold text-slate-900">
                        {order.wo_number || order.woNumber || order.id.substring(0, 8)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 font-medium text-slate-800 max-w-[200px] truncate">
                        {assetName}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3">
                        <span className={`inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase ${getPriorityBadge(order.priority)}`}>
                          {order.priority || 'média'}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 text-slate-600 truncate max-w-[180px]">
                        {techName}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-3 text-right">
                        <span className={`inline-block px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold ${statusInfo.color}`}>
                          {statusInfo.label}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

    </div>
  );
};

export default DashboardPage;


// ===== src/pages/Fuel.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api, getApiErrorMessage } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth, useSuspensionGuard } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import { formatMoney } from '../config/countriesCurrencies';
import {
  Plus,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Search,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  Calendar,
  Layers,
  Sparkles,
  Info,
  DollarSign,
  Fuel,
  TrendingUp,
  Award
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Asset {
  id: string;
  code_tag: string;
  codeTag?: string;
  name: string;
  fuel_tank_capacity_liters?: number;
  fuelTankCapacityLiters?: number;
  current_meter?: number;
  currentMeter?: number;
}

interface FuelLog {
  id: string;
  logged_at: string;
  loggedAt?: string;
  asset_id: string;
  assetId?: string;
  asset_name?: string;
  asset_code_tag?: string;
  asset?: {
    code_tag?: string;
    name?: string;
  };
  fuel_type: 'Diesel' | 'Gasolina' | string;
  fuelType?: string;
  liters: number;
  price_per_liter_mzn: number;
  pricePerLiterMzn?: number;
  total_cost_mzn: number;
  totalCostMzn?: number;
  current_meter: number;
  currentMeter?: number;
  station_name: string;
  stationName?: string;
  receipt_doc_ref: string;
  receiptDocRef?: string;
}

export const FuelPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { currentScope } = useAuth();
  const { checkSuspension, handleActionClick } = useSuspensionGuard();
  const [logs, setLogs] = useState<FuelLog[]>([]);
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<Asset[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Estados de pesquisa e filtragem
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [fuelTypeFilter, setFuelTypeFilter] = useState('all');

  // Modais e Toasts
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsModalOpen(false), isModalOpen);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Campos do Formulário
  const [selectedAssetId, setSelectedAssetId] = useState('');
  const [fuelType, setFuelType] = useState<'Diesel' | 'Gasolina'>('Diesel');
  const [liters, setLiters] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [pricePerLiterMzn, setPricePerLiterMzn] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [currentMeter, setCurrentMeter] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [stationName, setStationName] = useState('');
  const [receiptDocRef, setReceiptDocRef] = useState('');

  // Busca lista de abastecimentos
  const fetchFuelLogs = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/fuel');
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.fuelLogs || response.data?.data || [];
      setLogs(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar histórico de combustível:', err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.message ||
        'Não foi possível estabelecer ligação ao servidor para carregar o histórico de combustível.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega ativos cadastrados para o dropdown
  const fetchAssetsForDropdown = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/assets');
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.assets || response.data?.data || [];
      const filteredAssets = data.filter((asset: any) => {
        // Prioriza o domínio gravado no backend (coluna domain de GET /assets);
        // a categoria é apenas fallback para registos legados sem domínio.
        if (asset.domain === 'fleet' || asset.domain === 'plant') {
          return asset.domain === currentScope;
        }
        if (currentScope === 'plant') {
          return asset.category === 'generator'
            || asset.category === 'machinery'
            || asset.category === 'plant_equipment';
        }
        return asset.category === 'vehicle_light' || asset.category === 'vehicle_heavy';
      });
      setAssets(filteredAssets);
      if (filteredAssets.length > 0) {
        setSelectedAssetId(filteredAssets[0].id);
      } else {
        setSelectedAssetId('');
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar ativos para abastecimento:', err);
    }
  };

  // Preço de Referência e Moeda Dinâmica por Empresa / País
  const [refData, setRefData] = useState<{
    country: string;
    currency: string;
    prices: { Diesel: number; Gasolina: number };
  }>({
    country: 'Moçambique',
    currency: 'MZN',
    prices: { Diesel: 75.0, Gasolina: 86.25 }
  });

  const fetchReferencePrices = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/fuel/reference-prices').catch(() => null);
      if (response && response.data) {
        setRefData({
          country: response.data.country || 'Moçambique',
          currency: response.data.currency || 'MZN',
          prices: {
            Diesel: response.data.prices?.Diesel ?? response.data.diesel_price ?? 75.0,
            Gasolina: response.data.prices?.Gasolina ?? response.data.gasoline_price ?? 86.25
          }
        });
      } else {
        const savedPrices = localStorage.getItem('cmms_fuel_prices');
        if (savedPrices) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(savedPrices);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) {
            setRefData({
              country: parsed[0].country || 'Moçambique',
              currency: parsed[0].currency || 'MZN',
              prices: {
                Diesel: parsed[0].diesel_price || 75.0,
                Gasolina: parsed[0].gasoline_price || 86.25
              }
            });
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar preços de referência:', err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchFuelLogs();
    fetchReferencePrices();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAssetsForDropdown();
  }, [currentScope]);

  // Sugere automaticamente o preço por litro quando o modal abre ou muda o tipo de combustível
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isModalOpen && pricePerLiterMzn === '') {
      const suggested = fuelType === 'Diesel' ? refData.prices.Diesel : refData.prices.Gasolina;
      setPricePerLiterMzn(suggested);
    }
  }, [isModalOpen, fuelType, refData]);

  // Formatação de Moeda Padronizada em Meticais
  const formatMZN = (val?: number) => {
    const value = val ?? 0;
    return `${value.toLocaleString('pt-MZ', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} MZN`;
  };

  // Formatação de Litros
  const formatLiters = (val?: number) => {
    const value = val ?? 0;
    return `${value.toLocaleString('pt-MZ', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} L`;
  };

  // Submissão do Abastecimento
  const handleRegisterFuel = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!selectedAssetId || !liters || !pricePerLiterMzn || !currentMeter || !stationName || !receiptDocRef) {
      alert('Por favor, preencha todos os campos obrigatórios.');
      return;
    }

    const selectedAsset = assets.find((a) => a.id === selectedAssetId);
    const tankCapacity = selectedAsset?.fuel_tank_capacity_liters ?? selectedAsset?.fuelTankCapacityLiters;
    const currentMeterVal = selectedAsset?.current_meter ?? selectedAsset?.currentMeter;

    if (tankCapacity && Number(liters) > tankCapacity) {
      alert(`A quantidade informada não pode ser superior à capacidade do tanque (${tankCapacity} Litros).`);
      return;
    }

    if (currentMeterVal !== undefined && currentMeterVal !== null && Number(currentMeter) < currentMeterVal) {
      alert(`A quilometragem informada não pode ser inferior ao último registro (${currentMeterVal} km).`);
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);

    // Payload robusto mapeando camelCase e snake_case para maior segurança de integração
    const payload = {
      assetId: selectedAssetId,
      asset_id: selectedAssetId,
      fuelType,
      fuel_type: fuelType,
      liters: Number(liters),
      pricePerLiterMzn: Number(pricePerLiterMzn),
      price_per_liter_mzn: Number(pricePerLiterMzn),
      currentMeter: Number(currentMeter),
      current_meter: Number(currentMeter),
      stationName,
      station_name: stationName,
      receiptDocRef,
      receipt_doc_ref: receiptDocRef,
      loggedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      logged_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    try {
      await api.post('/fuel', payload);
      setSuccessMessage('Abastecimento registado com sucesso no servidor!');
      setIsModalOpen(false);

      // Limpa formulário
      setLiters('');
      setPricePerLiterMzn('');
      setCurrentMeter('');
      setStationName('');
      setReceiptDocRef('');

      // Recarrega os dados de histórico e lista de ativos com novo contador
      await fetchFuelLogs();
      await fetchAssetsForDropdown();

      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao guardar abastecimento:', err);
      alert(getApiErrorMessage(err, 'Erro ao guardar abastecimento no servidor.'));
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  // Filtros aplicados localmente
  const filteredLogs = logs.filter((log) => {
    const code = (log.asset_code_tag || log.asset?.code_tag || log.asset_id?.substring(0, 8) || '').toLowerCase();
    const name = (log.asset_name || log.asset?.name || '').toLowerCase();
    const station = (log.station_name || log.stationName || '').toLowerCase();
    const receipt = (log.receipt_doc_ref || log.receiptDocRef || '').toLowerCase();
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();

    const matchesSearch =
      code.includes(query) ||
      name.includes(query) ||
      station.includes(query) ||
      receipt.includes(query);

    const matchesFuelType = 
      fuelTypeFilter === 'all' || 
      (log.fuel_type || log.fuelType || '').toLowerCase() === fuelTypeFilter.toLowerCase();

    return matchesSearch && matchesFuelType;
  });

  // Métricas rápidas calculadas
  const totalLiters = filteredLogs.reduce((acc, log) => acc + Number(log.liters || 0), 0);
  const totalCost = filteredLogs.reduce((acc, log) => {
    const liters = Number(log.liters || 0);
    const cost = Number(log.total_cost_mzn || log.totalCostMzn || (liters * Number(log.price_per_liter_mzn || log.pricePerLiterMzn || 0)));
    return acc + cost;
  }, 0);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* Toast de Sucesso */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-fade-in">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Consumo Atualizado</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Cabeçalho */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>Gestão de Combustível</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Controlo analítico de abastecimentos, verificação de recibos e eficiência energética da frota.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={fetchFuelLogs}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Recarregar histórico"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => handleActionClick(() => {
              fetchAssetsForDropdown();
              setIsModalOpen(true);
            })}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            <span>Registar Abastecimento</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Alerta de erro da API */}
      {error && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start">
          <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="flex-1">
            <span className="font-bold block text-red-800 mb-0.5">Erro no Histórico de Combustível</span>
            <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{error}</span>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={fetchFuelLogs}
            className="text-xs font-medium bg-white text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-red-300 transition-colors"
          >
            Tentar Novamente
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grid de Estatísticas Rápidas baseadas nos filtros */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-5 rounded-md shadow-none">
          <div className="mb-2">
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider">PREÇO DE REFERÊNCIA</span>
            <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 block mt-0.5">Valores oficiais em vigor no país da empresa</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center py-2 border-b border-slate-100">
            <span className="text-slate-600 text-xs font-medium">Gasóleo (Diesel) / Litro</span>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">{formatMoney(refData.prices.Diesel, refData.currency)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center py-2">
            <span className="text-slate-600 text-xs font-medium">Gasolina / Litro</span>
            <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">{formatMoney(refData.prices.Gasolina, refData.currency)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-5 rounded-md shadow-none flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Combustível Total Adquirido</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono tracking-tight block mt-1">
              {loading ? '--- L' : formatLiters(totalLiters)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-2">Volume cumulativo dos registros filtrados</span>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-5 rounded-md shadow-none flex flex-col justify-between">
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">Investimento Acumulado</span>
            <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-700 font-mono tracking-tight block mt-1">
              {loading ? `--- ${refData.currency}` : formatMoney(totalCost, refData.currency)}
            </span>
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-2">Custo total dos abastecimentos faturados</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Caixa de Pesquisa e Filtros */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 shadow-none">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search size={14} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Filtrar por matrícula, nome, posto, ou nº de recibo..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
        <div>
          <select
            value={fuelTypeFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setFuelTypeFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-700 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Qualquer Tipo</option>
            <option value="diesel">Diesel (Gasóleo)</option>
            <option value="gasolina">Gasolina</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabela de Abastecimentos */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        {loading ? (
          /* SKELETON */
          <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="h-4 bg-slate-100 rounded w-1/4 animate-pulse"></div>
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="flex gap-4 items-center py-2.5">
                <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded w-12 animate-pulse"></div>
                <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded flex-1 animate-pulse"></div>
                <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded w-24 animate-pulse"></div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : filteredLogs.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 px-4">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border border-slate-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Fuel size={20} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Nenhum Registo de Combustível</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
              Não existem abastecimentos correspondentes aos filtros aplicados na base de dados real.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-50">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Data Abastecimento</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Viatura / Ativo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Tipo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Litragem</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Preço / L</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Custo Abastecido</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Odómetro</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Posto</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Fatura / Recibo</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredLogs.map((log) => {
                  const matchingAsset = assets.find(a => a.id === log.asset_id || a.id === log.assetId);
                  
                  let assetCode = log.asset_code_tag || log.asset?.code_tag || log.asset_id?.substring(0, 8) || '---';
                  let assetLabel = log.asset_name || log.asset?.name || 'Equipamento';
                  if (matchingAsset) {
                    assetCode = matchingAsset.code_tag || matchingAsset.codeTag || assetCode;
                    assetLabel = matchingAsset.name || assetLabel;
                  }

                  const loggedDate = log.logged_at || log.loggedAt;
                  const fuelKind = log.fuel_type || log.fuelType || 'Diesel';
                  const liters = Number(log.liters || 0);
                  const price = Number(log.price_per_liter_mzn || log.pricePerLiterMzn || 0);
                  const cost = Number(log.total_cost_mzn || log.totalCostMzn || (liters * Number(log.price_per_liter_mzn || log.pricePerLiterMzn || 0)));
                  const odometer = log.current_meter ?? log.currentMeter ?? 0;
                  const station = log.station_name || log.stationName || 'Não especificado';
                  const receipt = log.receipt_doc_ref || log.receiptDocRef || 'Sem ref';

                  return (
                    <tr key={log.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {loggedDate ? new Date(loggedDate).toLocaleString('pt-MZ') : '---'}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <div className="flex flex-col">
                          <span className="font-mono text-blue-700 text-xs font-bold">{assetCode}</span>
                          <span className="text-xs text-slate-500">{assetLabel}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700">
                        {fuelKind}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono text-xs text-slate-700">
                        {formatLiters(liters)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {formatMZN(price)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono text-xs text-blue-700 font-bold">
                        {formatMZN(cost)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right font-mono text-xs text-slate-700 tabular-nums">
                        {odometer.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-xs text-slate-700">
                        {station}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-mono text-xs text-slate-500">
                        {receipt}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MODAL DE CADASTRO: REGISTAR ABASTECIMENTO */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-lg rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
            
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Fuel className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Registar Abastecimento</h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleRegisterFuel} className="p-6 space-y-4">
              
              {(() => {
                const currentSelectedAsset = assets.find(a => a.id === selectedAssetId);
                const tankCap = currentSelectedAsset?.fuel_tank_capacity_liters ?? currentSelectedAsset?.fuelTankCapacityLiters;
                const lastMeter = currentSelectedAsset?.current_meter ?? currentSelectedAsset?.currentMeter;

                const hasCapacityError = Boolean(tankCap && liters !== '' && Number(liters) > tankCap);
                const hasMeterError = Boolean(lastMeter !== undefined && lastMeter !== null && currentMeter !== '' && Number(currentMeter) < lastMeter);

                return (
                  <>
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      <div>
                        <label htmlFor="fuelAsset" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Ativo Abastecido *
                        </label>
                        <select
                          id="fuelAsset"
                          required
                          value={selectedAssetId}
                          onChange={(e) => setSelectedAssetId(e.target.value)}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {assets.length === 0 ? (
                            <option value="">A carregar ativos...</option>
                          ) : (
                            assets.map((asset) => (
                              <option key={asset.id} value={asset.id}>
                                [{asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag}] {asset.name}
                              </option>
                            ))
                          )}
                        </select>

                        {/* Limites da Viatura selecionada */}
                        {currentSelectedAsset && (
                          <div className="p-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded mt-1.5 text-[11px] text-slate-600 font-mono space-y-0.5">
                            <div>Capacidade do Tanque: <strong className="text-slate-800">{tankCap ?? 'N/A'} L</strong></div>
                            <div>Último Contador Registado: <strong className="text-slate-800">{lastMeter ?? 0} km</strong></div>
                          </div>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <div>
                        <label htmlFor="fuelKindSelect" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Tipo de Combustível *
                        </label>
                        <select
                          id="fuelKindSelect"
                          value={fuelType}
                          onChange={(e) => setFuelType(e.target.value as any)}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <option value="Diesel">Diesel (Gasóleo)</option>
                          <option value="Gasolina">Gasolina</option>
                        </select>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Alertas em Vermelho */}
                    {hasCapacityError && (
                      <div className="p-2.5 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded flex items-center gap-2">
                        <AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-red-600 shrink-0" />
                        <span>Aviso: O abastecimento excede a capacidade máxima do tanque ({tankCap} Litros).</span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {hasMeterError && (
                      <div className="p-2.5 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded flex items-center gap-2">
                        <AlertTriangle size={14} className="text-red-600 shrink-0" />
                        <span>Aviso: A quilometragem informada não pode ser inferior ao odómetro atual ({lastMeter} km).</span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      <div>
                        <label htmlFor="litersInput" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Litros Abastecidos *
                        </label>
                        <div className="relative">
                          <input
                            id="litersInput"
                            type="number"
                            step="0.01"
                            required
                            min={0.1}
                            value={liters}
                            onChange={(e) => setLiters(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                            placeholder="ex: 120,50"
                            className={`w-full bg-white border rounded-md px-3.5 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none ${
                              hasCapacityError ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500' : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600'
                            }`}
                          />
                          <span className="absolute right-3 top-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">LITROS</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label htmlFor="priceInput" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Preço por Litro ({refData.currency}) *
                        </label>
                        <div className="relative">
                          <input
                            id="priceInput"
                            type="number"
                            step="0.01"
                            required
                            min={0.1}
                            value={pricePerLiterMzn}
                            onChange={(e) => setPricePerLiterMzn(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                            placeholder="ex: 75,00"
                            className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3.5 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                          />
                          <span className="absolute right-3 top-2 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500">{refData.currency}</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                      <div>
                        <label htmlFor="meterInput" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Odómetro Atual (km) *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="meterInput"
                          type="number"
                          required
                          min={0}
                          value={currentMeter}
                          onChange={(e) => setCurrentMeter(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                          placeholder="ex: 142500"
                          className={`w-full bg-white border rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none ${
                            hasMeterError ? 'border-red-500 focus:ring-red-500' : 'border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600'
                          }`}
                        />
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <label htmlFor="station" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Posto de Abastecimento *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          id="station"
                          type="text"
                          required
                          value={stationName}
                          onChange={(e) => setStationName(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="ex: Petromoc Maputo"
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                        />
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="receipt" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Referência do Recibo / Fatura *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="receipt"
                        type="text"
                        required
                        value={receiptDocRef}
                        onChange={(e) => setReceiptDocRef(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: FAT-2026/8942"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>

                    {/* Pré-visualização do Custo Estimado */}
                    {liters && pricePerLiterMzn && !hasCapacityError && (
                      <div className="p-3 bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                        <span className="font-medium text-blue-700 flex items-center gap-1">
                          <DollarSign size={14} />
                          Custo Total Calculado:
                        </span>
                        <span className="font-mono font-bold text-slate-900 text-sm">
                          {formatMoney(Number(liters) * Number(pricePerLiterMzn), refData.currency)}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {/* Botões */}
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 mt-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                        disabled={submitting}
                        className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        Cancelar
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={submitting || hasCapacityError || hasMeterError}
                        className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                      >
                        {submitting ? (
                          <>
                            <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />
                            <span>A guardar...</span>
                          </>
                        ) : (
                          <span>Guardar Abastecimento</span>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </>
                );
              })()}
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default FuelPage;


// ===== src/pages/Inventory.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api, getApiErrorMessage } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCurrentDomain } from '../config/domainRegistry';
import {
  Plus,
  Layers,
  Search,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Package,
  MapPin,
  TrendingUp,
  DollarSign,
  ArrowRightLeft,
  Loader2,
  ArrowDownToLine,
  ArrowUpFromLine
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Part {
  id: string;
  oem_reference?: string;
  oemReference?: string;
  part_number?: string;
  name: string;
  category: 'mechanical' | 'electrical' | 'hydraulic' | 'pneumatic' | 'lubricants' | 'tires_wheels' | 'other' | string;
  location?: string;
  warehouse_location?: string;
  warehouseLocation?: string;
  current_stock?: number;
  currentStock?: number;
  min_stock?: number;
  minStock?: number;
  unit_cost?: number;
  unitCost?: number;
  created_at?: string;
}

export const InventoryPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, isTenantAdmin, isSupervisor } = useAuth();
  const { partCategories } = useCurrentDomain();
  
  // Lista de Peças e Estados de Carregamento
  const [parts, setParts] = useState<Part[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Filtros
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [categoryFilter, setCategoryFilter] = useState('all');
  const [stockStatusFilter, setStockStatusFilter] = useState('all');

  // Modais
  const [isPartModalOpen, setIsPartModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [partModalMode, setPartModalMode] = useState<'create' | 'edit'>('create');
  const [editingPartId, setEditingPartId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [isStockModalOpen, setIsStockModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedStockPart, setSelectedStockPart] = useState<Part | null>(null);
  const [stockActionType, setStockActionType] = useState<'input' | 'output'>('input'); // input = Entrada, output = Saída
  const [stockQuantity, setStockQuantity] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [stockUnitCost, setStockUnitCost] = useState<number | ''>('');

  const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deletingPart, setDeletingPart] = useState<Part | null>(null);

  // Formulário de Nova Peça
  const [formOemReference, setFormOemReference] = useState('');
  const [formName, setFormName] = useState('');
  const [formCategory, setFormCategory] = useState(partCategories[0]?.value || 'mechanical');
  const [formWarehouseLocation, setFormWarehouseLocation] = useState('');
  const [formCurrentStock, setFormCurrentStock] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [formMinStock, setFormMinStock] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [formUnitCost, setFormUnitCost] = useState<number | ''>('');
  const [submittingPart, setSubmittingPart] = useState(false);

  // Carregar Peças (Requisito 6.2)
  const fetchInventory = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/parts');
      const iData = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : (res.data?.parts || res.data?.data || []);
      setParts(iData);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao obter inventário:', err);
      setError('Falha ao comunicar com o servidor de inventário.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchInventory();
  }, []);

  // Submissão de Cadastro / Edição de Peça (Requisito 6.3)
  const handleSavePart = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!formOemReference.trim() || !formName.trim() || formCurrentStock === '' || formMinStock === '' || formUnitCost === '') {
      alert('Por favor, preencha todos os campos obrigatórios.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingPart(true);
    const payload = {
      oem_reference: formOemReference,
      name: formName,
      category: formCategory,
      warehouse_location: formWarehouseLocation || 'Geral',
      current_stock: Number(formCurrentStock),
      min_stock: Number(formMinStock),
      unit_cost: Number(formUnitCost),
    };

    try {
      if (partModalMode === 'create') {
        await api.post('/parts', payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Nova peça adicionada com sucesso ao inventário!');
      } else {
        await api.put(`/parts/${editingPartId}`, payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Dados da peça atualizados com sucesso!');
      }

      setIsPartModalOpen(false);
      resetPartForm();
      await fetchInventory(); // Recarrega do banco real
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao guardar peça:', err);
      // O backend atual não expõe PUT /parts/:id (rota inexistente → 404).
      if (partModalMode === 'edit' && err.response?.status === 404) {
        alert('A edição de peças não está disponível no backend: a rota PUT /api/parts/:id não existe. Crie uma nova peça ou atualize o backend.');
      } else {
        alert(getApiErrorMessage(err, 'Erro ao guardar dados da peça.'));
      }
    } finally {
      setSubmittingPart(false);
    }
  };

  const resetPartForm = () => {
    setFormOemReference('');
    setFormName('');
    setFormCategory('mechanical');
    setFormWarehouseLocation('');
    setFormCurrentStock('');
    setFormMinStock('');
    setFormUnitCost('');
    setEditingPartId(null);
  };

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    resetPartForm();
    setPartModalMode('create');
    setIsPartModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (part: Part) => {
    setPartModalMode('edit');
    setEditingPartId(part.id);
    setFormOemReference(part.part_number || part.oem_reference || part.oemReference || '');
    setFormName(part.name || '');
    setFormCategory(part.category || 'mechanical');
    setFormWarehouseLocation(part.location || part.warehouse_location || part.warehouseLocation || '');
    setFormCurrentStock(part.current_stock ?? part.currentStock ?? 0);
    setFormMinStock(part.min_stock ?? part.minStock ?? 0);
    setFormUnitCost(part.unit_cost ?? part.unitCost ?? 0);
    setIsPartModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Processar Entrada ou Saída de Material (Repor Stock)
  const handleStockUpdateSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!selectedStockPart || stockQuantity === '') return;

    const qty = Number(stockQuantity);
    const newCost = stockUnitCost !== '' ? Number(stockUnitCost) : (selectedStockPart.unit_cost ?? selectedStockPart.unitCost ?? 0);
    
    // Validar saída
    if (stockActionType === 'output' && qty > (selectedStockPart.current_stock ?? selectedStockPart.currentStock ?? 0)) {
      alert('Quantidade de saída excede o stock disponível atualmente.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingPart(true);

    try {
      if (stockActionType === 'input') {
        // POST /parts/:id/restock — rota de entradas suportada pelo backend
        // (o backend não possui PATCH /parts/:id, portanto não há fallback).
        await api.post(`/parts/${selectedStockPart.id}/restock`, {
          quantity: qty,
          unitCost: newCost,
          unit_cost: newCost
        });
      } else {
        // POST /parts/:id/withdraw — rota de saídas suportada pelo backend.
        await api.post(`/parts/${selectedStockPart.id}/withdraw`, {
          quantity: qty
        });
      }

      setSuccessMessage(
        stockActionType === 'input'
          ? `Adicionadas ${qty} unidades de "${selectedStockPart.name}" ao inventário.`
          : `Retiradas ${qty} unidades de "${selectedStockPart.name}" do inventário.`
      );
      
      setIsStockModalOpen(false);
      setSelectedStockPart(null);
      setStockQuantity('');
      setStockUnitCost('');
      await fetchInventory(); // recarrega a lista diretamente do banco de dados
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao atualizar stock:', err);
      alert(getApiErrorMessage(err, 'Erro ao processar movimentação.'));
    } finally {
      setSubmittingPart(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenStockModal = (part: Part, type: 'input' | 'output' = 'input') => {
    setSelectedStockPart(part);
    setStockActionType(type);
    setStockQuantity('');
    setStockUnitCost(part.unit_cost ?? part.unitCost ?? '');
    setIsStockModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Excluir Peça (Soft Delete)
  const handleDeletePartConfirm = async () => {
    if (!deletingPart) return;

    try {
      // DELETE /parts/:id — única rota de eliminação suportada pelo backend
      // (o fallback /inventory/:id não existe no servidor).
      await api.delete(`/parts/${deletingPart.id}`);

      setSuccessMessage(`Peça "${deletingPart.name}" eliminada do inventário.`);
      setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
      setDeletingPart(null);
      await fetchInventory(); // Recarrega do banco real
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao eliminar peça:', err);
      if (err.response?.status === 404) {
        alert('A eliminação de peças não está disponível no backend: a rota DELETE /api/parts/:id não existe. Oculte a peça por outra forma ou atualize o backend.');
      } else {
        alert(getApiErrorMessage(err, 'Não foi possível eliminar a peça.'));
      }
    }
  };

  // Auxiliares de Formatação
  const formatMZN = (val?: number) => {
    const value = val ?? 0;
    return `${value.toLocaleString('pt-MZ', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} MZN`;
  };

  const getCategoryLabel = (cat: string) => {
    const found = partCategories.find(c => c.value === cat);
    if (found) return found.label;
    return cat;
  };

  // Cálculos de Indicadores Rápidos
  const totalItems = parts.length;
  const lowStockItems = parts.filter(p => Number(p.current_stock ?? p.currentStock ?? 0) <= Number(p.min_stock ?? p.minStock ?? 0)).length;
  const totalValue = parts.reduce((acc, p) => acc + (Number(p.current_stock ?? p.currentStock ?? 0) * Number(p.unit_cost ?? p.unitCost ?? 0)), 0);

  // Filtragem
  const filteredParts = parts.filter(p => {
    const name = (p.name || '').toLowerCase();
    const ref = (p.part_number || p.oem_reference || p.oemReference || '').toLowerCase();
    const loc = (p.location || p.warehouse_location || p.warehouseLocation || '').toLowerCase();
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();

    const matchesSearch = name.includes(query) || ref.includes(query) || loc.includes(query);
    const matchesCategory = categoryFilter === 'all' || p.category === categoryFilter;
    
    let matchesStatus = true;
    if (stockStatusFilter === 'low') {
      matchesStatus = Number(p.current_stock ?? p.currentStock ?? 0) <= Number(p.min_stock ?? p.minStock ?? 0);
    } else if (stockStatusFilter === 'available') {
      matchesStatus = Number(p.current_stock ?? p.currentStock ?? 0) > 0;
