    } else if (stockStatusFilter === 'out_of_stock') {
      matchesStatus = Number(p.current_stock ?? p.currentStock ?? 0) === 0;
    }

    return matchesSearch && matchesCategory && matchesStatus;
  });

  // Apenas admins e supervisores podem aceder
  if (!isTenantAdmin && !isSupervisor) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-8 text-center max-w-md mx-auto my-12 shadow-none">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
          <AlertTriangle size={32} />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Acesso Restrito</h3>
        <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          O módulo de Inventário e Armazém é acessível apenas a Administradores da Empresa e Supervisores de Manutenção.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* Toast de Sucesso */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-bounce">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-blue-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Gestão de Peças</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Cabeçalho */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 animate-fade-in">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>Gestão de Inventário</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Controle de stocks de peças sobressalentes, rastreabilidade de consumíveis e alertas de rotura em Moçambique.
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={fetchInventory}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
            title="Recarregar"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
          </button>
          
          <button
            onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 active:bg-blue-900 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            <span>Nova Peça</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Indicadores Rápidos (Requisito 1.1) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Package size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-semibold tracking-wider">Total de Itens</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 block mt-0.5">{totalItems}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <AlertTriangle size={20} className={lowStockItems > 0 ? "animate-pulse text-red-600" : ""} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-semibold tracking-wider">Itens Abaixo do Mínimo (Alerta)</span>
            <span className={`text-xl font-bold block mt-0.5 ${lowStockItems > 0 ? "text-red-700" : "text-slate-900"}`}>
              {lowStockItems}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <TrendingUp size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 uppercase font-semibold tracking-wider">Valor Total Imobilizado</span>
            <span className="text-lg font-bold text-blue-700 block mt-0.5">{formatMZN(totalValue)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Caixa de Busca e Filtros */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-3 shadow-none">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search size={14} className="absolute left-3.5 top-3.5 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            type="text"
            placeholder="Pesquisar por OEM/Referência, nome da peça ou localização no armazém..."
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 placeholder-slate-400 focus:outline-none"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <select
            value={categoryFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setCategoryFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-700 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
          >
            <option value="all">Todas as Categorias</option>
            {partCategories.map((c) => (
              <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>
                {c.label}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          <select
            value={stockStatusFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setStockStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
            className="bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-700 focus:outline-none"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os Stocks</option>
            <option value="low">Abaixo do Mínimo</option>
            <option value="available">Disponível em Stock</option>
            <option value="out_of_stock">Esgotado</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Tabela de Peças (Requisito 1.2) */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
            <div className="h-4 bg-slate-100 rounded w-1/4 animate-pulse"></div>
            {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
              <div key={n} className="flex gap-4 items-center py-2">
                <div className="h-8 bg-slate-100 rounded flex-1 animate-pulse"></div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : filteredParts.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="text-center py-16 px-4">
            <Package size={36} className="text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-4" />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">Nenhuma peça encontrada</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">Registe novas peças sobressalentes para o armazém.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 font-semibold uppercase text-[10px] bg-slate-50">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Referência / OEM</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Nome da Peça / Artigo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Categoria</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Localização</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">Stock Atual</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Custo Unitário</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-center">Ações Rápidas</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                {filteredParts.map((part) => {
                  const oemRef = part.part_number || part.oem_reference || part.oemReference || 'S/N';
                  const loc = part.location || part.warehouse_location || part.warehouseLocation || 'Geral';
                  const currentStock = Number(part.current_stock ?? part.currentStock ?? 0);
                  const minStock = Number(part.min_stock ?? part.minStock ?? 0);
                  const unitCost = Number(part.unit_cost ?? part.unitCost ?? 0);
                  const isLowStock = currentStock <= minStock;
                  
                  return (
                    <tr key={part.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono font-semibold text-blue-700">
                        {oemRef}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                        <span className="text-slate-900 font-medium block">{part.name}</span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">
                        {getCategoryLabel(part.category)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                          <MapPin size={12} className="text-slate-400" />
                          <span>{loc}</span>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">
                        {isLowStock ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-semibold bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200">
                            <AlertTriangle size={10} className="animate-pulse" />
                            <span>{currentStock} un (Min: {minStock})</span>
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
                            {currentStock} un
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right font-mono text-slate-900 font-bold">
                        {formatMZN(unitCost)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                          
                          {/* Botão Ação Rápida: Repor Stock */}
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleOpenStockModal(part, 'input')}
                            className="px-2 py-1 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white font-medium rounded-md text-[10px] flex items-center gap-0.5 transition-all shadow-none cursor-pointer"
                            title="Entrada de Material / Repor"
                          >
                            <Plus size={10} />
                            <span>Repor</span>
                          </button>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleOpenStockModal(part, 'output')}
                            className="px-2 py-1 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 font-medium rounded-md text-[10px] flex items-center gap-0.5 transition-all border border-slate-300"
                            title="Dar Saída para Serviço"
                          >
                            <span>Retirar</span>
                          </button>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(part)}
                            className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
                            title="Editar Peça"
                          >
                            <Edit2 size={13} />
                          </button>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => {
                              setDeletingPart(part);
                              setIsDeleteModalOpen(true);
                            }}
                            className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer"
                            title="Eliminar Artigo"
                          >
                            <Trash2 size={13} />
                          </button>

                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* ==========================================
          MODAL: CADASTRAR OU EDITAR PEÇA
          ========================================== */}
      {isPartModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-md rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
            
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Package className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {partModalMode === 'create' ? 'Cadastrar Peça de Reposição' : 'Editar Ficha da Peça'}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsPartModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSavePart} className="p-5 space-y-4">
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Referência / OEM *</label>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={formOemReference}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormOemReference(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ex: FIL-LF16015"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 uppercase focus:outline-none font-mono placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Categoria *</label>
                  <select
                    value={formCategory}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormCategory(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer focus:outline-none"
                  >
                    {partCategories.map((c) => (
                      <option key={c.value} value={c.value}>
                        {c.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Nome Completo da Peça / Descrição Técnica *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formName}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormName(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ex: Filtro de Combustível Secundário Cat"
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none placeholder-slate-400"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Localização no Armazém *</label>
                <input
                  type="text"
                  required
                  value={formWarehouseLocation}
                  onChange={(e) => setFormWarehouseLocation(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="ex: Prateleira B2, Gaveta 3"
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none placeholder-slate-400"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Stock Inicial *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    disabled={partModalMode === 'edit'}
                    value={formCurrentStock}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormCurrentStock(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="ex: 10"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none font-mono placeholder-slate-400 disabled:bg-slate-50 disabled:text-slate-500"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Stock Mínimo *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    value={formMinStock}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormMinStock(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="ex: 3"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none font-mono placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Custo Unitário *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    value={formUnitCost}
                    onChange={(e) => setFormUnitCost(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="MZN"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none font-mono placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsPartModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-colors"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submittingPart}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all flex items-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-50 shadow-none"
                >
                  {submittingPart ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <span>Guardar Peça</span>}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ==========================================
          MODAL: ENTRADA / SAÍDA RÁPIDA DE STOCK (REPOR STOCK)
          ========================================== */}
      {isStockModalOpen && selectedStockPart && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-sm rounded-md shadow-xl overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
            
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-white">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <ArrowRightLeft className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                <span className="font-bold text-sm text-slate-900">
                  {stockActionType === 'input' ? 'Entrada de Material' : 'Saída para Consumo'}
                </span>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsStockModalOpen(false);
                  setSelectedStockPart(null);
                }}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleStockUpdateSubmit} className="p-5 space-y-4">
              <div className="bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 p-3 rounded-md text-xs space-y-1 text-slate-600">
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Peça:</span> <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{selectedStockPart.name}</span></div>
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">OEM / Ref:</span> <span className="font-mono text-blue-700 font-bold">{selectedStockPart.part_number || selectedStockPart.oem_reference || selectedStockPart.oemReference || 'S/N'}</span></div>
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Localização:</span> <span className="text-slate-800">{selectedStockPart.location || selectedStockPart.warehouse_location || selectedStockPart.warehouseLocation || 'Geral'}</span></div>
                <div><span className="font-semibold text-slate-700">Stock Disponível:</span> <span className="text-slate-900 font-bold">{selectedStockPart.current_stock ?? selectedStockPart.currentStock ?? 0} un</span></div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Operação Técnica</label>
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setStockActionType('input')}
                    className={`py-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-md border transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${
                      stockActionType === 'input'
                        ? 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border-blue-300 font-semibold'
                        : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <ArrowDownToLine size={13} />
                    <span>Entrada (Aumento)</span>
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setStockActionType('output')}
                    className={`py-2 text-xs font-medium rounded-md border transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 ${
                      stockActionType === 'output'
                        ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 border-red-300 font-semibold'
                        : 'bg-white text-slate-600 border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <ArrowUpFromLine size={13} />
                    <span>Saída (Consumo)</span>
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Quantidade *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={1}
                    value={stockQuantity}
                    onChange={(e) => setStockQuantity(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="unidades"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-600 mb-1.5 uppercase">Novo Custo / Uni (MZN) *</label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    value={stockUnitCost}
                    onChange={(e) => setStockUnitCost(e.target.value !== '' ? Number(e.target.value) : '')}
                    placeholder="MZN"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none placeholder-slate-400"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setIsStockModalOpen(false);
                    setSelectedStockPart(null);
                  }}
                  className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md"
                >
                  Voltar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submittingPart || stockQuantity === ''}
                  className={`px-4 py-1.5 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 shadow-none ${
                    stockActionType === 'input' ? 'bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800' : 'bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700'
                  }`}
                >
                  {submittingPart ? <Loader2 size={13} className="animate-spin" /> : <span>Confirmar</span>}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* ==========================================
          MODAL: CONFIRMAR ELIMINAÇÃO
          ========================================== */}
      {isDeleteModalOpen && deletingPart && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-sm rounded-md shadow-xl p-6 space-y-4 animate-slide-up">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-50 text-red-600 border border-red-200 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center">
              <AlertTriangle size={24} />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Eliminar Artigo do Inventário</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 mt-1.5 leading-relaxed">
                Tem a certeza que deseja eliminar a peça <span className="font-bold text-slate-900 font-mono">{deletingPart.part_number || deletingPart.oem_reference || deletingPart.oemReference || 'S/N'}</span> - <span className="font-bold text-slate-900">{deletingPart.name}</span>?
                Esta ação apagará as informações do armazém de forma irreversível.
              </p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
              <button
                onClick={() => {
                  setIsDeleteModalOpen(false);
                  setDeletingPart(null);
                }}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleDeletePartConfirm}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800 text-white text-xs font-medium rounded-md transition-all shadow-none"
              >
                Confirmar Eliminação
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

    </div>
  );
};

export default InventoryPage;


// ===== src/pages/Login.tsx =====
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  RefreshCw,
  Eye,
  EyeOff
} from 'lucide-react';

export const LoginPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { login } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [capsLockActive, setCapsLockActive] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!email || !password) return;

    setError(null);
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const loggedUser = await login(email, password);
      
      // Redirecionamento condicional baseado no papel (role) do utilizador
      if (loggedUser.role === 'super_admin') {
        navigate('/super-admin');
      } else {
        navigate('/app/dashboard');
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro durante o login:', err);
      const message = err.message || 'Ocorreu um erro ao conectar ao servidor. Verifique a sua ligação à internet.';
      setError(message);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div 
      className="min-h-screen w-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-4 relative"
      style={{
        backgroundColor: '#F8FAFC',
        backgroundImage: 'radial-gradient(#CBD5E1 1px, transparent 1px)',
        backgroundSize: '24px 24px'
      }}
    >
      
      {/* Logótipo FORA do Cartão */}
      <div className="mb-6 flex justify-center z-10">
        <img
          src="https://i.postimg.cc/NfvHTQTR/avexas-logo-transp-copy.png"
          alt="Avexas CMMS"
          draggable={false}
          onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
          onDragStart={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
          className="h-10 w-auto object-contain select-none pointer-events-none"
          style={{ userSelect: 'none', WebkitUserSelect: 'none', WebkitTouchCallout: 'none' }}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Cartão do Formulário Compacto */}
      <div className="bg-white rounded-xl border border-slate-200/80 shadow-sm w-full max-w-sm p-6 sm:p-7 space-y-4 z-10">
        
        {/* Banner de Alerta de Erro */}
        {error && (
          <div className="p-3 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-lg flex gap-2.5 items-start animate-fade-in">
            <AlertTriangle size={16} className="shrink-0 text-red-600 mt-0.5" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <span className="font-bold block text-red-800">Falha na Autenticação</span>
              <span className="leading-relaxed text-red-700">{error}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              E-mail
            </label>
            <input
              id="email"
              type="email"
              required
              disabled={loading}
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full h-10 px-3 text-sm rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] outline-none transition-all"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
              Senha
            </label>
            <div className="relative">
              <input
                id="password"
                type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                required
                disabled={loading}
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => setCapsLockActive(e.getModifierState('CapsLock'))}
                onKeyUp={(e) => setCapsLockActive(e.getModifierState('CapsLock'))}
                className="w-full h-10 pl-3 pr-10 text-sm rounded-lg border border-slate-300 focus:border-[#0066FF] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#0066FF] outline-none transition-all"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowPassword(!showPassword)}
                className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-600 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                aria-label={showPassword ? 'Ocultar senha' : 'Exibir senha'}
              >
                {showPassword ? <EyeOff size={16} /> : <Eye size={16} />}
              </button>
            </div>
            {capsLockActive && (
              <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-600 font-medium flex items-center gap-1 mt-1">
                ⇪ Fixador de Maiúsculas (Caps Lock) ativado
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 text-center my-2">
            Acesso restrito a utilizadores autorizados
          </div>

          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={loading}
            className="w-full h-10 bg-[#0066FF] hover:bg-[#0052CC] text-white font-semibold text-sm rounded-lg transition-colors shadow-none flex items-center justify-center cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {loading ? (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <RefreshCw size={16} className="animate-spin" />
                <span>Acessar...</span>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <span>Acessar</span>
            )}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default LoginPage;


// ===== src/pages/MaintenancePlans.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCurrentDomain } from '../config/domainRegistry';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  CalendarClock,
  Plus,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Play,
  Pause,
  Edit2,
  Trash2,
  Zap,
  CheckSquare,
  Search,
  Wrench,
  Clock,
  Layers,
  X,
  User,
  AlertCircle
} from 'lucide-react';

export interface Asset {
  id: string;
  code_tag?: string;
  codeTag?: string;
  name: string;
  operational_scope?: string;
  operationalScope?: string;
}

export interface Technician {
  id: string;
  name?: string;
  fullName?: string;
  full_name?: string;
  email?: string;
  specialty?: string;
  role?: string;
  operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
}

export interface ChecklistItem {
  id: string;
  task: string;
}

export interface MaintenancePlan {
  id: string;
  code?: string;
  title: string;
  asset_id: string;
  asset_name?: string;
  assetName?: string;
  asset_code?: string;
  assetCode?: string;
  trigger_type: 'time' | 'meter' | 'both';
  triggerType?: 'time' | 'meter' | 'both';
  interval_days?: number;
  intervalDays?: number;
  interval_meter?: number;
  intervalMeter?: number;
  priority?: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';
  assigned_technician_id?: string;
  assignedTechnicianId?: string;
  technician_name?: string;
  technicianName?: string;
  checklist_items?: string[] | ChecklistItem[];
  checklist?: ChecklistItem[];
  is_active?: boolean;
  isActive?: boolean;
  status?: 'active' | 'paused';
  next_due_date?: string;
  nextDueDate?: string;
  next_due_meter?: number;
  nextDueMeter?: number;
  created_at?: string;
}

export const MaintenancePlansPage: React.FC = () => {
  const { user, isTechnician, currentScope } = useAuth();
  const domainConfig = useCurrentDomain();
  const { domain } = domainConfig;

  // Estados sem qualquer dado mockado
  const [plans, setPlans] = useState<MaintenancePlan[]>([]);
  const [assets, setAssets] = useState<Asset[]>([]);
  const [technicians, setTechnicians] = useState<Technician[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [loadingFormData, setLoadingFormData] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Filtros e Notificações
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal de Criação / Edição
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [editingPlan, setEditingPlan] = useState<MaintenancePlan | null>(null);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsModalOpen(false), isModalOpen);

  // Campos do Formulário
  const [title, setTitle] = useState<string>('');
  const [assetId, setAssetId] = useState<string>('');
  const [triggerType, setTriggerType] = useState<'time' | 'meter' | 'both'>('meter');
  const [intervalDays, setIntervalDays] = useState<number>(90);
  const [intervalMeter, setIntervalMeter] = useState<number>(10000);
  const [priority, setPriority] = useState<'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical'>('medium');
  const [assignedTechnicianId, setAssignedTechnicianId] = useState<string>('');
  const [checklist, setChecklist] = useState<ChecklistItem[]>([
    { id: '1', task: 'Verificar nível de óleo e filtros principais' },
    { id: '2', task: 'Inspecionar componentes de segurança e aperto geral' }
  ]);
  const [newTaskInput, setNewTaskInput] = useState<string>('');

  // 1. Consumo de dados reais da API (/maintenance-plans)
  const fetchPlans = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/maintenance-plans');
      const data = Array.isArray(res.data)
        ? res.data
        : (res.data?.plans || res.data?.data || []);
      setPlans(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar planos preventivos:', err);
      setPlans([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPlans();
  }, []);

  // 2. Carrega dependências reais do Modal (Ativos e Técnicos)
  const loadModalDependencies = async () => {
    setLoadingFormData(true);
    try {
      const [assetsRes, techsRes] = await Promise.allSettled([
        api.get('/assets'),
        api.get('/technicians')
      ]);

      if (assetsRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        const aData = Array.isArray(assetsRes.value.data)
          ? assetsRes.value.data
          : (assetsRes.value.data?.assets || assetsRes.value.data?.data || []);
        
        // Filtrar ativos do domínio ativo se especificado
        const domainAssets = aData.filter((a: any) => {
          if (domain === 'plant') {
            return a.operational_scope === 'plant' || a.operationalScope === 'plant';
          }
          if (domain === 'fleet') {
            return a.operational_scope === 'fleet' || a.operationalScope === 'fleet';
          }
          return true;
        });

        const activeAssetsList = domainAssets.length > 0 ? domainAssets : aData;
        setAssets(activeAssetsList);

        if (activeAssetsList.length > 0 && !assetId) {
          setAssetId(activeAssetsList[0].id);
        }
      }

      if (techsRes.status === 'fulfilled') {
        const tData = Array.isArray(techsRes.value.data)
          ? techsRes.value.data
          : (techsRes.value.data?.technicians || techsRes.value.data?.data || []);
        const uniqueTechs = Array.from(
          new Map<string, Technician>(tData.map((t: Technician) => {
            const key = t.email?.toLowerCase() || t.name?.toLowerCase() || t.fullName?.toLowerCase() || t.full_name?.toLowerCase() || t.id;
            return [key, t];
          })).values()
        );
        setTechnicians(uniqueTechs);

        if (uniqueTechs.length > 0 && !assignedTechnicianId) {
          setAssignedTechnicianId(uniqueTechs[0].id);
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar dados do formulário:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingFormData(false);
    }
  };

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    setEditingPlan(null);
    setTitle('');
    setTriggerType('meter');
    setIntervalDays(90);
    setIntervalMeter(10000);
    setPriority('medium');
    setChecklist([
      { id: '1', task: 'Verificar nível de óleo e filtros de substituição' },
      { id: '2', task: 'Inspeção técnica dos travões e fixações mecânicas' }
    ]);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
    loadModalDependencies();
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (plan: MaintenancePlan) => {
    setEditingPlan(plan);
    setTitle(plan.title || '');
    setAssetId(plan.asset_id || '');
    setTriggerType(plan.trigger_type || plan.triggerType || 'meter');
    setIntervalDays(plan.interval_days || plan.intervalDays || 90);
    setIntervalMeter(plan.interval_meter || plan.intervalMeter || 10000);
    setPriority(plan.priority || 'medium');
    setAssignedTechnicianId(plan.assigned_technician_id || plan.assignedTechnicianId || '');

    // Formatar checklist
    if (Array.isArray(plan.checklist)) {
      setChecklist(plan.checklist);
    } else if (Array.isArray(plan.checklist_items)) {
      const formatted = plan.checklist_items.map((item: any, idx) =>
        typeof item === 'string' ? { id: String(idx + 1), task: item } : item
      );
      setChecklist(formatted);
    } else {
      setChecklist([]);
    }

    setIsModalOpen(true);
    loadModalDependencies();
  };

  const handleAddTask = () => {
    if (!newTaskInput.trim()) return;
    setChecklist([
      ...checklist,
      { id: String(Date.now()), task: newTaskInput.trim() }
    ]);
    setNewTaskInput('');
  };

  const handleRemoveTask = (id: string) => {
    setChecklist(checklist.filter(item => item.id !== id));
  };

  // 3. Salvar Plano via API (POST / PUT /maintenance-plans)
  const handleSavePlan = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title || !assetId) return;

    const payload = {
      title,
      asset_id: assetId,
      trigger_type: triggerType,
      interval_days: Number(intervalDays),
      interval_meter: Number(intervalMeter),
      priority,
      assigned_technician_id: assignedTechnicianId || undefined,
      checklist_items: checklist.map(c => c.task)
    };

    try {
      if (editingPlan) {
        await api.put(`/maintenance-plans/${editingPlan.id}`, payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Plano preventivo atualizado com sucesso!');
      } else {
        await api.post('/maintenance-plans', payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Novo plano de manutenção preventivo criado com sucesso!');
      }
      setIsModalOpen(false);
      fetchPlans();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar plano preventivo:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao comunicar com a API ao guardar o plano.');
    }
  };

  // 4. Alternar Ativo/Pausado via API (PATCH /maintenance-plans/:id/toggle)
  const handleToggleActive = async (plan: MaintenancePlan) => {
    try {
      try {
        await api.patch(`/maintenance-plans/${plan.id}/toggle`);
      } catch {
        const currentActive = plan.is_active ?? plan.status === 'active';
        await api.patch(`/maintenance-plans/${plan.id}`, { is_active: !currentActive, status: !currentActive ? 'active' : 'paused' });
      }
      fetchPlans();
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao alterar estado do plano:', err);
    }
  };

  // 5. Gerar OS Preventiva Imediata via API (POST /maintenance-plans/:id/generate-wo)
  const handleGenerateWO = async (plan: MaintenancePlan) => {
    try {
      try {
        await api.post(`/maintenance-plans/${plan.id}/generate-wo`);
      } catch {
        // Fallback direto via POST /work-orders
        await api.post('/work-orders', {
          title: `[Preventiva] ${plan.title}`,
          asset_id: plan.asset_id,
          type: 'preventive',
          priority: plan.priority || 'medium',
          description: `Ordem de Serviço Preventiva gerada a partir do plano ${plan.code || plan.id}.\n\nChecklist:\n` +
            (Array.isArray(plan.checklist)
              ? plan.checklist.map((c, i) => `${i + 1}. [ ] ${c.task}`).join('\n')
              : 'Verificação periódica')
        });
      }
      setSuccessMessage(`Ordem de Serviço Preventiva gerada com sucesso!`);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao gerar Ordem de Serviço Preventiva:', err);
      alert('Erro ao gerar Ordem de Serviço na API.');
    }
  };

  // 6. Eliminar Plano via API (DELETE /maintenance-plans/:id)
  const handleDeletePlan = async (id: string) => {
    if (!confirm('Tem a certeza que deseja eliminar definitivamente este plano preventivo?')) return;
    try {
      await api.delete(`/maintenance-plans/${id}`);
      setSuccessMessage('Plano preventivo eliminado com sucesso.');
      fetchPlans();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao eliminar plano:', err);
      alert('Erro ao eliminar plano na API.');
    }
  };

  // Indicadores do Topo Calculados em Tempo Real (Zero Números Fixos)
  const activePlansCount = plans.filter(p => p.is_active ?? p.status === 'active').length;
  const pausedPlansCount = plans.filter(p => (p.is_active === false) || p.status === 'paused').length;

  const upcomingCount = plans.filter(p => {
    const isActive = p.is_active ?? p.status === 'active';
    const dueDate = p.next_due_date || p.nextDueDate;
    if (!dueDate || !isActive) return false;
    const diffDays = Math.ceil((new Date(dueDate).getTime() - new Date().getTime()) / (1000 * 3600 * 24));
    return diffDays >= 0 && diffDays <= 15;
  }).length;

  // Filtros
  const filteredPlans = plans.filter(plan => {
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const assetName = plan.asset_name || plan.assetName || '';
    const code = plan.code || '';

    const matchesSearch =
      !searchQuery ||
      plan.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      code.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      assetName.toLowerCase().includes(q);

    const isActive = plan.is_active ?? plan.status === 'active';
    const matchesStatus =
      statusFilter === 'all' ||
      (statusFilter === 'active' && isActive) ||
      (statusFilter === 'paused' && !isActive);

    return matchesSearch && matchesStatus;
  });

  const availableTechnicians = technicians.filter((tech) =>
    !tech.operational_scope ||
    tech.operational_scope === 'both' ||
    tech.operational_scope === 'mixed' ||
    tech.operational_scope === currentScope
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* TOAST DE SUCESSO */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-fade-in">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Operação Concluída</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <CalendarClock className="text-blue-700" size={24} />
            <span>Plano de Manutenção Preventiva</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 leading-relaxed">
            Programação sistemática de revisões por calendário, quilometragem ou horímetro corporativo.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={fetchPlans}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Recarregar"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>

          {!isTechnician && (
            <button
              onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
              className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 active:bg-blue-900 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
            >
              <Plus size={16} />
              <span>Novo Plano Preventivo</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* MÉTRICAS RÁPIDAS NO TOPO (3 CARDS CALCULADOS EM TEMPO REAL) */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Planos Ativos</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : activePlansCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Revisões Próximas (15 dias)</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : upcomingCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Pause size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Planos Pausados</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : pausedPlansCount}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* BARRA DE PESQUISA E FILTROS */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 space-y-3 shadow-none">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3">
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Pesquisar plano por código, título ou ativo..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <select
              value={statusFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="all">Todos os Estados</option>
              <option value="active">Ativos</option>
              <option value="paused">Pausados</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* TABELA DE PLANOS PREVENTIVOS OU ESTADO VAZIO REAL */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
        {loading ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center">
            <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700 mx-auto" size={32} />
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-3">A carregar planos preventivos da API...</p>
          </div>
        ) : plans.length === 0 ? (
          /* ESTADO VAZIO REAL (EMPTY STATE) */
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center">
            <CalendarClock size={36} className="text-slate-300 mx-auto mb-3" />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800">Nenhum Plano de Manutenção Preventiva</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
              Não existem planos preventivos configurados para esta empresa. Clique em "+ Novo Plano Preventivo" para programar revisões por calendário ou quilometragem.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : filteredPlans.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center">
            <Search className="text-slate-300 mx-auto" size={36} />
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 mt-2">Nenhum plano encontrado</h3>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
              Tente ajustar os critérios de pesquisa ou limpar os filtros.
            </p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-xs border-collapse">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 uppercase font-semibold text-[10px] tracking-wider">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Código & Título</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Ativo Alvo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Periodicidade / Gatilho</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Checklist</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Estado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-slate-800">
                {filteredPlans.map((plan) => {
                  const isActive = plan.is_active ?? plan.status === 'active';
                  const tType = plan.trigger_type || plan.triggerType;
                  const intDays = plan.interval_days || plan.intervalDays;
                  const intMeter = plan.interval_meter || plan.intervalMeter;
                  const assetName = plan.asset_name || plan.assetName || 'Ativo Corporativo';
                  const assetCode = plan.asset_code || plan.assetCode || '';
                  const checklistCount = Array.isArray(plan.checklist)
                    ? plan.checklist.length
                    : Array.isArray(plan.checklist_items)
                    ? plan.checklist_items.length
                    : 0;

                  return (
                    <tr key={plan.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                      <td className="py-3 px-4 font-medium">
                        <span className="text-[10px] text-blue-700 font-mono font-bold block">{plan.code || `PLN-${plan.id.substring(0, 5)}`}</span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-900 font-semibold">{plan.title}</span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 block">{assetName}</span>
                        {assetCode && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-mono">{assetCode}</span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        {tType === 'time' && (
                          <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium block">A cada {intDays} dias</span>
                        )}
                        {tType === 'meter' && (
                          <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium block">A cada {intMeter?.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km/hrs</span>
                        )}
                        {tType === 'both' && (
                          <span className="text-slate-700 font-medium block">
                            {intDays} dias ou {intMeter?.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                          <CheckSquare size={12} />
                          <span>{checklistCount} itens</span>
                        </span>
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4">
                        {isActive ? (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                            <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                            <span>Ativo</span>
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                            <Pause size={12} />
                            <span>Pausado</span>
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>

                      <td className="py-3 px-4 text-right">
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleGenerateWO(plan)}
                            className="bg-amber-50 hover:bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 border border-amber-200 px-2 py-1 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1 cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                            title="Gerar Ordem de Serviço Preventiva Imediata"
                          >
                            <Zap size={12} />
                            <span>Gerar OS</span>
                          </button>

                          <button
                            onClick={() => handleToggleActive(plan)}
                            className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            title={isActive ? 'Pausar Plano' : 'Ativar Plano'}
                          >
                            {isActive ? <Pause size={14} /> : <Play size={14} />}
                          </button>

                          {!isTechnician && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(plan)}
                                className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                title="Editar Plano"
                              >
                                <Edit2 size={14} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                onClick={() => handleDeletePlan(plan.id)}
                                className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                title="Eliminar Plano"
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={14} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* MODAL DE CRIAÇÃO / EDIÇÃO DE PLANO PREVENTIVO */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-3xl rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            
            {/* Header do Modal */}
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <CalendarClock className="text-blue-700" size={20} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {editingPlan ? 'Editar Plano Preventivo' : 'Novo Plano de Manutenção Preventiva'}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Form */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSavePlan} className="p-6 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto flex-1 text-xs">
              
              {loadingFormData ? (
                <div className="py-8 text-center">
                  <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700 mx-auto" size={24} />
                  <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-2">A carregar ativos e técnicos...</p>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Título do Plano Preventivo *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={title}
                      onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: Revisão Periódica dos 10.000 Km ou Inspeção Trimestral"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Ativo / Viatura Alvo *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        required
                        value={assetId}
                        onChange={(e) => setAssetId(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <option value="">Selecione o ativo...</option>
                        {assets.map((asset) => (
                          <option key={asset.id} value={asset.id}>
                            {asset.name} ({asset.code_tag || asset.codeTag || asset.id})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Tipo de Gatilho / Disparo *
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={triggerType}
                        onChange={(e) => setTriggerType(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <option value="meter">Por Odómetro / Horímetro (Contador)</option>
                        <option value="time">Por Tempo (Dias / Calendário)</option>
                        <option value="both">Ambos (O que ocorrer primeiro)</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    {(triggerType === 'time' || triggerType === 'both') && (
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Intervalo de Tempo (Dias) *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          required
                          min={1}
                          value={intervalDays}
                          onChange={(e) => setIntervalDays(Number(e.target.value))}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900"
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {(triggerType === 'meter' || triggerType === 'both') && (
                      <div>
                        <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                          Intervalo de Contador (Km / Horas) *
                        </label>
                        <input
                          type="number"
                          required
                          min={1}
                          value={intervalMeter}
                          onChange={(e) => setIntervalMeter(Number(e.target.value))}
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900"
                        />
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Prioridade da OS Gerada
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={priority}
                        onChange={(e) => setPriority(e.target.value as any)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <option value="low">Baixa</option>
                        <option value="medium">Média</option>
                        <option value="high">Alta</option>
                        <option value="critical">Crítica</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Técnico Responsável Recomendado
                      </label>
                      <select
                        value={assignedTechnicianId}
                        onChange={(e) => setAssignedTechnicianId(e.target.value)}
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                      >
                        <option value="">Atribuição automática / Ninguém</option>
                        {availableTechnicians.map((tech) => (
                          <option key={tech.id} value={tech.id}>
                            {tech.fullName || tech.full_name || tech.email} ({tech.specialty || 'Geral'})
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* CHECKLIST DINÂMICA DE INSPEÇÃO */}
                  <div className="pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 mb-2">
                      Checklist de Inspeção do Plano
                    </label>
                    
                    <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
                      <input
                        type="text"
                        placeholder="Adicionar tarefa de inspeção (ex: Verificar aperto de terminais)..."
                        value={newTaskInput}
                        onChange={(e) => setNewTaskInput(e.target.value)}
                        onKeyDown={(e) => {
                          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            handleAddTask();
                          }
                        }}
                        className="flex-1 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:bg-white"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={handleAddTask}
                        className="bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-900 text-white px-3 py-2 rounded-md text-xs font-semibold cursor-pointer transition-colors"
                      >
                        Adicionar
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="space-y-1.5 max-h-40 overflow-y-auto border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-2 bg-slate-50">
                      {checklist.length === 0 ? (
                        <p className="text-slate-400 text-center py-2 text-[11px]">Nenhum item adicionado à checklist.</p>
                      ) : (
                        checklist.map((item, idx) => (
                          <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center justify-between bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5">
                            <span className="text-slate-800 text-[11px] font-medium">
                              {idx + 1}. {item.task}
                            </span>
                            <button
                              type="button"
                              onClick={() => handleRemoveTask(item.id)}
                              className="text-slate-400 hover:text-rose-600 cursor-pointer p-0.5"
                            >
                              <X size={14} />
                            </button>
                          </div>
                        ))
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}

              {/* FOOTER DO MODAL */}
              <div className="pt-4 border-t border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 border border-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 font-medium rounded-md text-xs cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loadingFormData}
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white font-semibold rounded-md text-xs cursor-pointer shadow-none"
                >
                  {editingPlan ? 'Atualizar Plano' : 'Criar Plano Preventivo'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default MaintenancePlansPage;


// ===== src/pages/SuperAdmin.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  COUNTRIES_CURRENCIES,
  ALL_CURRENCIES,
  formatMoney
} from '../config/countriesCurrencies';
import {
  Plus,
  Building2,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  User,
  Shield,
  Layers,
  Edit2,
  Users,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Trash2,
  Fuel,
  Globe,
  DollarSign,
  Search
} from 'lucide-react';

interface Tenant {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  trade_name?: string;
  tradeName?: string;
  nuit: string;
  province: string;
  country?: string;
  currency?: string;
  max_assets_limit?: number;
  maxAssetsLimit?: number;
  max_users_limit?: number;
  maxUsersLimit?: number;
  max_users?: number;
  maxUsers?: number;
  operational_scope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed' | string;
  operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed' | string;
  is_active?: boolean;
  isActive?: boolean;
  created_at?: string;
}

interface FuelPriceRecord {
  id: string;
  country: string;
  currency: string;
  diesel_price: number;
  gasoline_price: number;
  scope_type: 'national' | 'company_exception';
  tenant_id?: string;
  tenant_name?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
}

export const SuperAdminPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  
  // Aba Ativa: 'tenants' ou 'fuel_pricing'
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'tenants' | 'fuel_pricing'>('tenants');

  const [tenants, setTenants] = useState<Tenant[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [statusFilter, setStatusFilter] = useState('all');

  // Modais do Tenant
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [modalMode, setModalMode] = useState<'create' | 'edit'>('create');
  const [editingTenantId, setEditingTenantId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal de Exclusão/Arquivamento
  const [isDeleteModalOpen, setIsDeleteModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [deletingTenant, setDeletingTenant] = useState<Tenant | null>(null);

  // Modal de suspensão de empresa
  const [isSuspendModalOpen, setIsSuspendModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [suspensionTenant, setSuspensionTenant] = useState<Tenant | null>(null);
  const [suspensionReason, setSuspensionReason] = useState('');
  const [suspensionError, setSuspensionError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSuspending, setIsSuspending] = useState(false);

  // Modal de Tabela de Preços de Combustível
  const [isFuelModalOpen, setIsFuelModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingFuelPriceId, setEditingFuelPriceId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEscapeKey(() => setIsModalOpen(false), isModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsDeleteModalOpen(false), isDeleteModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsSuspendModalOpen(false), isSuspendModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsFuelModalOpen(false), isFuelModalOpen);

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showAdminPassword, setShowAdminPassword] = useState(false);

  // Campos do Formulário de Empresa (Tenant)
  const [tenantName, setTenantName] = useState('');
  const [tradeName, setTradeName] = useState('');
  const [nuit, setNuit] = useState('');
  const [province, setProvince] = useState('');
  const [country, setCountry] = useState('Moçambique');
  const [currency, setCurrency] = useState('MZN');
  const [maxAssetsLimit, setMaxAssetsLimit] = useState<number>(50);
  const [maxUsersLimit, setMaxUsersLimit] = useState<number>(15);
  const [operationalScope, setOperationalScope] = useState<'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed'>('mixed');

  // Campos do Administrador Inicial (no cadastro)
  const [adminFullName, setAdminFullName] = useState('');
  const [adminEmail, setAdminEmail] = useState('');
  const [adminPassword, setAdminPassword] = useState('');
  const [adminPhone, setAdminPhone] = useState('');

  // Campos da Tabela de Preços de Combustível
  const [fuelPrices, setFuelPrices] = useState<FuelPriceRecord[]>([]);
  const [fpCountry, setFpCountry] = useState('Moçambique');
  const [fpCurrency, setFpCurrency] = useState('MZN');
  const [fpScopeType, setFpScopeType] = useState<'national' | 'company_exception'>('national');
  const [fpTenantId, setFpTenantId] = useState('');
  const [fpDieselPrice, setFpDieselPrice] = useState<number>(75.0);
  const [fpGasolinePrice, setFpGasolinePrice] = useState<number>(86.25);

  // Auto-vinculação País -> Moeda no formulário de Tenant
  const handleCountryChange = (c: string) => {
    setCountry(c);
    const found = COUNTRIES_CURRENCIES.find(item => item.country === c);
    if (found) {
      setCurrency(found.currency);
    }
  };

  // Auto-vinculação País -> Moeda no formulário de Combustível
  const handleFpCountryChange = (c: string) => {
    setFpCountry(c);
    const found = COUNTRIES_CURRENCIES.find(item => item.country === c);
    if (found) {
      setFpCurrency(found.currency);
    }
  };

  // Busca lista de empresas/tenants
  const fetchTenants = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      let data = [];
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      } catch {
        const response = await api.get('/super-admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      }
      setTenants(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar empresas:', err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.message ||
        'Não foi possível ligar ao servidor para carregar a lista de empresas.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Busca lista de preços de combustível
  const fetchFuelPrices = async () => {
    try {
      let data: FuelPriceRecord[] = [];
      try {
