        const response = await api.get('/super-admin/fuel-prices');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.prices || [];
      } catch {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem('cmms_fuel_prices');
        if (saved) {
          data = JSON.parse(saved);
        } else {
          data = [
            {
              id: 'fp-1',
              country: 'Moçambique',
              currency: 'MZN',
              diesel_price: 75.0,
              gasoline_price: 86.25,
              scope_type: 'national',
              updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
            },
            {
              id: 'fp-2',
              country: 'África do Sul',
              currency: 'ZAR',
              diesel_price: 21.5,
              gasoline_price: 22.8,
              scope_type: 'national',
              updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
            },
            {
              id: 'fp-3',
              country: 'Angola',
              currency: 'AOA',
              diesel_price: 200.0,
              gasoline_price: 300.0,
              scope_type: 'national',
              updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
            }
          ];
          localStorage.setItem('cmms_fuel_prices', JSON.stringify(data));
        }
      }
      setFuelPrices(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar preços de combustível:', err);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTenants();
    fetchFuelPrices();
  }, []);

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    setModalMode('create');
    setEditingTenantId(null);
    setTenantName('');
    setTradeName('');
    setNuit('');
    setProvince('Maputo');
    setCountry('Moçambique');
    setCurrency('MZN');
    setMaxAssetsLimit(50);
    setMaxUsersLimit(15);
    setOperationalScope('mixed');
    setAdminFullName('');
    setAdminEmail('');
    setAdminPassword('');
    setAdminPhone('');
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (tenant: Tenant) => {
    setModalMode('edit');
    setEditingTenantId(tenant.id);
    setTenantName(tenant.name || '');
    setTradeName(tenant.trade_name || tenant.tradeName || '');
    setNuit(tenant.nuit || '');
    setProvince(tenant.province || '');
    setCountry(tenant.country || 'Moçambique');
    setCurrency(tenant.currency || 'MZN');
    setMaxAssetsLimit(tenant.max_assets_limit ?? tenant.maxAssetsLimit ?? 50);
    setMaxUsersLimit(tenant.max_users_limit ?? tenant.maxUsersLimit ?? tenant.max_users ?? tenant.maxUsers ?? 15);
    setOperationalScope((tenant.operational_scope || tenant.operationalScope || 'mixed') as any);
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSaveTenant = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);
    setError(null);

    const payload: any = {
      name: tenantName,
      trade_name: tradeName,
      tradeName: tradeName,
      nuit,
      province,
      country,
      currency,
      max_assets_limit: Number(maxAssetsLimit),
      maxAssetsLimit: Number(maxAssetsLimit),
      max_users_limit: Number(maxUsersLimit),
      maxUsersLimit: Number(maxUsersLimit),
      operational_scope: operationalScope,
      operationalScope: operationalScope
    };

    if (modalMode === 'create') {
      payload.adminFullName = adminFullName;
      payload.adminEmail = adminEmail;
      payload.adminPassword = adminPassword;
      payload.adminPhone = adminPhone;
    }

    try {
      if (modalMode === 'create') {
        try {
          await api.post('/admin/tenants', payload);
        } catch {
          await api.post('/super-admin/tenants', payload);
        }
        setSuccessMessage(`Empresa "${tenantName}" registada e provisionada com sucesso!`);
      } else {
        try {
          await api.put(`/admin/tenants/${editingTenantId}`, payload);
        } catch {
          await api.put(`/super-admin/tenants/${editingTenantId}`, payload);
        }
        setSuccessMessage(`Parâmetros da empresa "${tenantName}" atualizados com sucesso!`);
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      fetchTenants();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao guardar empresa:', err);
      const msg = err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.error || err.message || 'Falha ao guardar os dados da empresa.';
      setError(msg);
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleActiveStatus = async (tenant: Tenant) => {
    const currentActive = tenant.is_active ?? tenant.isActive ?? true;
    if (currentActive) {
      setSuspensionTenant(tenant);
      setSuspensionReason('');
      setSuspensionError(null);
      setIsSuspendModalOpen(true);
      return;
    }

    try {
      try {
        await api.patch(`/admin/tenants/${tenant.id}/toggle-status`, { is_active: true });
      } catch {
        await api.put(`/super-admin/tenants/${tenant.id}`, { ...tenant, is_active: true });
      }
      setSuccessMessage(`Empresa "${tenant.name}" reativada com sucesso.`);
      await fetchTenants();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao reativar empresa:', err);
      setError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível reativar a empresa.');
    }
  };

  const handleSuspendTenant = async () => {
    if (!suspensionTenant || !suspensionReason.trim()) return;

    setIsSuspending(true);
    try {
      await api.patch('/admin/tenants/' + suspensionTenant.id + '/toggle-status', {
        is_active: false,
        suspension_reason: suspensionReason.trim()
      });
      setIsSuspendModalOpen(false);
      setSuccessMessage(`Empresa "${suspensionTenant.name}" suspensa com sucesso.`);
      setSuspensionTenant(null);
      setSuspensionReason('');
      setSuspensionError(null);
      await fetchTenants();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao suspender empresa:', err);
      setSuspensionError(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível suspender a empresa.');
    } finally {
      setIsSuspending(false);
    }
  };

  // Fuel Price Handlers
  const handleOpenFuelModal = (record?: FuelPriceRecord) => {
    if (record) {
      setEditingFuelPriceId(record.id);
      setFpCountry(record.country);
      setFpCurrency(record.currency);
      setFpScopeType(record.scope_type);
      setFpTenantId(record.tenant_id || '');
      setFpDieselPrice(record.diesel_price);
      setFpGasolinePrice(record.gasoline_price);
    } else {
      setEditingFuelPriceId(null);
      setFpCountry('Moçambique');
      setFpCurrency('MZN');
      setFpScopeType('national');
      setFpTenantId('');
      setFpDieselPrice(75.0);
      setFpGasolinePrice(86.25);
    }
    setIsFuelModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSaveFuelPrice = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setSubmitting(true);

    const selectedTenant = tenants.find(t => t.id === fpTenantId);

    const recordPayload: FuelPriceRecord = {
      id: editingFuelPriceId || `fp-${Date.now()}`,
      country: fpCountry,
      currency: fpCurrency,
      diesel_price: Number(fpDieselPrice),
      gasoline_price: Number(fpGasolinePrice),
      scope_type: fpScopeType,
      tenant_id: fpScopeType === 'company_exception' ? fpTenantId : undefined,
      tenant_name: fpScopeType === 'company_exception' ? selectedTenant?.name : undefined,
      updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
    };

    try {
      await api.post('/super-admin/fuel-prices', recordPayload).catch(() => {});
    } catch {
      // Local storage fallback
    } finally {
      let updated: FuelPriceRecord[];
      if (editingFuelPriceId) {
        updated = fuelPrices.map(fp => (fp.id === editingFuelPriceId ? recordPayload : fp));
      } else {
        updated = [recordPayload, ...fuelPrices];
      }
      setFuelPrices(updated);
      localStorage.setItem('cmms_fuel_prices', JSON.stringify(updated));
      setIsFuelModalOpen(false);
      setSubmitting(false);
      setSuccessMessage('Tabela de preços de combustível atualizada com sucesso!');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    }
  };

  // Cálculo de Métricas Globais
  const totalTenants = tenants.length;
  const activeTenants = tenants.filter(t => (t.is_active ?? t.isActive ?? true)).length;
  const totalAssetsSum = tenants.reduce((acc, t) => acc + (t.max_assets_limit ?? t.maxAssetsLimit ?? 0), 0);
  const filteredTenants = tenants.filter((tenant) => {
    const active = tenant.is_active ?? tenant.isActive ?? true;
    const matchesStatus = statusFilter === 'all' || (statusFilter === 'active' ? active : !active);
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const matchesSearch = [tenant.name, tenant.trade_name, tenant.tradeName, tenant.nuit, tenant.province, tenant.country]
      .some((value) => (value || '').toLowerCase().includes(query));
    return matchesStatus && matchesSearch;
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* Toast de Sucesso */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 bg-white border border-slate-200 text-slate-900 p-4 rounded-md shadow-lg max-w-md flex gap-3 items-start animate-fade-in">
          <CheckCircle2 className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0 mt-0.5" size={18} />
          <div>
            <span className="font-bold block text-sm text-slate-900">Operação Concluída</span>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-600">{successMessage}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Cabeçalho */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
            <span>Administração Global CMMS</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Painel administrativo global de controlo de licenças, empresas contratantes e tabela oficial de combustível.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={() => { fetchTenants(); fetchFuelPrices(); }}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Recarregar dados"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
          </button>

          {activeTab === 'tenants' ? (
            <button
              onClick={handleOpenCreateModal}
              className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
            >
              <Plus size={16} />
              <span>Registar Nova Empresa</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button
              onClick={() => handleOpenFuelModal()}
              className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
            >
              <Plus size={16} />
              <span>Definir Tabela de Preços</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ABAS DE NAVEGAÇÃO SUPER ADMIN */}
      <div className="border-b border-slate-200 flex gap-4">
        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('tenants')}
          className={`pb-3 px-1 text-xs font-bold border-b-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2 ${
            activeTab === 'tenants'
              ? 'border-blue-700 text-blue-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Building2 size={16} />
          <span>Empresas & Contratos</span>
          <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono">{tenants.length}</span>
        </button>

        <button
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('fuel_pricing')}
          className={`pb-3 px-1 text-xs font-bold border-b-2 transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2 ${
            activeTab === 'fuel_pricing'
              ? 'border-blue-700 text-blue-700'
              : 'border-transparent text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800'
          }`}
        >
          <Fuel size={16} />
          <span>Preços de Combustível por País / Moeda</span>
          <span className="bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-mono">{fuelPrices.length}</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Alerta de erro */}
      {error && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-start">
          <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-red-600 shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
          <div className="flex-1">
            <span className="font-bold block text-red-800 mb-0.5">Erro de Administração Global</span>
            <span className="leading-relaxed block text-red-700">{error}</span>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={fetchTenants}
            className="text-xs font-medium bg-white text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-md border border-red-300 transition-colors"
          >
            Tentar Novamente
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CONTEÚDO DA ABA 1: EMPRESAS & CONTRATOS */}
      {activeTab === 'tenants' && (
        <>
          {/* Grid de Métricas Globais Reais */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Total de Empresas</span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
                {loading ? '---' : totalTenants}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">Sistemas industriais ativos</span>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Delegações Ativas</span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-emerald-700 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
                {loading ? '---' : activeTenants}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">Clientes com licenças válidas</span>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Limite Total de Ativos</span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-700 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
                {loading ? '---' : totalAssetsSum}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">Capacidade contratada global</span>
            </div>
            <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-4 rounded-md shadow-none">
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold block uppercase tracking-wider">Taxa de Operação</span>
              <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-amber-700 tracking-tight mt-1 block font-mono tabular-nums">
                {loading ? '---' : '100%'}
              </span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 block mt-1">SLA do Servidor CMMS Cloud</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tabela de Empresas Contratantes */}
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 bg-slate-50">
              <div>
                <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-900">Empresas Contratantes</span>
                <span className="block text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">A mostrar {filteredTenants.length} de {tenants.length} registadas</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 w-full sm:w-auto">
                <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64">
                  <Search size={14} className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" />
                  <input
                    type="search"
                    value={searchQuery}
                    onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Pesquisar empresas..."
                    aria-label="Pesquisar empresas"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
                  />
                </div>
                <select
                  value={statusFilter}
                  onChange={(e) => setStatusFilter(e.target.value)}
                  aria-label="Filtrar empresas por estado"
                  className="w-full sm:w-36 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <option value="all">Todos</option>
                  <option value="active">Ativos</option>
                  <option value="suspended">Suspensos</option>
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
              <div className="p-8 space-y-4">
                <div className="h-4 bg-slate-100 rounded w-1/4 animate-pulse"></div>
                {[1, 2, 3].map((n) => (
                  <div key={n} className="flex gap-4 items-center py-2.5">
                    <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded w-12 animate-pulse"></div>
                    <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded flex-1 animate-pulse"></div>
                    <div className="h-6 bg-slate-100 rounded w-24 animate-pulse"></div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : tenants.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="text-center py-16 px-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-slate-100 text-slate-500 border border-slate-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
                  <Building2 size={20} />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900">Nenhuma Empresa Cadastrada</h3>
                <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                  Utilize o botão acima para registar e provisionar o primeiro cliente corporativo no CMMS.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-sm">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs uppercase tracking-wider bg-slate-50">
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Nome da Empresa</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Nome Comercial</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">País / Moeda</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">NUIT</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold">Âmbito Operacional</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Limites (Ativos / Users)</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Estado</th>
                      <th className="py-3 px-4 font-semibold text-right">Ações</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100">
                    {filteredTenants.length === 0 ? (
                      <tr>
                        <td colSpan={8} className="py-10 px-4 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">
                          Nenhuma empresa corresponde aos filtros aplicados.
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ) : filteredTenants.map((tenant) => {
                      const companyName = tenant.name;
                      const trade = tenant.trade_name || tenant.tradeName || '---';
                      const active = tenant.is_active ?? tenant.isActive ?? true;
                      const assetsLimit = tenant.max_assets_limit ?? tenant.maxAssetsLimit ?? 50;
                      const usersLimit = tenant.max_users_limit ?? tenant.maxUsersLimit ?? tenant.max_users ?? tenant.maxUsers ?? 15;
                      const scope = tenant.operational_scope || tenant.operationalScope || 'mixed';
                      const tenantCountry = tenant.country || 'Moçambique';
                      const tenantCurrency = tenant.currency || 'MZN';

                      return (
                        <tr key={tenant.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                            {companyName}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600 text-xs">
                            {trade}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-xs font-medium text-slate-800">
                            <span className="block">{tenantCountry}</span>
                            <span className="text-[10px] text-blue-700 font-mono font-bold">{tenantCurrency}</span>
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono text-xs text-slate-600">
                            {tenant.nuit}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-xs">
                            {scope === 'plant' ? (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
                                Instalação Fabril
                              </span>
                            ) : scope === 'fleet' ? (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
                                Frota
                              </span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200">
                                Operação Mista
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono text-xs text-right text-slate-700">
                            {assetsLimit} ativos / {usersLimit} users
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-center">
                            {active ? (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500"></span>
                                Ativo
                              </span>
                            ) : (
                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                                <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500"></span>
                                Suspenso
                              </span>
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                              <button
                                onClick={() => handleOpenEditModal(tenant)}
                                className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                title="Editar Parâmetros"
                              >
                                <Edit2 size={15} />
                              </button>
                              <button
                                onClick={() => handleToggleActiveStatus(tenant)}
                                className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                                title={active ? 'Suspender Licença' : 'Reativar Licença'}
                              >
                                {active ? <Pause size={15} /> : <Play size={15} />}
                              </button>
                            </div>
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                      );
                    })}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {/* CONTEÚDO DA ABA 2: PREÇOS DE COMBUSTÍVEL POR PAÍS / MOEDA */}
      {activeTab === 'fuel_pricing' && (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
          <div className="p-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
            <div>
              <span className="font-semibold text-sm text-slate-900 block">Tabela Oficial de Preços de Referência de Combustível</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5 block">
                Valores de referência por litro vigentes para cálculo de eficiência e custos de abastecimento no CMMS.
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse text-xs">
              <thead>
                <tr className="border-b border-slate-200 text-slate-600 uppercase font-semibold text-[10px] tracking-wider bg-slate-50">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">País</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Moeda Oficial</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Preço Diesel / Litro</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Preço Gasolina / Litro</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Âmbito da Regra</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-slate-800">
                {fuelPrices.map((fp) => (
                  <tr key={fp.id} className="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors">
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Globe size={15} className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" />
                      <span>{fp.country}</span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono font-bold text-blue-800">
                      {fp.currency}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      {formatMoney(fp.diesel_price, fp.currency)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      {formatMoney(fp.gasoline_price, fp.currency)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      {fp.scope_type === 'national' ? (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
                          Padrão Nacional
                        </span>
                      ) : (
                        <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200">
                          Exceção: {fp.tenant_name || 'Empresa Específica'}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </td>

                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleOpenFuelModal(fp)}
                        className="p-1.5 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900 hover:bg-slate-100 rounded transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        title="Editar Tabela de Preços"
                      >
                        <Edit2 size={15} />
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL 1: REGISTRO / EDIÇÃO DE EMPRESA (TENANT) */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-2xl rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]">
            
            {/* Header */}
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Building2 className="text-blue-700" size={20} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {modalMode === 'create' ? 'Registar Nova Empresa Contratante' : 'Editar Parâmetros da Empresa'}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            {/* Form */}
            <form onSubmit={handleSaveTenant} className="p-6 space-y-4 overflow-y-auto flex-1 text-xs">
              
              {/* Seção 1: Dados Institucionais */}
              <div className="space-y-3">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <Shield size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                  <span>1. Dados Institucionais e Contratuais</span>
                </h3>
                
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="tenantName" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Nome Oficial / Razão Social *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="tenantName"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={tenantName}
                      onChange={(e) => setTenantName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: Caminhos de Ferro, Lda"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="tradeName" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Nome Comercial / Fantasia *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="tradeName"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={tradeName}
                      onChange={(e) => setTradeName(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: CFM Logística"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="nuit" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      NUIT (Identificação Fiscal) *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="nuit"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={nuit}
                      onChange={(e) => setNuit(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: 400123456"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="province" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Província Sede / Cidade *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="province"
                      type="text"
                      required
                      value={province}
                      onChange={(e) => setProvince(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="ex: Maputo, Beira"
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* PAÍS E MOEDA COM AUTO-VINCULAÇÃO */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="country" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      País *
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="country"
                      required
                      value={country}
                      onChange={(e) => handleCountryChange(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      {COUNTRIES_CURRENCIES.map((c) => (
                        <option key={c.code} value={c.country}>
                          {c.country} ({c.currency})
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="currency" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Moeda Oficial *
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="currency"
                      required
                      value={currency}
                      onChange={(e) => setCurrency(e.target.value)}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer font-mono"
                    >
                      {ALL_CURRENCIES.map((cur) => (
                        <option key={cur.code} value={cur.code}>
                          {cur.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="operationalScope" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Âmbito Operacional *
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="operationalScope"
                      required
                      value={operationalScope}
                      onChange={(e) => setOperationalScope(e.target.value as 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed')}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <option value="fleet">Frota (Transportes/Logística)</option>
                      <option value="plant">Instalação Fabril (Indústria)</option>
                      <option value="mixed">Operação Mista (Frota + Fábrica)</option>
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="maxAssets" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Limite de Ativos *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="maxAssets"
                      type="number"
                      required
                      min={1}
                      value={maxAssetsLimit}
                      onChange={(e) => setMaxAssetsLimit(Number(e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="maxUsers" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                      Limite de Utilizadores *
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="maxUsers"
                      type="number"
                      required
                      min={1}
                      value={maxUsersLimit}
                      onChange={(e) => setMaxUsersLimit(Number(e.target.value))}
                      className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Seção 2: Administrador Inicial (Apenas no cadastro) */}
              {modalMode === 'create' && (
                <div className="border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                  <h3 className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <User size={14} className="text-blue-700" />
                    <span>2. Utilizador Administrador Inicial</span>
                  </h3>
                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminName" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Nome Completo *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adminName"
                        type="text"
                        required={modalMode === 'create'}
                        value={adminFullName}
                        onChange={(e) => setAdminFullName(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: João Sitoe"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminPhone" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Telefone / Celular
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adminPhone"
                        type="text"
                        value={adminPhone}
                        onChange={(e) => setAdminPhone(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: +258 84 123 4567"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-3">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminEmail" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        E-mail Corporativo *
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="adminEmail"
                        type="email"
                        required={modalMode === 'create'}
                        value={adminEmail}
                        onChange={(e) => setAdminEmail(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="ex: admin@empresa.co.mz"
                        className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="adminPass" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                        Palavra-passe de Acesso *
                      </label>
                      <div className="relative">
                        <input
                          id="adminPass"
                          type={showAdminPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                          required={modalMode === 'create'}
                          minLength={8}
                          value={adminPassword}
                          onChange={(e) => setAdminPassword(e.target.value)}
                          placeholder="Mínimo 8 caracteres"
                          className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-3 pr-10 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono focus:outline-none"
                        />
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => setShowAdminPassword(!showAdminPassword)}
                          className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          {showAdminPassword ? <EyeOff size={14} /> : <Eye size={14} />}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {/* Botões do Modal */}
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 mt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  {submitting && <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />}
                  <span>{modalMode === 'create' ? 'Provisionar Empresa' : 'Guardar Alterações'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL 2: TABELA DE PREÇOS DE COMBUSTÍVEL */}
      {isFuelModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 w-full max-w-lg rounded-xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            
            <div className="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-200 flex items-center justify-between bg-slate-50">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Fuel className="text-blue-700" size={20} />
                <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">
                  {editingFuelPriceId ? 'Editar Preço de Referência' : 'Definir Tabela de Preços de Combustível'}
                </h2>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsFuelModalOpen(false)}
                className="p-1 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSaveFuelPrice} className="p-6 space-y-4 text-xs">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  País *
                </label>
                <select
                  required
                  value={fpCountry}
                  onChange={(e) => handleFpCountryChange(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {COUNTRIES_CURRENCIES.map((c) => (
                    <option key={c.code} value={c.country}>
                      {c.country} ({c.currency})
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Moeda Oficial *
                </label>
                <select
                  required
                  value={fpCurrency}
                  onChange={(e) => setFpCurrency(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono cursor-pointer"
                >
                  {ALL_CURRENCIES.map((cur) => (
                    <option key={cur.code} value={cur.code}>
                      {cur.label}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                  Âmbito da Regra *
                </label>
                <select
                  value={fpScopeType}
                  onChange={(e) => setFpScopeType(e.target.value as any)}
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  <option value="national">Padrão Nacional (Para todas as empresas do país)</option>
                  <option value="company_exception">Exceção para Empresa Específica</option>
                </select>
              </div>

              {fpScopeType === 'company_exception' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Empresa Beneficiária da Exceção *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required={fpScopeType === 'company_exception'}
                    value={fpTenantId}
                    onChange={(e) => setFpTenantId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="">Selecione a empresa...</option>
                    {tenants.map((t) => (
                      <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                        {t.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4 pt-2 border-t border-slate-100">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Preço Diesel ({fpCurrency}/Litro) *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.01"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    value={fpDieselPrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setFpDieselPrice(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono"
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1">
                    Preço Gasolina ({fpCurrency}/Litro) *
                  </label>
                  <input
                    type="number"
                    step="0.01"
                    required
                    min={0}
                    value={fpGasolinePrice}
                    onChange={(e) => setFpGasolinePrice(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4 mt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsFuelModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-5 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-semibold transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none flex items-center gap-2"
                >
                  {submitting && <Loader2 size={14} className="animate-spin" />}
                  <span>Guardar Preço de Referência</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {isSuspendModalOpen && suspensionTenant && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md w-full max-w-md shadow-xl p-6 animate-slide-up">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-700 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Pause size={22} />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 text-center mb-2">Suspender Empresa</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 text-center leading-relaxed mb-5">
              Suspender Empresa: <strong className="text-slate-900">{suspensionTenant.name}</strong>. A empresa entrará em modo de bloqueio.
            </p>
            <form
              onSubmit={(e) => {
                e.preventDefault();
                handleSuspendTenant();
              }}
              className="space-y-4"
            >
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="suspensionReason" className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Justificativa da suspensão *
                </label>
                <textarea
                  id="suspensionReason"
                  required
                  value={suspensionReason}
                  onChange={(e) => setSuspensionReason(e.target.value)}
                  rows={4}
                  placeholder="Descreva o motivo da suspensão..."
                  className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none resize-y"
                />
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setIsSuspendModalOpen(false);
                    setSuspensionTenant(null);
                    setSuspensionReason('');
                    setSuspensionError(null);
                  }}
                  disabled={isSuspending}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-300 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  Cancelar
              {suspensionError && (
                <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-md p-2.5">
                  {suspensionError}
                </p>
              )}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={isSuspending || !suspensionReason.trim()}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                >
                  {isSuspending ? 'A suspender...' : 'Confirmar Suspensão'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default SuperAdminPage;


// ===== src/pages/Teams.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { Technician } from '../types/technician';
import {
  Users,
  Search,
  RefreshCw,
  Loader2,
  Wrench,
  Truck,
  Building2,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  CheckCircle2,
  Clock,
  Briefcase,
  AlertCircle
} from 'lucide-react';

export const TeamsPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [technicians, setTechnicians] = useState<Technician[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [specialtyFilter, setSpecialtyFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [scopeFilter, setScopeFilter] = useState<string>('all');

  const fetchTeam = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/technicians');
      const list = Array.isArray(res.data)
        ? res.data
        : (res.data?.technicians || res.data?.data || []);
      const uniqueTechs = Array.from(
        new Map<string, Technician>(list.map((t: Technician) => {
          const key = t.email?.toLowerCase() || t.name?.toLowerCase() || t.full_name?.toLowerCase() || t.id;
          return [key, t];
        })).values()
      );
      setTechnicians(uniqueTechs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar equipa técnica:', err);
      setTechnicians([]);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTeam();
  }, []);

  // Extrair lista de especialidades únicas para o filtro
  const availableSpecialties = Array.from(
    new Set(technicians.map(t => t.specialty).filter(Boolean))
  );

  // Filtragem
  const filteredTechnicians = technicians.filter((tech) => {
    const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const name = tech.name || tech.full_name || '';
    const email = tech.email || '';
    const phone = tech.phone || '';
    const spec = tech.specialty || '';

    const matchesSearch =
      !searchQuery ||
      name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      email.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      spec.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
      phone.includes(q);

    const matchesSpecialty =
      specialtyFilter === 'all' || tech.specialty === specialtyFilter;

    const matchesScope =
      scopeFilter === 'all' || tech.operational_scope === scopeFilter;

    return matchesSearch && matchesSpecialty && matchesScope;
  });

  // Métricas do Topo
  const totalTeam = technicians.length;
  const inServiceCount = technicians.filter(t => (t.active_orders_count || 0) > 0).length;
  const availableCount = technicians.filter(t => (t.active_orders_count || 0) === 0).length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      
      {/* CABEÇALHO DA PÁGINA (READ-ONLY CONSULTATION) */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <Users className="text-blue-700" size={24} />
            <span>Equipes Técnicas & Operacionais</span>
            {loading && <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin text-slate-400" />}
          </h1>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 leading-relaxed">
            Consulta e alocação em tempo real de colaboradores técnicos e operacionais da empresa.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
          <button
            onClick={fetchTeam}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-all cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium"
            title="Atualizar dados"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={15} className={loading ? 'animate-spin text-blue-700' : ''} />
            <span>Atualizar Equipes</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* CARDS DE MÉTRICAS REAIS NO TOPO */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Users size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Total da Equipa</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : totalTeam}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <Clock size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Técnicos em Serviço</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : `${inServiceCount} em serviço`}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex items-center gap-4 shadow-none">
          <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-md bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
            <CheckCircle2 size={20} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider block">Técnicos Disponíveis</span>
            <span className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 mt-0.5 block">{loading ? '---' : `${availableCount} disponíveis`}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* FILTROS E PESQUISA */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 space-y-3 shadow-none">
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          
          {/* Caixa de Busca */}
          <div className="relative">
            <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-slate-400" size={16} />
            <input
              type="text"
              placeholder="Pesquisar colaborador por nome, e-mail ou especialidade..."
              value={searchQuery}
              onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Filtro por Especialidade */}
          <div>
            <select
              value={specialtyFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setSpecialtyFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="all">Todas as Especialidades</option>
              {availableSpecialties.map((spec) => (
                <option key={spec} value={spec}>
                  {spec}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Filtro por Âmbito */}
          <div>
            <select
              value={scopeFilter}
              onChange={(e) => setScopeFilter(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 focus:bg-white cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="all">Todos os Âmbitos Operacionais</option>
              <option value="fleet">Frota</option>
              <option value="plant">Instalação Fabril</option>
              <option value="both">Ambos os Âmbitos</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LISTA / GRID DE COLABORADORES */}
      {loading ? (
        <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md">
          <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-blue-700 mx-auto" size={32} />
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-3">A carregar equipa técnica...</p>
        </div>
      ) : technicians.length === 0 ? (
        /* EMPTY STATE REAL */
        <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md">
          <Users className="text-slate-300 mx-auto" size={40} />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mt-2">Nenhum técnico cadastrado para esta empresa</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 max-w-md mx-auto">
            O Administrador pode cadastrar novos técnicos no menu Gestão de Utilizadores.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : filteredTechnicians.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="p-12 text-center bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md">
          <Search className="text-slate-300 mx-auto" size={36} />
          <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mt-2">Nenhum técnico encontrado para o filtro aplicado</h3>
          <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1">
            Tente redefinir os termos de pesquisa ou o filtro de especialidade.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
          {filteredTechnicians.map((tech) => {
            const techName = tech.name || tech.full_name || 'Técnico';
            const initials = techName
              .split(' ')
              .map(n => n[0])
              .join('')
              .substring(0, 2)
              .toUpperCase();

            const activeOrders = tech.active_orders_count || 0;

            return (
              <div
                key={tech.id}
                className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-5 flex flex-col justify-between hover:border-blue-300 transition-colors shadow-none"
              >
                <div>
                  {/* Topo do Cartão: Avatar + Nome + Especialidade */}
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-3 border-b border-slate-100 pb-3">
                    <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-sm shrink-0 uppercase">
                      {initials}
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">
                        {techName}
                      </h3>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 font-medium truncate mt-0.5">
                        Técnico de Manutenção
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Detalhes: Especialidade + Âmbito + Contactos */}
                  <div className="py-3 space-y-2 text-xs">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-slate-500">Especialidade:</span>
                      <span className="font-semibold text-slate-800 bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[11px]">
                        {tech.specialty || 'Geral'}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <span className="text-slate-500">Âmbito:</span>
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-medium text-slate-700">
                        {tech.operational_scope === 'plant' ? (
                          <>
                            <Building2 size={13} className="text-slate-500" />
                            <span>Instalação Fabril</span>
                          </>
                        ) : (
                          <>
                            <Truck size={13} className="text-slate-500" />
                            <span>Frota</span>
                          </>
                        )}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {tech.email && (
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between pt-1">
                        <span className="text-slate-500">E-mail:</span>
                        <span className="text-slate-800 font-mono text-[11px] truncate max-w-[170px]" title={tech.email}>
                          {tech.email}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}

                    {tech.phone && (
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                        <span className="text-slate-500">Contacto:</span>
                        <span className="text-slate-800 font-mono text-[11px]">
                          {tech.phone}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Rodapé do Cartão: Estado Operacional */}
                <div className="pt-3 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="text-slate-500 text-[11px]">Estado Atual:</span>
                  {activeOrders > 0 ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                      <Clock size={12} />
                      <span>Em Serviço ({activeOrders} {activeOrders === 1 ? 'ordem' : 'ordens'})</span>
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold">
                      <CheckCircle2 size={12} />
                      <span>Disponível</span>
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default TeamsPage;


// ===== src/pages/Users.tsx =====
import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { api } from '../services/api';
import { useAuth, useSuspensionGuard } from '../contexts/AuthContext';
import { useCurrentDomain } from '../config/domainRegistry';
import { useEscapeKey } from '../hooks/useEscapeKey';
import {
  Plus,
  Users as UsersIcon,
  Search,
  X,
  RefreshCw,
  Edit2,
  Shield,
  Briefcase,
  AlertTriangle,
  CheckCircle2,
  Lock,
  Unlock,
  Mail,
  User as UserIcon,
  Layers,
  Building2,
  ArrowLeft
} from 'lucide-react';

interface TenantUser {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  fullName_?: string;
  email: string;
  role: 'tenant_admin' | 'supervisor' | 'technician' | 'driver_operator';
  operational_scope: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
  operationalScope?: 'fleet' | 'plant' | 'both' | 'mixed';
  specialty?: string;
  created_at?: string;
  is_active: boolean;
  is_suspended?: boolean;
}

interface TenantItem {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  trade_name?: string;
  tradeName?: string;
  max_users_limit?: number;
  max_users?: number;
  maxUsers?: number;
  operational_scope?: string;
}

export const UsersPage: React.FC = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { user, isSuperAdmin } = useAuth();
  const { checkSuspension, handleActionClick } = useSuspensionGuard();
  const { technicianSpecialties } = useCurrentDomain();
  const currentUserScope = user?.operational_scope || user?.operationalScope || user?.tenant?.operational_scope || user?.tenant?.operationalScope;
  const canSelectAllScopes = isSuperAdmin || currentUserScope === 'mixed' || currentUserScope === 'both';
  const availableOperationalScopes: Array<'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed'> = canSelectAllScopes
    ? ['fleet', 'plant', 'mixed']
    : currentUserScope === 'plant'
      ? ['plant']
      : ['fleet'];

  const [tenants, setTenants] = useState<TenantItem[]>([]);
  const [selectedTenantId, setSelectedTenantId] = useState<string>('');
  const [loadingTenants, setLoadingTenants] = useState<boolean>(true);

  const [users, setUsers] = useState<TenantUser[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [roleFilter, setRoleFilter] = useState('all');
  const [scopeFilter, setScopeFilter] = useState('all');

  // Modais
  const [isModalOpen, setIsModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [modalMode, setModalMode] = useState<'create' | 'edit'>('create');
  const [editingUserId, setEditingUserId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isSuspendModalOpen, setIsSuspendModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [suspendingUser, setSuspendingUser] = useState<TenantUser | null>(null);
  const [suspensionReason, setSuspensionReason] = useState('');
  const [isSuspending, setIsSuspending] = useState(false);

  useEscapeKey(() => setIsModalOpen(false), isModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsSuspendModalOpen(false), isSuspendModalOpen);

  // Campos do Formulário
  const [modalTenantId, setModalTenantId] = useState<string>('');
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState<'tenant_admin' | 'supervisor' | 'technician' | 'driver_operator'>('technician');
  const [operationalScope, setOperationalScope] = useState<'fleet' | 'plant' | 'mixed'>('fleet');
  const [specialty, setSpecialty] = useState('');

  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [successMessage, setSuccessMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Tenant atualmente selecionado para cálculo de licenças
  const selectedTenant = tenants.find((t) => t.id === (selectedTenantId || modalTenantId));
  const maxUsersLimit = selectedTenant?.max_users_limit || selectedTenant?.max_users || selectedTenant?.maxUsers || 15;

  // Carrega lista de empresas/tenants (Super Admin)
  const fetchTenants = async () => {
    setLoadingTenants(true);
    try {
      let data = [];
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      } catch {
        const response = await api.get('/super-admin/tenants');
        data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.tenants || response.data?.data || [];
      }
      setTenants(data);
      if (data.length > 0 && !selectedTenantId) {
        setSelectedTenantId(data[0].id);
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar lista de empresas:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingTenants(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega utilizadores filtrados por empresa
  const fetchUsers = async (tenantIdToFetch?: string) => {
    const targetTenantId = tenantIdToFetch !== undefined ? tenantIdToFetch : selectedTenantId;
    if (!targetTenantId) {
      setUsers([]);
      setLoading(false);
      return;
    }

    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/users?tenant_id=${targetTenantId}`);
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.users || response.data?.data || [];

      // Normaliza utilizadores sem remover contas suspensas da lista.
      const normalizedUsers = data.map((u: any) => ({
        id: u.id,
        fullName: u.fullName || u.fullName_ || u.full_name || u.name || 'Utilizador Técnico',
        email: u.email,
        role: u.role,
        operational_scope: u.operational_scope || u.operationalScope || 'fleet',
        specialty: u.specialty || u.technical_specialty || u.specialization || '',
        is_active: u.is_active ?? u.isActive ?? !(u.is_suspended ?? u.isSuspended ?? u.status === 'suspended'),
        is_suspended: u.is_suspended ?? u.isSuspended ?? u.status === 'suspended'
      }));

      setUsers(normalizedUsers);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar utilizadores:', err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        err.message ||
        'Não foi possível ligar ao servidor para carregar a lista de utilizadores da empresa selecionada.'
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchTenants();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedTenantId) {
      fetchUsers(selectedTenantId);
    } else {
      setUsers([]);
    }
  }, [selectedTenantId]);

  if (!isSuperAdmin && user?.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return (
      <div className="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-8 text-center max-w-md mx-auto my-12 shadow-2xl">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-red-950/40 text-red-500 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 border border-red-800/30">
          <Shield size={24} />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-md font-bold text-slate-100">Acesso Restrito ao Super Admin</h2>
        <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-2 leading-relaxed">
          Apenas Administradores Globais (Super Admin) têm permissão para gerir os utilizadores de empresas e contratos.
        </p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
    if (!selectedTenantId && tenants.length > 0) {
      setModalTenantId(tenants[0].id);
    } else {
      setModalTenantId(selectedTenantId);
    }
    setModalMode('create');
    setEditingUserId(null);
    setFullName('');
    setEmail('');
    setPassword('');
    setRole('technician');
    setOperationalScope(currentUserScope === 'plant' ? 'plant' : currentUserScope === 'mixed' || currentUserScope === 'both' ? 'mixed' : 'fleet');
    setSpecialty(technicianSpecialties.length > 0 ? technicianSpecialties[0].value : '');
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleOpenEditModal = (targetUser: TenantUser) => {
    const targetScope = targetUser.operational_scope === 'plant'
      ? 'plant'
      : targetUser.operational_scope === 'mixed' || targetUser.operational_scope === 'both'
        ? 'mixed'
        : 'fleet';
    setModalMode('edit');
    setEditingUserId(targetUser.id);
    setFullName(targetUser.fullName);
    setEmail(targetUser.email);
    setPassword('');
    setRole(targetUser.role);
    setOperationalScope(targetScope);
    setSpecialty(targetUser.specialty || (technicianSpecialties.length > 0 ? technicianSpecialties[0].value : ''));
    setIsModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!fullName.trim() || !email.trim()) {
      alert('Por favor, preencha os campos obrigatórios (*).');
      return;
    }

    const activeTenantId = selectedTenantId || modalTenantId;
    if (modalMode === 'create' && !activeTenantId) {
      alert('Por favor, selecione uma empresa (tenant) antes de criar o utilizador.');
      return;
    }

    if (modalMode === 'create' && !password.trim()) {
      alert('A palavra-passe é obrigatória para novos utilizadores.');
      return;
    }
    if (modalMode === 'create' && !canSelectAllScopes && (currentUserScope === 'fleet' || currentUserScope === 'plant') && operationalScope !== currentUserScope) {
      alert('Não pode criar utilizadores fora do seu âmbito operacional.');
      return;
    }
    if (role === 'technician' && !specialty) {
      alert('Por favor, selecione a especialidade técnica.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (modalMode === 'create') {
        if (users.length >= maxUsersLimit) {
          alert(`Limite de utilizadores atingido para esta empresa (${maxUsersLimit}). Não é possível criar mais contas.`);
          setSubmitting(false);
          return;
        }

        const createPayload = {
          tenant_id: activeTenantId,
          tenantId: activeTenantId,
          full_name: fullName.trim(),
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          password: password,
          role: role,
          operational_scope: operationalScope,
          operationalScope: operationalScope,
          ...(role === 'technician' && {
            specialty: specialty,
            technical_specialty: specialty,
            specialization: specialty
          })
        };

        await api.post('/users', createPayload);
        setSuccessMessage('Utilizador criado com sucesso!');
      } else {
        const editPayload = {
          name: fullName.trim(),
          full_name: fullName.trim(),
          fullName: fullName.trim(),
          email: email.trim().toLowerCase(),
          role: role,
          operational_scope: operationalScope,
          operationalScope: operationalScope,
          ...(role === 'technician' && {
            specialty: specialty,
            technical_specialty: specialty,
            specialization: specialty
          }),
          ...(password.trim() ? { password } : {})
        };

        await api.put(`/users/${editingUserId}`, editPayload);
        setSuccessMessage('Utilizador atualizado com sucesso!');
      }

      setIsModalOpen(false);
      await fetchUsers(activeTenantId);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
