    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao salvar utilizador:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.response?.data?.error || err.message || 'Erro ao gravar utilizador.');
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmSuspend = (targetUser: TenantUser) => {
    setSuspendingUser(targetUser);
    setSuspensionReason('');
    setIsSuspendModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSuspendUser = async () => {
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!suspendingUser) return;
    if (!suspensionReason.trim()) {
      alert('Indique uma justificativa para suspender este utilizador.');
      return;
    }

    const targetUserId = suspendingUser.id;
    setIsSuspending(true);
    try {
      await api.patch('/users/' + targetUserId + '/suspend', { reason: suspensionReason.trim() });
      setUsers((prev) => prev.map((user) => user.id === targetUserId
        ? { ...user, is_active: false, is_suspended: true }
        : user));
      setSuccessMessage('Utilizador suspenso com sucesso.');
      setIsSuspendModalOpen(false);
      setSuspendingUser(null);
      setSuspensionReason('');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao suspender utilizador:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao suspender utilizador.');
    } finally {
      setIsSuspending(false);
    }
  };

  const handleReactivateUser = async (targetUser: TenantUser) => {
    if (checkSuspension()) return;

    try {
      await api.patch(`/users/${targetUser.id}/reactivate`);
      setUsers((previous) => previous.map((user) => user.id === targetUser.id
        ? { ...user, is_active: true, is_suspended: false }
        : user));
      setSuccessMessage('Utilizador reativado com sucesso.');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao reativar utilizador:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao reativar utilizador.');
    }
  };

  // Filtragem local de busca e tabela
  const filteredUsers = users.filter((u) => {
    const query = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
    const matchesSearch = (
      u.fullName.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      u.email.toLowerCase().includes(query) ||
      u.role.toLowerCase().includes(query)
    );
    const matchesRole = roleFilter === 'all' || u.role === roleFilter;
    const matchesScope = scopeFilter === 'all' || u.operational_scope === scopeFilter;
    return matchesSearch && matchesRole && matchesScope;
  });

  const getRoleBadgeColor = (userRole: string) => {
    switch (userRole) {
      case 'tenant_admin':
        return 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200';
      case 'supervisor':
        return 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200';
      case 'technician':
        return 'bg-cyan-50 text-cyan-700 border border-cyan-200';
      case 'driver_operator':
        return 'bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200';
    }
  };

  const formatRoleName = (userRole: string) => {
    switch (userRole) {
      case 'tenant_admin':
        return 'Gestor de Contrato (Admin)';
      case 'supervisor':
        return 'Supervisor Operacional';
      case 'technician':
        return 'Técnico de Manutenção';
      case 'driver_operator':
        return 'Motorista / Operador';
      default:
        return userRole;
    }
  };

  const formatScopeName = (scope: string) => {
    switch (scope) {
      case 'fleet':
        return 'Frota';
      case 'plant':
        return 'Instalação Fabril';
      case 'both':
      case 'mixed':
        return 'Frota e Instalação Fabril';
      default:
        return 'Frota';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* CABEÇALHO */}
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center md:justify-between gap-4">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button
            onClick={() => navigate(-1)}
            className="p-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer"
            title="Voltar"
          >
            <ArrowLeft size={18} />
          </button>
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-xl md:text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2.5">
              <UsersIcon className="text-blue-700" size={24} />
              Gestão de Utilizadores & Técnicos
            </h1>
            <p className="text-slate-500 text-xs mt-1 leading-relaxed">
              Selecione uma empresa para gerir as contas e atribuir níveis de acesso de supervisores e técnicos.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <button
            onClick={() => selectedTenantId && fetchUsers(selectedTenantId)}
            disabled={loading}
            className="p-2.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border border-slate-300 text-slate-700 rounded-md transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold"
            title="Atualizar lista"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={16} className={loading ? "animate-spin" : ""} />
            <span>Atualizar</span>
          </button>
          <button
            onClick={() => handleActionClick(handleOpenCreateModal)}
            className="bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white text-xs font-medium py-2.5 px-4 rounded-md flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-colors cursor-pointer shadow-none"
          >
            <Plus size={16} />
            <span>Registar Novo Utilizador / Técnico</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SELETOR DE EMPRESA (TENANT) NO TOPO */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4 shadow-none">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 w-full md:w-auto">
          <Building2 className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" size={20} />
          <label htmlFor="tenant-select" className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 whitespace-nowrap">
            Empresa:
          </label>
          {loadingTenants ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
              <RefreshCw className="animate-spin" size={14} />
              <span>A carregar empresas...</span>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <select
              id="tenant-select"
              value={selectedTenantId}
              onChange={(e) => setSelectedTenantId(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full md:w-72 bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
            >
              <option value="">[ Selecione uma Empresa ▾ ]</option>
              {tenants.map((t) => (
                <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                  {t.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* INDICADOR DE UTILIZADORES USADOS DO TENANT SELECIONADO */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 self-start md:self-auto bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-md">
          <Layers className="text-blue-700 shrink-0" size={16} />
          <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700">Licenças do Contrato:</span>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 bg-white px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded border border-slate-200">
            {selectedTenantId ? `${users.length} / ${maxUsersLimit} Utilizadores Utilizados` : 'Selecione uma Empresa'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* FEEDBACK DE SUCESSO */}
      {successMessage && (
        <div className="p-4 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-200 text-emerald-800 text-xs rounded-md flex gap-3 items-center animate-fade-in shadow-none">
          <CheckCircle2 size={18} className="text-emerald-700 shrink-0" />
          <span className="font-semibold">{successMessage}</span>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* FILTROS E BUSCA */}
      <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-4 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 items-center justify-between shadow-none">
        <div className="relative w-full md:max-w-md">
          <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3.5 pointer-events-none text-slate-400">
            <Search size={16} />
          </span>
          <input
            type="text"
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            placeholder={selectedTenantId ? "Pesquisar por nome, email ou nível..." : "Selecione uma empresa primeiro..."}
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-10 pr-4 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors font-mono disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-2.5 w-full md:w-auto">
          <select
            value={roleFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setRoleFilter(e.target.value)}
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            aria-label="Filtrar por cargo"
            className="w-full sm:w-48 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 cursor-pointer disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os cargos</option>
            <option value="tenant_admin">Gestor de Contrato</option>
            <option value="supervisor">Supervisor</option>
            <option value="technician">Técnico</option>
            <option value="driver_operator">Motorista / Operador</option>
          </select>
          <select
            value={scopeFilter}
            onChange={(e) => setScopeFilter(e.target.value)}
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            aria-label="Filtrar por âmbito operacional"
            className="w-full sm:w-44 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none focus:border-blue-600 cursor-pointer disabled:bg-slate-100 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <option value="all">Todos os âmbitos</option>
            <option value="fleet">Frota</option>
            <option value="plant">Instalação Fabril</option>
            <option value="both">Frota e Fábrica</option>
            <option value="mixed">Misto</option>
          </select>
          <button
            onClick={() => fetchUsers(selectedTenantId)}
            disabled={!selectedTenantId}
            className="p-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 hover:text-slate-900 rounded-md border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            title="Recarregar Lista"
          >
            <RefreshCw size={14} className={loading ? 'animate-spin' : ''} />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* LISTA DE UTILIZADORES */}
      {!selectedTenantId ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center text-slate-500 shadow-none">
          <Building2 className="mx-auto text-slate-300 mb-3" size={40} />
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-800">Selecione uma Empresa</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 max-w-md mx-auto">
            Por favor, escolha uma empresa no seletor acima para visualizar, editar e criar contas de utilizadores.
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : loading ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center shadow-none">
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3">
            <RefreshCw className="text-blue-700 animate-spin" size={28} />
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-medium">A carregar utilizadores da empresa...</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : error ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-8 text-center text-red-700 flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          <AlertTriangle size={32} />
          <p className="text-xs">{error}</p>
          <button onClick={() => fetchUsers(selectedTenantId)} className="bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 text-xs px-4 py-2 rounded-md border border-slate-300 cursor-pointer">
            Tentar Novamente
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : filteredUsers.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md p-12 text-center text-slate-500">
          <UsersIcon className="mx-auto text-slate-400 mb-3" size={36} />
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">Nenhum utilizador encontrado para esta empresa.</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1">Utilize "Registar Novo Utilizador / Técnico" para adicionar o primeiro utilizador a este contrato.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md overflow-hidden shadow-none">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left border-collapse">
              <thead>
                <tr className="bg-slate-50 border-b border-slate-200 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-600 uppercase tracking-wider">
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Nome Completo</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">E-mail</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Nível de Acesso (Cargo)</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Escopo Operacional</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4">Estado</th>
                  <th className="py-3 px-4 text-right">Ações</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-slate-100 text-xs">
                {filteredUsers.map((u) => (
                  <tr key={u.id} className={`hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition-colors ${u.is_active ? '' : 'opacity-60'}`}>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-slate-900">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                      <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded-md bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 font-bold flex items-center justify-center text-xs border border-blue-200">
                        {u.fullName.substring(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
                      </div>
                      {u.fullName}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 font-mono text-slate-600">{u.email}</td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center w-fit px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium ${getRoleBadgeColor(u.role)}`}>
                          {formatRoleName(u.role)}
                        </span>
                        {u.role === 'technician' && u.specialty && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
                            {u.specialty}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-slate-600">{formatScopeName(u.operational_scope)}</td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4">
                      <span className={`inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold border ${
                        u.is_active
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200'
                          : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border-slate-300'
                      }`}>
                        {u.is_active ? 'Ativo' : 'Suspenso'}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="py-3.5 px-4 text-right space-x-2 whitespace-nowrap">
                      <button
                        onClick={() => handleActionClick(() => handleOpenEditModal(u))}
                        className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-800 rounded-md border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                        title="Editar Utilizador"
                      >
                        <Edit2 size={13} />
                      </button>
                      {u.is_active ? (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleActionClick(() => handleConfirmSuspend(u))}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 hover:text-amber-700 rounded-md border border-slate-300 hover:border-amber-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          title="Suspender Utilizador"
                        >
                          <Lock size={13} />
                        </button>
                      ) : (
                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleActionClick(() => handleReactivateUser(u))}
                          className="p-1.5 bg-white hover:bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 hover:text-emerald-700 rounded-md border border-slate-300 hover:border-emerald-200 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                          title="Reativar Utilizador"
                        >
                          <Unlock size={13} />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* MODAL DE CRIAÇÃO / EDIÇÃO */}
      {isModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md w-full max-w-lg shadow-xl relative overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
            
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-5 border-b border-slate-200 bg-white">
              <h2 className="text-md font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                <UsersIcon className="text-blue-700" size={18} />
                {modalMode === 'create' ? 'Novo Utilizador do Contrato' : 'Editar Informações do Utilizador'}
              </h2>
              <button onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)} className="text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-700 p-1 rounded-md hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer">
                <X size={18} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="p-6 space-y-4">
              {/* Seleção de Empresa no Modal se não selecionado */}
              {modalMode === 'create' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Empresa (Tenant) *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={modalTenantId || selectedTenantId}
                    onChange={(e) => setModalTenantId(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="">Selecione a empresa...</option>
                    {tenants.map((t) => (
                      <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>
                        {t.name}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Nome Completo *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3 text-slate-400">
                    <UserIcon size={14} />
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="text"
                    required
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ex: João Macamo"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  E-mail Corporativo *
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3 text-slate-400">
                    <Mail size={14} />
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="email"
                    required
                    value={email}
                    onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="ex: joao.macamo@empresa.co.mz"
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                  Palavra-passe {modalMode === 'edit' && '(deixe em branco para manter)'}
                </label>
                <div className="relative">
                  <span className="absolute inset-y-0 left-0 flex items-center pl-3 text-slate-400">
                    <Lock size={14} />
                  </span>
                  <input
                    type="password"
                    required={modalMode === 'create'}
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder={modalMode === 'create' ? 'Mínimo 6 caracteres' : '••••••••'}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md pl-9 pr-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none transition-colors font-mono"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Nível de Acesso (Cargo) *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    value={role}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const newRole = e.target.value as any;
                      setRole(newRole);
                      if (newRole === 'technician' && !specialty && technicianSpecialties.length > 0) {
                        setSpecialty(technicianSpecialties[0].value);
                      }
                    }}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="tenant_admin">Gestor de Contrato (Admin)</option>
                    <option value="supervisor">Supervisor Operacional</option>
                    <option value="technician">Técnico de Manutenção</option>
                    <option value="driver_operator">Motorista / Operador</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Âmbito Operacional *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={operationalScope}
                    onChange={(e) => setOperationalScope(e.target.value as any)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {availableOperationalScopes.includes('fleet') && <option value="fleet">Frota</option>}
                    {availableOperationalScopes.includes('plant') && <option value="plant">Instalação Fabril</option>}
                    {availableOperationalScopes.includes('mixed') && <option value="mixed">Mista</option>}
                  </select>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* CAMPO CONDICIONAL PARA TÉCNICO DE MANUTENÇÃO */}
              {role === 'technician' && (
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                    Especialidade Técnica *
                  </label>
                  <select
                    required
                    value={specialty}
                    onChange={(e) => setSpecialty(e.target.value)}
                    className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <option value="">Selecione a especialidade...</option>
                    {technicianSpecialties.map((spec) => (
                      <option key={spec.value} value={spec.value}>
                        {spec.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-4 border-t border-slate-200">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setIsModalOpen(false)}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors border border-slate-300 cursor-pointer"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submitting}
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-blue-700 hover:bg-blue-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors shadow-none flex items-center gap-1.5 cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {submitting ? (
                    <>
                      <RefreshCw className="animate-spin" size={13} />
                      <span>A gravar...</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <span>Guardar Alterações</span>
                  )}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* CONFIRMAÇÃO DE SUSPENSÃO */}
      {isSuspendModalOpen && suspendingUser && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/40 backdrop-blur-xs flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-md w-full max-w-md shadow-xl p-6 animate-slide-up">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 text-amber-700 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4">
              <Lock size={22} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 mb-2 text-center">Suspender Utilizador?</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed mb-4 text-center">
              A conta de <strong className="text-slate-900">{suspendingUser.fullName}</strong> perderá acesso até ser reativada.
            </p>
            <label className="block text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 mb-1.5" htmlFor="suspension-reason">
              Justificativa da suspensão *
            </label>
            <textarea
              id="suspension-reason"
              required
              value={suspensionReason}
              onChange={(e) => setSuspensionReason(e.target.value)}
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Indique o motivo da suspensão..."
              className="w-full bg-white border border-slate-300 focus:border-blue-600 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-blue-600 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-900 focus:outline-none resize-y"
            />
            <div className="flex gap-3 justify-end mt-5">
              <button
                onClick={() => setIsSuspendModalOpen(false)}
                disabled={isSuspending}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 text-slate-700 rounded-md text-xs font-medium border border-slate-300 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                onClick={handleSuspendUser}
                disabled={isSuspending || !suspensionReason.trim()}
                className="px-4 py-2 bg-amber-700 hover:bg-amber-800 text-white rounded-md text-xs font-medium transition-colors cursor-pointer disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              >
                {isSuspending ? 'A suspender...' : 'Suspender Utilizador'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default UsersPage;

// ==============================================
// SINCRONIZAÇÃO COM OS ENUMS DO BACKEND
// ==============================================
// POST/PUT /api/work-orders valida:
//  - orderType em { corrective, preventive, inspection }
//  - priority em { low, medium, high, urgent }
// A triagem de notificações pode trazer textos livres (ex.: "Preventiva",
// "Alta") que originariam 400 "Dados obrigatórios da ordem de serviço inválidos".
const VALID_ORDER_TYPES = new Set(['corrective', 'preventive', 'inspection']);
const VALID_PRIORITIES = new Set(['low', 'medium', 'high', 'urgent']);

const toValidOrderType = (value?: string | null): 'corrective' | 'preventive' | 'inspection' => {
  const normalized = String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (VALID_ORDER_TYPES.has(normalized)) {
    return normalized as 'corrective' | 'preventive' | 'inspection';
  }
  if (normalized.includes('prevent')) return 'preventive';
  if (normalized.includes('insp')) return 'inspection';
  return 'corrective';
};

const toValidPriority = (value?: string | null): 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent' => {
  const normalized = String(value ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
  if (VALID_PRIORITIES.has(normalized)) return normalized as 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'urgent';
  if (['baixa', 'baixo'].includes(normalized)) return 'low';
  if (['alta', 'alto'].includes(normalized)) return 'high';
  if (['urgente', 'crítica', 'critica', 'critical'].includes(normalized)) return 'urgent';
  return 'medium';
};

    });

    setSelectedPartsList([]);

  };

  // Estados de Notificações
  const [isBellOpen, setIsBellOpen] = useState(false);
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<any[]>([]);

  // Toasts / Feedback

  // Modais de Controle
  const [isFormModalOpen, setIsFormModalOpen] = useState(false);

  const [editingOrderId, setEditingEditingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Modal Novo Técnico
  const [isTechModalOpen, setIsTechModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [techName, setTechName] = useState('');

  const handlePrintWorkOrder = (order: WorkOrder, parts?: any[]) => {
    const printWindow = window.open('', '_blank');
    if (!printWindow) {
      alert('Não foi possível abrir a janela de impressão. Verifique se o navegador bloqueou pop-ups.');
      return;
    }

    const escapeHtml = (value: unknown) => String(value ?? '—').replace(/[&<>"']/g, (character) => ({
      '&': '&amp;',
      '<': '&lt;',
      '>': '&gt;',
      '"': '&quot;',
      "'": '&#39;'
    }[character] || character));
    const assetId = order.asset_id || order.assetId || '';
    const asset = assets.find((item) => item.id === assetId);
    const technicianId = order.technician_id || order.technicianId || order.assigned_to || order.assignedTo || '';
    const technician = technicians.find((item) => item.id === technicianId);
    const technicianName = (order as any).assigned_technician_name || order.technician_name ||
      technician?.name || technician?.full_name || '—';
    const printableParts = parts ?? (Array.isArray((order as any).parts) ? (order as any).parts : usedParts);
    const partRows = printableParts.length > 0
      ? printableParts.map((part: any) => `
          <tr>
            <td>${escapeHtml(part.oem_reference || part.part_number || part.partNumber || '—')}</td>
            <td>${escapeHtml(part.name || part.description || '—')}</td>
            <td class="center">${escapeHtml(part.quantity ?? '—')}</td>
          </tr>`).join('')
      : '<tr><td colspan="3" class="center muted">Nenhuma peça requisitada para esta Ordem de Serviço.</td></tr>';
    const openedAt = order.opened_at || order.openedAt || new Date().toISOString();
    const description = escapeHtml(order.description || '—').replace(/\r?\n/g, '<br>');
    const html = `<!doctype html>
      <html lang="pt">
        <head>
          <meta charset="utf-8" />
          <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1" />
          <title>Ordem de Serviço - AVEXAS CMMS</title>
          <style>
            @page { size: A4; margin: 15mm; }
            * { box-sizing: border-box; }
            body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; color: #1e293b; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.4; margin: 0; }
            .print-container { width: 100%; max-width: 190mm; margin: 0 auto; }
            .header { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; border-bottom: 2px solid #0f172a; padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 14px; }
            .brand { font-size: 18px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: 1px; color: #0f172a; }
            .brand-subtitle { display: block; color: #64748b; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 500; letter-spacing: 0; margin-top: 2px; }
            h1 { font-size: 19px; text-align: right; margin: 0 0 4px; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .order-number { text-align: right; color: #1d4ed8; font-size: 13px; font-weight: 700; }
            .block-title { background: #f1f5f9; font-weight: bold; padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; margin-top: 10px; text-transform: uppercase; font-size: 10px; }
            table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 10px; }
            th, td { border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding: 6px 8px; text-align: left; vertical-align: top; }
            th { background: #f8fafc; text-transform: uppercase; font-size: 9px; }
            .details td { width: 50%; }
            .label { color: #64748b; display: block; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 3px; text-transform: uppercase; }
            .value { color: #0f172a; font-weight: 600; overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
            .description { border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; min-height: 62px; padding: 9px; white-space: normal; }
            .center { text-align: center; }
            .muted { color: #64748b; }
            .signatures { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 28px; margin-top: 28px; }
            .signature { border-top: 1px solid #64748b; padding-top: 6px; text-align: center; font-size: 10px; }
            .footer { border-top: 1px solid #cbd5e1; color: #64748b; font-size: 9px; margin-top: 18px; padding-top: 6px; text-align: center; }
            tr, .block-title, .description, .signatures { break-inside: avoid; }
          </style>
        </head>
        <body>
          <main class="print-container">
            <header class="header">
              <div><div class="brand">AVEXAS<span class="brand-subtitle">CMMS · Gestão de Manutenção</span></div></div>
              <div>
                <h1>Ordem de Serviço</h1>
                <div class="order-number">OS N.º ${escapeHtml(order.wo_number || order.woNumber || order.id)}</div>
              </div>
            </header>

            <div class="block-title">1. Equipamento / Ativo</div>
            <table class="details"><tbody>
              <tr><td><span class="label">Código / Tag</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(order.asset?.code_tag || asset?.code_tag || asset?.codeTag || '—')}</span></td><td><span class="label">Descrição do ativo</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(order.asset_name || order.asset?.name || asset?.name || '—')}</span></td></tr>
              <tr><td><span class="label">Marca / Modelo</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(`${(asset as any)?.brand || ''} ${(asset as any)?.model || ''}`.trim() || '—')}</span></td><td><span class="label">Contador no atendimento</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(order.meter_at_service ?? order.meterAtService ?? '—')} ${escapeHtml(counterLabel)}</span></td></tr>
            </tbody></table>

            <div class="block-title">2. Dados da Ordem</div>
            <table class="details"><tbody>
              <tr><td><span class="label">Data de abertura</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(new Date(openedAt).toLocaleString('pt-MZ'))}</span></td><td><span class="label">Tipo de manutenção</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(order.order_type || order.orderType || 'corrective')}</span></td></tr>
              <tr><td><span class="label">Prioridade</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(order.priority || '—')}</span></td><td><span class="label">Estado</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(order.status || 'open')}</span></td></tr>
            </tbody></table>

            <div class="block-title">3. Descrição Técnica / Avaria</div>
            <div class="description"><strong>${escapeHtml(order.title || 'Ordem de Serviço')}</strong><br /><br />${description}</div>

            <div class="block-title">4. Técnico Responsável</div>
            <table><tbody><tr><td><span class="label">Técnico atribuído</span><span class="value">${escapeHtml(technicianName)}</span></td></tr></tbody></table>

            <div class="block-title">5. Peças e Materiais</div>
            <table>
              <thead><tr><th>Código / Referência</th><th>Descrição</th><th class="center">Quantidade</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>${partRows}</tbody>
            </table>

            <div class="block-title">6. Execução e Aprovação</div>
            <div class="signatures">
              <div class="signature">Técnico responsável</div>
              <div class="signature">Supervisor / Cliente</div>
            </div>
            <footer class="footer">Documento gerado pelo AVEXAS CMMS · ${escapeHtml(new Date().toLocaleString('pt-MZ'))}</footer>
          </main>
          <script>
            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
              window.focus();
              window.print();
            });
            window.addEventListener('afterprint', function () { window.close(); });
          </script>
        </body>
      </html>`;

    printWindow.document.open();
    printWindow.document.write(html);
    printWindow.document.close();
  };

  const openPrintModal = async (order: WorkOrder) => {
    setPrintOrder(order);
    try {
      const tagOrNumber = order.wo_number || order.woNumber || order.id;
      const url = await QRCode.toDataURL(tagOrNumber, { margin: 1, width: 120 });
      setPrintQrDataUrl(url);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao gerar QR code:', err);
    }
    setIsPrintModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Modal de Cancelamento de OS
  const [isCancelModalOpen, setIsCancelModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [cancelReason, setCancelReason] = useState('');

  // Ativação da tecla ESC em todos os modais
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsFormModalOpen(false), isFormModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsPrintModalOpen(false), isPrintModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsCancelModalOpen(false), isCancelModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsPauseModalOpen(false), isPauseModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsCloseModalOpen(false), isCloseModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsTechModalOpen(false), isTechModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsAddPartModalOpen(false), isAddPartModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => setIsNotificationModalOpen(false), isNotificationModalOpen);
  useEscapeKey(() => { setIsDrawerOpen(false); setSelectedDrawerOrder(null); }, isDrawerOpen);
  const [submittingCancel, setSubmittingCancel] = useState(false);

  // Submeter Cancelamento de OS
  const handleCancelOrderSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!selectedDrawerOrder || !cancelReason.trim()) {
      alert('Por favor, informe a justificativa do cancelamento.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingCancel(true);
    try {
      try {
        await api.post(`/work-orders/${selectedDrawerOrder.id}/transitions`, {
          eventType: 'cancel',
          reason: cancelReason,
          justification: cancelReason
        });
      } catch {
        await api.patch(`/work-orders/${selectedDrawerOrder.id}`, {
          status: 'cancelled',
          resolution_notes: cancelReason
        });
      }

      setSuccessMessage('Ordem de Serviço cancelada com sucesso!');
      setIsCancelModalOpen(false);
      setCancelReason('');
      setIsDrawerOpen(false);
      setSelectedDrawerOrder(null);
      await fetchWorkOrders();
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao cancelar ordem de serviço:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao cancelar ordem de serviço.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingCancel(false);
    }
  };

  // Submissões de Formulários
  const [submittingForm, setSubmittingForm] = useState(false);

  // Campos de Criação / Edição de OS (Conforme Requisito 5)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState<any>({
    assetId: '',
    asset_id: '',
    title: '',
    description: '',
    orderType: 'corrective',
    order_type: 'corrective',
    priority: 'medium',
    meterAtService: '',
    meter_at_service: '',
    assignedTo: '',
    technicianId: '',
    technician_id: ''
  });

  // Secção de Peças no Modal da OS (Criação e Edição - Requisito 5.2)
  const [selectedPartsList, setSelectedPartsList] = useState<any[]>([]);
  // Marca alterações reais na lista de peças: numa edição sem alterações o
  // payload NÃO envia "parts", para não substituir (apagar) as peças já
  // registadas no backend (PUT /work-orders/:id sincroniza por substituição).
  const [partsDirty, setPartsDirty] = useState(false);
  const [modalSelectedPartId, setModalSelectedPartId] = useState('');
  const [modalPartQty, setModalPartQty] = useState<number>(1);

  // Carrega Ordens de Serviço
  const fetchWorkOrders = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/work-orders');
      const raw = res.data;
      const data = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw : (raw?.workOrders || raw?.work_orders || raw?.data || raw?.orders || []);
      setOrders(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar ordens:', err);
      setError('Não foi possível carregar as Ordens de Serviço.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega lista de ativos
  const fetchAssetsForDropdown = async () => {
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/assets');
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.assets || response.data?.data || [];
      setAssets(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar dropdown de ativos:', err);
    }
  };

  // Carrega técnicos cadastrados
  const fetchTechnicians = async () => {
    setLoadingTechs(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/technicians');
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.technicians || response.data?.data || [];
      const uniqueTechs = Array.from(
        new Map<string, Technician>(data.map((t: Technician) => {
          const key = t.email?.toLowerCase() || t.name?.toLowerCase() || t.full_name?.toLowerCase() || t.id;
          return [key, t];
        })).values()
      );
      setTechnicians(uniqueTechs);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao buscar técnicos:', err);
    } finally {
      setLoadingTechs(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega linha do tempo de uma OS específica
  const fetchTimeline = async (orderId: string) => {
    setLoadingTimeline(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get(`/work-orders/${orderId}/timeline`);
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data)
        ? response.data
        : response.data?.timeline || response.data?.data || [];
      setTimeline(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao carregar linha do tempo:', err);
      // Fallback amigável se a rota de linha do tempo não tiver registros ou falhar
      setTimeline([]);
    } finally {
      setLoadingTimeline(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega as peças utilizadas nesta OS específica
  const fetchUsedParts = async (orderId: string) => {
    setLoadingUsedParts(true);
    try {
      const res = await api.get(`/work-orders/${orderId}/parts`);
      const data = Array.isArray(res.data)
        ? res.data
        : res.data?.parts || res.data?.data || [];
      setUsedParts(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao buscar peças utilizadas:', err);
      setUsedParts([]);
    } finally {
      setLoadingUsedParts(false);
    }
  };

  // Carrega catálogo do inventário para apontar peças
  const fetchInventoryParts = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/parts');
      const data = Array.isArray(res.data)
        ? res.data
        : res.data?.parts || res.data?.data || [];
      setInventoryParts(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Erro ao buscar catálogo de peças:', err);
      setInventoryParts([]);
    }
  };

  // Submeter apontamento de peça (Requisito 10.2)
  const handleAddPartSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!selectedDrawerOrder || !selectedPartId || partQtyToUse === '') return;

    const qty = Number(partQtyToUse);
    const selectedInventoryPart = inventoryParts.find(p => p.id === selectedPartId);
    if (!selectedInventoryPart) return;

    const availableStock = selectedInventoryPart.current_stock ?? selectedInventoryPart.currentStock ?? 0;
    if (qty > availableStock) {
      alert(`Quantidade insuficiente em stock! Apenas ${availableStock} un de "${selectedInventoryPart.name}" disponíveis.`);
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingAddPart(true);

    const cost = selectedInventoryPart.unit_cost ?? selectedInventoryPart.unitCost ?? 0;
    const subtotal = qty * cost;

    const newUsedPartRecord = {
      id: `used-part-${Date.now()}`,
      work_order_id: selectedDrawerOrder.id,
      workOrderId: selectedDrawerOrder.id,
      part_id: selectedPartId,
      partId: selectedPartId,
      name: selectedInventoryPart.name,
      oem_reference: selectedInventoryPart.oem_reference || selectedInventoryPart.oemReference,
      quantity: qty,
      unit_cost: cost,
      unitCost: cost,
      subtotal
    };

    try {
      try {
        // Envia para o backend se disponível
        await api.post(`/work-orders/${selectedDrawerOrder.id}/parts`, {
          partId: selectedPartId,
          quantity: qty
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('API de apontamento de peças indisponível, aplicando localmente.');
      }

      // Atualiza o inventário local (debita do stock real do inventário)
      const cachedInventory = localStorage.getItem('cmms_inventory_parts');
      if (cachedInventory) {
        const parsedInv = JSON.parse(cachedInventory);
        const updatedInv = parsedInv.map((p: any) => {
          if (p.id === selectedPartId) {
            const finalStk = Math.max(0, (p.current_stock ?? p.currentStock ?? 0) - qty);
            return {
              ...p,
              current_stock: finalStk,
              currentStock: finalStk
            };
          }
          return p;
        });
        localStorage.setItem('cmms_inventory_parts', JSON.stringify(updatedInv));
        setInventoryParts(updatedInv);
      }

      // Salva na lista de peças utilizadas
      const allUsed = localStorage.getItem('cmms_work_orders_parts');
      const parsedUsed = allUsed ? JSON.parse(allUsed) : [];
      const updatedUsed = [newUsedPartRecord, ...parsedUsed];
      localStorage.setItem('cmms_work_orders_parts', JSON.stringify(updatedUsed));

      // Recarrega lista
      await fetchUsedParts(selectedDrawerOrder.id);
      
      // Atualiza custo total da OS localmente
      const totalPartCostOfOS = [newUsedPartRecord, ...usedParts].reduce((acc, p) => acc + p.subtotal, 0);
      
      const updatedOrders = orders.map(o => {
        if (o.id === selectedDrawerOrder.id) {
          const currentPartsCost = (o.parts_cost_mzn ?? o.partsCostMzn ?? 0) + subtotal;
          return {
            ...o,
            parts_cost_mzn: currentPartsCost,
            partsCostMzn: currentPartsCost,
            total_cost_mzn: (o.labor_cost_mzn ?? o.laborCostMzn ?? 0) + currentPartsCost,
            totalCostMzn: (o.labor_cost_mzn ?? o.laborCostMzn ?? 0) + currentPartsCost
          };
        }
        return o;
      });
      setOrders(updatedOrders);

      // Fecha modal e limpa
      setIsAddPartModalOpen(false);
      setSelectedPartId('');
      setPartQtyToUse('');
      setSuccessMessage('Peça apontada e debitada do stock com sucesso!');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao apontar peça:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Falha ao apontar peça utilizada.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingAddPart(false);
    }
  };

  // Helper para identificar se a OS é atribuída ao utilizador logado (Mecânico/Técnico)
  const isAssignedToMe = (order: WorkOrder) => {
    if (!user) return false;
    
    // 1. Cruzamento direto por ID de técnico se bater com o ID do utilizador
    if (order.technician_id === user.id || order.technicianId === user.id) {
      return true;
    }
    
    // 2. Cruzamento por nome do técnico
    const tech = technicians.find(t => t.id === order.technician_id || t.id === order.technicianId);
    if (tech && tech.name.toLowerCase().trim() === user.fullName.toLowerCase().trim()) {
      return true;
    }
    
    // 3. Cruzamento direto pelo campo text 'assigned_to'
    const assignedName = order.assigned_to || order.assignedTo || '';
    if (assignedName.toLowerCase().trim() === user.fullName.toLowerCase().trim()) {
      return true;
    }
    
    return false;
  };

  const calculateWorkedHours = () => {
    if (!selectedDrawerOrder) return 0;
    if (selectedDrawerOrder.downtime_hours || selectedDrawerOrder.downtimeHours) {
      return Number(selectedDrawerOrder.downtime_hours || selectedDrawerOrder.downtimeHours || 0);
    }
    
    // Calcular a partir da timeline
    if (!timeline || timeline.length === 0) return 0;
    
    // Ordenar a timeline por data ascendente para simular os eventos
    const sortedEvents = [...timeline].sort((a, b) => {
      const da = new Date(a.created_at || a.createdAt || 0).getTime();
      const db = new Date(b.created_at || b.createdAt || 0).getTime();
      return da - db;
    });
    
    let totalMs = 0;
    let lastStartTime: number | null = null;
    
    for (const ev of sortedEvents) {
      const type = ev.eventType || ev.event_type;
      const timeStr = ev.created_at || ev.createdAt || '';
      const time = timeStr ? new Date(timeStr).getTime() : 0;
      
      if (type === 'start' || type === 'resume') {
        lastStartTime = time;
      } else if (type === 'pause' || type === 'complete') {
        if (lastStartTime !== null) {
          totalMs += (time - lastStartTime);
          lastStartTime = null;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // Se ainda estiver em curso, soma até o momento atual
    if (selectedDrawerOrder.status === 'in_progress' && lastStartTime !== null) {
      totalMs += (new Date().getTime() - lastStartTime);
    }
    
    const hours = totalMs / (1000 * 60 * 60);
    return Number(hours.toFixed(2));
  };

  const fetchNotifications = async () => {
    setLoadingNotifications(true);
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/notifications');
      const data = Array.isArray(response.data) ? response.data : response.data?.notifications || response.data?.data || [];
      setNotifications(data);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Endpoint /notifications não encontrado, gerando pendentes locais:', err);
      // Fallback: Gerar notificações virtuais a partir de ordens abertas atribuídas ao técnico atual
      const pendingOS = orders.filter(o => o.status === 'open' && isAssignedToMe(o));
      const fallbackNotes = pendingOS.map(os => ({
        id: `virtual-${os.id}`,
        title: `Nova OS Atribuída: ${os.wo_number || os.woNumber || `OS-${os.id.substring(0, 4)}`}`,
        message: os.title,
        order: os,
        createdAt: os.opened_at || os.openedAt || new Date().toISOString()
      }));
      setNotifications(fallbackNotes);
    } finally {
      setLoadingNotifications(false);
    }
  };

  // Atualiza as notificações assim que orders ou technicians forem carregados
  useEffect(() => {
    if (orders.length > 0) {
      const pendingOS = orders.filter(o => o.status === 'open' && isAssignedToMe(o));
      const fallbackNotes = pendingOS.map(os => ({
        id: `virtual-${os.id}`,
        title: `Nova OS Atribuída: ${os.wo_number || os.woNumber || `OS-${os.id.substring(0, 4)}`}`,
        message: os.title,
        order: os,
        createdAt: os.opened_at || os.openedAt || new Date().toISOString()
      }));
      setNotifications(fallbackNotes);
    }
  }, [orders, technicians]);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchWorkOrders();
    fetchAssetsForDropdown();
    fetchTechnicians();
    fetchMaintenanceRequests();

    api.get('/parts')
      .then(res => {
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : (res.data.parts || []);
        setAvailableParts(list);
      })
      .catch(err => console.error('Erro ao carregar peças para a OS:', err));
  }, []);

  // Sincroniza dados da OS no Drawer se a lista de ordens for recarregada
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedDrawerOrder) {
      const updated = orders.find(o => o.id === selectedDrawerOrder.id);
      if (updated) {
        setSelectedDrawerOrder(updated);
      }
    }
  }, [orders]);

  // Formatação de Moeda Metical Moçambicano (MZN)
  const formatMZN = (val?: number) => {
    const value = val ?? 0;
    return `${value.toLocaleString('pt-MZ', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} MZN`;
  };

  // Helper para adicionar peças provisórias à OS no próprio Modal (Requisito 5.2)
  const handleAddPartToModalList = () => {
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!selectedPartId) {
      alert('Por favor, selecione uma peça.');
      return;
    }
    const part = availableParts.find(p => p.id === selectedPartId);
    if (!part) return;

    const availableStock = Number(part.current_stock ?? part.currentStock ?? 0);
    if (modalPartQty > availableStock) {
      setSuccessMessage('Stock insuficiente no armazém');
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
      alert('Stock insuficiente no armazém');
      return;
    }

    if (modalPartQty <= 0) {
      alert('A quantidade deve ser superior a zero.');
      return;
    }

    // Verificar se já existe na lista provisória
    const alreadySelected = selectedPartsList.find(p => p.id === part.id);
    if (alreadySelected) {
      const newQty = alreadySelected.quantity + modalPartQty;
      if (newQty > availableStock) {
        setSuccessMessage('Stock insuficiente no armazém');
        setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
        alert('Stock insuficiente no armazém');
        return;
      }
      setSelectedPartsList(selectedPartsList.map(p => p.id === part.id ? { ...p, quantity: newQty } : p));
    } else {
      setSelectedPartsList([...selectedPartsList, {
        id: part.id,
        part_number: part.oem_reference || part.oemReference || part.part_number || part.partNumber || '',
        name: part.name,
        quantity: modalPartQty,
        unit_cost: part.unit_cost ?? part.unitCost ?? 0
      }]);
    }

    setPartsDirty(true);

    setSelectedPartId('');
    setModalSelectedPartId('');
    setModalPartQty(1);
    setSuccessMessage('Peça adicionada à lista provisória!');
    setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
  };

  // Abre criação de OS
  const handleOpenCreateModal = () => {
      if (checkSuspension()) return;
    setFormMode('create');
    setEditingEditingOrderId(null);
    const initialAssetId = assets.length > 0 ? assets[0].id : '';
    const initialMeter = assets.length > 0 ? (assets[0].current_meter ?? assets[0].currentMeter ?? 0) : '';
    
    setFormData({
      assetId: initialAssetId,
      asset_id: initialAssetId,
      title: '',
      description: '',
      orderType: 'corrective',
      order_type: 'corrective',
      priority: 'medium',
      meterAtService: initialMeter,
      meter_at_service: initialMeter,
      assignedTo: '',
      technicianId: '',
      technician_id: ''
    });
    setSelectedPartsList([]);
    setPartsDirty(false);
    setModalSelectedPartId('');
    setSelectedPartId('');
    setModalPartQty(1);
    setIsFormModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Abre edição de OS
  const handleOpenEditModal = (order: WorkOrder, e?: React.MouseEvent) => {
      if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (e) {
      e.stopPropagation(); // impede abrir o drawer ao clicar no botão editar
    }
    setFormMode('edit');
    setEditingEditingOrderId(order.id);
    
    // Conforme Requisito 5.1: Correção da Pré-Seleção do Técnico na Edição
    const currentTechId = order.technician_id || order.technicianId || order.assigned_to || order.assignedTo || '';
    
    setFormData({
      ...order,
      assetId: order.asset_id || order.assetId || '',
      asset_id: order.asset_id || order.assetId || '',
      title: order.title || '',
      description: order.description || '',
      orderType: order.order_type || order.orderType || 'corrective',
      order_type: order.order_type || order.orderType || 'corrective',
      priority: order.priority || 'medium',
      meterAtService: order.meter_at_service ?? order.meterAtService ?? '',
      meter_at_service: order.meter_at_service ?? order.meterAtService ?? '',
      assignedTo: currentTechId,
      technicianId: currentTechId,
      technician_id: currentTechId
    });

    // Carrega as peças reais da OS para o modal de edição (GET /work-orders/:id/parts)
    // para que a lista exibida e enviada corresponda ao que está registado no backend.
    const loadOrderPartsForEdit = async () => {
      try {
        const res = await api.get(`/work-orders/${order.id}/parts`);
        const list = Array.isArray(res.data) ? res.data : (res.data?.parts || res.data?.data || []);
        if (Array.isArray(list)) {
          setSelectedPartsList(list.map((p: any) => ({
            id: p.part_id || p.partId || p.id,
            name: p.name,
            part_number: p.part_number || p.partNumber || p.oem_reference || p.oemReference || '',
            quantity: p.quantity,
            unit_cost: p.unit_cost ?? p.unitCost ?? 0,
          })));
          return;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Não foi possível carregar as peças da ordem de serviço:', err);
      }
      // Fallback: peças já carregadas nesta sessão
      const orderParts = usedParts.filter(p => p.work_order_id === order.id || p.workOrderId === order.id);
      setSelectedPartsList(orderParts.map((p: any) => ({
        id: p.part_id || p.partId,
        name: p.name,
        part_number: p.oem_reference || p.oemReference || p.part_number || p.partNumber || '',
        quantity: p.quantity,
        unit_cost: p.unit_cost || p.unitCost || 0,
      })));
    };
    setPartsDirty(false);
    loadOrderPartsForEdit();

    setModalSelectedPartId('');
    setSelectedPartId('');
    setModalPartQty(1);
    setIsFormModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Trata troca de ativo para herdar o odómetro/horímetro sugerido
  const handleAssetChange = (assetId: string) => {
    const selected = assets.find(a => a.id === assetId);
    const meter = selected ? (selected.current_meter ?? selected.currentMeter ?? 0) : '';
    setFormData((prev: any) => ({
      ...prev,
      assetId,
      asset_id: assetId,
      meterAtService: meter,
      meter_at_service: meter
    }));
  };

  // Cadastro de novo técnico rápido
  const handleCreateTechnicianSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!techName.trim() || !techSpecialty.trim()) {
      alert('Nome e Especialidade são campos obrigatórios.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingTech(true);
    const payload = {
      name: techName,
      specialty: techSpecialty,
      phone: techPhone || undefined,
      costPerHour: techCostPerHour !== '' ? Number(techCostPerHour) : undefined,
      cost_per_hour: techCostPerHour !== '' ? Number(techCostPerHour) : undefined
    };

    try {
      const res = await api.post('/technicians', payload);
      const newTech = res.data?.technician || res.data?.data || res.data;
      
      // Feedback e recarga de técnicos
      setSuccessMessage(`Técnico "${techName}" cadastrado com sucesso!`);
      await fetchTechnicians();

      // Seleciona automaticamente o técnico criado no formulário de OS
      if (newTech && newTech.id) {
        setFormData((prev: any) => ({
          ...prev,
          assignedTo: newTech.id,
          technicianId: newTech.id,
          technician_id: newTech.id
        }));
      }

      // Fecha modal rápido e limpa
      setIsTechModalOpen(false);
      setTechName('');
      setTechSpecialty('');
      setTechPhone('');
      setTechCostPerHour('');
      
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 3000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao cadastrar técnico:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível cadastrar o técnico.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingTech(false);
    }
  };

  // Submeter OS (Conforme Requisito 5.3)
  const handleSaveOrder = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!formData.assetId || !formData.title?.trim() || !formData.description?.trim()) {
      alert('Por favor, preencha todos os campos obrigatórios.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingForm(true);

    // Enums normalizados contra o backend (wo_type / wo_priority) e números
    // saneados para nunca enviar NaN/strings inválidas (400 no servidor).
    const orderType = toValidOrderType(formData.orderType || formData.order_type);
    const priority = toValidPriority(formData.priority);
    const meterAtService = formData.meterAtService === '' || formData.meterAtService === null || formData.meterAtService === undefined
      ? undefined
      : toFiniteNumber(formData.meterAtService);

    const payload = {
      ...formData,
      assetId: formData.assetId,
      asset_id: formData.assetId,
      title: formData.title,
      description: formData.description,
      orderType,
      order_type: orderType,
      priority,
      meterAtService,
      meter_at_service: meterAtService,
      technicianId: formData.assignedTo || undefined,
      technician_id: formData.assignedTo || undefined,
      assignedTo: formData.assignedTo || undefined,
      assigned_to: formData.assignedTo || undefined,
      status: formMode === 'create' ? 'open' : undefined,
      // Em edição, "parts" só é enviado se o utilizador alterou a lista:
      // o backend substitui todas as peças da OS quando recebe o campo.
      parts: formMode === 'create' || partsDirty
        ? selectedPartsList.map((item: any) => ({
            partId: item.id || item.partId,
            part_id: item.id || item.partId,
            id: item.id,
            quantity: toFiniteNumber(item.quantity) ?? 1,
            unitCost: toFiniteNumber(item.unit_cost ?? item.unitCost) ?? 0
          }))
        : undefined
    };

    try {
      if (formMode === 'create') {
        const res = await api.post('/work-orders', payload);
        const createdWO = res.data?.workOrder || res.data?.order || res.data || payload;
        setSuccessMessage('Nova Ordem de Serviço aberta com sucesso!');
        setIsFormModalOpen(false);
        await fetchWorkOrders();
        await fetchMaintenanceRequests();
        openPrintModal(createdWO);
      } else {
        await api.put(`/work-orders/${editingOrderId}`, payload);
        setSuccessMessage('Ordem de Serviço atualizada com sucesso!');
        setIsFormModalOpen(false);
        await fetchWorkOrders();
      }
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao guardar ordem de serviço:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao guardar ordem de serviço.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingForm(false);
    }
  };

  const handleGenerateWorkOrderPDF = async () => {
    const description = String(formData.description || '').trim();
    const assignedTechnicianId = formData.assignedTo || formData.technicianId || formData.technician_id || '';
    if (!description || !assignedTechnicianId) {
      alert('Atenção: Esta Ordem de Serviço ainda está incompleta (Rascunho). Preencha a descrição técnica e atribua um técnico antes de gerar a Ficha A4 para impressão.');
      return;
    }

    const selectedAsset = assets.find((asset) => asset.id === (formData.assetId || formData.asset_id));
    const selectedTechnician = technicians.find((technician) => technician.id === assignedTechnicianId);
    const existingOrder = formMode === 'edit' && editingOrderId
      ? orders.find((order) => order.id === editingOrderId)
      : undefined;
    const now = new Date().toISOString();
    const workOrderForPrint = {
      ...existingOrder,
      id: existingOrder?.id || 'draft-preview',
      wo_number: existingOrder?.wo_number || existingOrder?.woNumber || formData.wo_number || 'RASCUNHO',
      woNumber: existingOrder?.woNumber || existingOrder?.wo_number || formData.woNumber,
      title: String(formData.title || '').trim() || 'Ordem de Serviço',
      description,
      asset_id: formData.assetId || formData.asset_id,
      assetId: formData.assetId || formData.asset_id,
      asset_name: selectedAsset?.name || existingOrder?.asset_name,
      asset: selectedAsset
        ? { code_tag: selectedAsset.code_tag || selectedAsset.codeTag, name: selectedAsset.name }
        : existingOrder?.asset,
      order_type: formData.orderType || formData.order_type || 'corrective',
      orderType: formData.orderType || formData.order_type || 'corrective',
      priority: formData.priority || 'medium',
      status: existingOrder?.status || 'open',
      opened_at: existingOrder?.opened_at || existingOrder?.openedAt || now,
      openedAt: existingOrder?.openedAt || existingOrder?.opened_at || now,
      technician_id: assignedTechnicianId,
      technicianId: assignedTechnicianId,
      assigned_to: assignedTechnicianId,
      assignedTo: assignedTechnicianId,
      assigned_technician_name: selectedTechnician?.name || selectedTechnician?.full_name || existingOrder?.assigned_to,
      technician_name: selectedTechnician?.name || selectedTechnician?.full_name || existingOrder?.technician_name,
      meter_at_service: formData.meterAtService ?? formData.meter_at_service,
      meterAtService: formData.meterAtService ?? formData.meter_at_service,
      parts: selectedPartsList.map((part: any) => ({
        oem_reference: part.part_number || part.oem_reference,
        part_number: part.part_number || part.oem_reference,
        name: part.name,
        quantity: part.quantity
      }))
    } as WorkOrder;

    handlePrintWorkOrder(workOrderForPrint, selectedPartsList);
  };

  // Iniciar Serviço (Transição 'start')
  const handleStartService = async (order: WorkOrder) => {
      if (checkSuspension()) return;
    try {
      // Tenta submeter na rota de transições, com fallback para PATCH direto de status
      try {
        await api.post(`/work-orders/${order.id}/transitions`, { eventType: 'start' });
      } catch (err) {
        await api.patch(`/work-orders/${order.id}`, { status: 'in_progress' });
      }

      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(100);
      setSuccessMessage('Serviço iniciado! Status alterado para "Em Curso".');
      await fetchWorkOrders();
      fetchTimeline(order.id);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao iniciar serviço:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível iniciar o serviço.');
    }
  };

  // Abrir Modal de Pausa / Interrupção
  const handleOpenPauseModal = (order: WorkOrder) => {
      if (checkSuspension()) return;
    setSelectedDrawerOrder(order);
    setPauseReason('Aguardando Peças no Armazém');
    setPauseJustification('');
    setIsPauseModalOpen(true);
  };

  // Submeter Pausa / Interrupção (Transição 'pause' / 'waiting_parts')
  const handlePauseServiceSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!selectedDrawerOrder || !pauseJustification.trim()) {
      alert('A justificação detalhada é estritamente obrigatória.');
      return;
    }

    setSubmittingPause(true);
    // 'paused' não existe no enum STATUSES do backend
    // (open, assigned, in_progress, waiting_parts, blocked, completed, cancelled).
    const targetStatus = pauseReason === 'Aguardando Peças no Armazém' ? 'waiting_parts' : 'blocked';

    try {
      try {
        await api.post(`/work-orders/${selectedDrawerOrder.id}/transitions`, {
          eventType: 'pause',
          reason: pauseReason,
          justification: pauseJustification
        });
      } catch (err) {
        // Fallback robusto para PATCH direto de status e descrição
        await api.patch(`/work-orders/${selectedDrawerOrder.id}`, {
          status: targetStatus,
          description: `${selectedDrawerOrder.description}\n\n[Pausa - ${pauseReason}]: ${pauseJustification}`
        });
      }

      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(100);
      setSuccessMessage(`Serviço interrompido! Motivo: ${pauseReason}`);
      setIsPauseModalOpen(false);
      await fetchWorkOrders();
      fetchTimeline(selectedDrawerOrder.id);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao pausar serviço:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível registrar a pausa.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingPause(false);
    }
  };

  // Retomar Serviço (Transição 'resume')
  const handleResumeService = async (order: WorkOrder) => {
      if (checkSuspension()) return;
    try {
      try {
        await api.post(`/work-orders/${order.id}/transitions`, { eventType: 'resume' });
      } catch (err) {
        await api.patch(`/work-orders/${order.id}`, { status: 'in_progress' });
      }

      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(100);
      setSuccessMessage('Serviço retomado com sucesso!');
      await fetchWorkOrders();
      fetchTimeline(order.id);
      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao retomar serviço:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Não foi possível retomar o serviço.');
    }
  };

  // Concluir Serviço (Ação Rápida de Fecho)
  const handleCloseOrderSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (checkSuspension()) return;
    if (!selectedOrder) return;

    setSubmittingClose(true);

    const payload = {
      downtimeHours: Number(downtimeHours || 0),
      downtime_hours: Number(downtimeHours || 0),
      partsCostMzn: Number(partsCostMzn || 0),
      parts_cost_mzn: Number(partsCostMzn || 0),
      laborCostMzn: Number(laborCostMzn || 0),
      labor_cost_mzn: Number(laborCostMzn || 0),
      resolutionNotes: resolutionNotes,
      resolution_notes: resolutionNotes,
      status: 'completed'
    };

    try {
      // Registra transição de fechamento se suportado
      try {
        await api.post(`/work-orders/${selectedOrder.id}/transitions`, {
          eventType: 'complete',
          justification: resolutionNotes
        });
      } catch (err) {
        // Ignoramos se rota de transição não existir para fechamento
      }

      try {
        await api.patch(`/work-orders/${selectedOrder.id}/close`, payload);
      } catch (patchErr) {
        try {
          await api.post(`/work-orders/${selectedOrder.id}/complete`, payload);
        } catch (postErr) {
          await api.patch(`/work-orders/${selectedOrder.id}`, payload);
        }
      }
      
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(120);
      setSuccessMessage(`OS ${selectedOrder.wo_number || selectedOrder.woNumber} encerrada com sucesso!`);
      setIsCloseModalOpen(false);
      setSelectedOrder(null);

      // Limpa formulário
      setPartsCostMzn('');
      setLaborCostMzn('');
      setDowntimeHours('');
      setResolutionNotes('');

      // Recarrega
      await fetchWorkOrders();

      setTimeout(() => setSuccessMessage(null), 4000);
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Erro ao fechar ordem de serviço:', err);
      alert(err.response?.data?.message || err.message || 'Erro ao fechar a ordem de serviço.');
    } finally {
      setSubmittingClose(false);
    }
  };

  // Detetar se a ordem de serviço está em atraso (Aberta ou Em Curso e com mais de 3 dias de criação)
  const isDelayed = (order: WorkOrder) => {
    if (order.status === 'completed') return false;
    const openedDate = new Date(order.opened_at || order.openedAt || '');
    const diffTime = Math.abs(new Date().getTime() - openedDate.getTime());
    const diffDays = Math.ceil(diffTime / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24));
    return diffDays > 3;
  };

  // Abre Drawer do Técnico
  const handleOpenDrawer = (order: WorkOrder) => {
    setSelectedDrawerOrder(order);
    setIsDrawerOpen(true);
    fetchTimeline(order.id);
    fetchUsedParts(order.id);
    fetchInventoryParts();
  };

  // Renderização das badges de prioridade
  const renderPriorityBadge = (prio: string) => {
    switch (prio) {
      case 'low':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
            Baixa
          </span>
        );
      case 'medium':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
            Média
          </span>
        );
      case 'high':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">
            Alta
          </span>
        );
      case 'urgent':
      case 'critical':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-semibold bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 animate-pulse">
            ⚠️ Urgente
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200">
            {prio}
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  // Renderização das badges de status
  const renderStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status) {
      case 'open':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-amber-50 text-amber-700 border border-amber-200">
            Aberta
          </span>
        );
      case 'in_progress':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 border border-blue-200">
            Em Curso
          </span>
        );
      case 'waiting_parts':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 border border-purple-200">
            Aguardar Peças
          </span>
        );
      case 'paused':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
            Pausada
          </span>
        );
      case 'blocked':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200">
            Bloqueada
          </span>
        );
      case 'completed':
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200">
            Concluída
          </span>
        );
      default:
        return (
          <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[11px] font-medium bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200">
            {status}
          </span>
        );
    }
  };

  // Resolução do Nome do Técnico Encarregue (Eliminar UUIDs nos Cards)
  const resolveTechnicianName = (order: any) => {
    if (!order) return 'Pendente de Atribuição';
    if (order.assigned_technician_name) return order.assigned_technician_name;
    if (order.assignedTechnicianName) return order.assignedTechnicianName;
    if (order.technician_name) return order.technician_name;
    if (order.technician?.name) return order.technician.name;
    
    // Fallback: procura na lista de técnicos/utilizadores já carregada em memória
    const matchedTech = technicians.find(t => 
      t.id === order.assigned_to || 
      t.id === order.assignedTo || 
      t.id === order.technician_id || 
      t.id === order.technicianId
    );
    if (matchedTech) return matchedTech.name || matchedTech.full_name || (matchedTech as any).fullName;

    // Proteção estrita: se for um UUID ou estiver vazio, NUNCA exibir o código na tela
    const isUUID = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i;
    const assignedVal = order.assigned_to || order.assignedTo;
    if (!assignedVal || isUUID.test(String(assignedVal))) {
      return 'Pendente de Atribuição';
    }
    return String(assignedVal);
  };

  // Helper de Formatação dos Contadores (KM vs Horas)
  const formatMeterReading = (val: any, unit: string) => {
    if (val === undefined || val === null || val === '') return 'Sem registo';
    const num = Number(val);
    if (isNaN(num)) return 'Sem registo';
    if (unit === 'km') {
      return `${num.toLocaleString('pt-MZ')} km`;
    }
    return `${num.toLocaleString('pt-MZ', { minimumFractionDigits: 2, maximumFractionDigits: 2 })} horas`;
  };

  // Filtro de isolamento de escopo (fleet vs plant)
